C:/amttp/contracts/AMTTPBiconomyModule.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.24;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
   5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   6 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/Pausable.sol";
   7 |     | import "./interfaces/IBiconomy.sol";
   8 |     | 
   9 |     | /**
  10 |     |  * @title AMTTPBiconomyModule
  11 |     |  * @notice ERC-4337 compliant validation module for Biconomy Smart Accounts
  12 |     |  * @dev Integrates AMTTP risk assessment with Account Abstraction for gasless transactions
  13 |     |  * 
  14 |     |  * Features:
  15 |     |  * - UserOperation validation with ML risk scoring
  16 |     |  * - Session key management with AMTTP policies
  17 |     |  * - Spending limits with risk-based adjustments
  18 |     |  * - Paymaster integration for sponsored transactions
  19 |     |  * - Compliance-aware transaction batching
  20 |     |  */
  21 |     | contract AMTTPBiconomyModule is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard, Pausable {
  22 |     |     
  23 |     |     // ============ Constants ============
  24 |     |     uint256 public constant RISK_PRECISION = 1000;
  25 |     |     uint256 public constant DEFAULT_RISK_THRESHOLD = 600;
  26 |     |     uint256 public constant SESSION_KEY_VALIDITY = 24 hours;
  27 |     |     bytes4 public constant VALIDATION_SUCCESS = 0x00000000;
  28 |     |     bytes4 public constant VALIDATION_FAILED = 0xffffffff;
  29 |     |     
  30 |     |     // ============ Structs ============
  31 |     |     
  32 |     |     struct AccountConfig {
  33 |     |         bool enabled;
  34 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold;          // Max allowed risk score
  35 |     |         uint256 dailySpendLimit;        // Daily spending limit in wei
  36 |     |         uint256 singleTxLimit;          // Single transaction limit
  37 |     |         bool requireMLValidation;       // Require ML check for all transactions
  38 |     |         bool allowSessionKeys;          // Allow session key delegation
  39 |     |         bool allowBatching;             // Allow transaction batching
  40 |     |     }
  41 |     |     
  42 |     |     struct SessionKey {
  43 |     |         address key;
  44 |     |         uint48 validAfter;
  45 |     |         uint48 validUntil;
  46 |     |         address[] allowedTargets;
  47 |     |         bytes4[] allowedSelectors;
  48 |     |         uint256 spendingLimit;
  49 |     |         uint256 spentAmount;
  50 |     |         uint256 maxRiskAllowed;
  51 |     |         bool active;
  52 |     |     }
  53 |     |     
  54 |     |     struct SpendingTracker {
  55 |     |         uint256 dailySpent;
  56 |     |         uint256 weeklySpent;
  57 |     |         uint256 lastDayReset;
  58 |     |         uint256 lastWeekReset;
  59 |     |         uint256 transactionCount;
  60 |     |     }
  61 |     |     
  62 |     |     struct ValidationResult {
  63 |     |         bool valid;
  64 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
  65 |     |         string reason;
  66 |     |         bool requiresReview;
  67 |     |     }
  68 |     |     
  69 |     |     struct RiskAssessment {
  70 |     |         uint256 score;
  71 |     |         string category;
  72 |     |         bool sanctioned;
  73 |     |         uint256 timestamp;
  74 |     |     }
  75 |     |     
  76 |     |     // ============ State Variables ============
  77 |     |     
  78 |     |     // AMTTP Integration
  79 |     |     address public policyEngine;
  80 |     |     address public mlOracleService;
  81 |     |     
  82 |     |     // ERC-4337 EntryPoint
  83 |     |     IEntryPoint public immutable entryPoint;
  84 |     |     
  85 |     |     // Account configurations
  86 |     |     mapping(address => AccountConfig) public accountConfigs;
  87 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public registeredAccounts;
  88 |     |     
  89 |     |     // Session keys per account
  90 |     |     mapping(address => mapping(address => SessionKey)) public sessionKeys;
  91 |     |     mapping(address => address[]) public accountSessionKeys;
  92 |     |     
  93 |     |     // Spending tracking
  94 |     |     mapping(address => SpendingTracker) public spendingTrackers;
  95 |     |     
  96 |     |     // Risk cache (to avoid repeated oracle calls)
  97 |     |     // Note: Mappings are implicitly initialized - entries default to zero values until set
  98 |     |     // slither-disable-next-line uninitialized-state
  99 |     |     mapping(address => RiskAssessment) public riskCache;
 100 |     |     uint256 public riskCacheTTL = 5 minutes;
 101 |     |     
 102 |     |     // Trusted forwarders for meta-transactions
 103 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public trustedForwarders;
 104 |     |     
 105 |     |     // Paymaster whitelist
 106 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public approvedPaymasters;
 107 |     |     
 108 |     |     // ============ Events ============
 109 |     |     
 110 |     |     event AccountRegistered(address indexed account, AccountConfig config);
 111 |     |     event AccountConfigUpdated(address indexed account);
 112 |     |     event SessionKeyCreated(address indexed account, address indexed sessionKey, uint48 validUntil);
 113 |     |     event SessionKeyRevoked(address indexed account, address indexed sessionKey);
 114 |     |     event TransactionValidated(address indexed account, bytes32 userOpHash, uint256 riskScore, bool approved);
 115 |     |     event SpendingLimitUpdated(address indexed account, uint256 newLimit);
 116 |     |     event RiskThresholdUpdated(address indexed account, uint256 newThreshold);
 117 |     |     event PaymasterApproved(address indexed paymaster, bool status);
 118 |     |     event ForwarderUpdated(address indexed forwarder, bool trusted);
 119 |     |     
 120 |     |     // ============ Errors ============
 121 |     |     
 122 |     |     error AccountNotRegistered();
 123 |     |     error InvalidSessionKey();
 124 |     |     error SessionKeyExpired();
 125 |     |     error SpendingLimitExceeded();
 126 |     |     error RiskThresholdExceeded();
 127 |     |     error UnauthorizedTarget();
 128 |     |     error UnauthorizedSelector();
 129 |     |     error InvalidSignature();
 130 |     |     error BatchingNotAllowed();
 131 |     |     
 132 |     |     // ============ Constructor ============
 133 |     |     
 134 |     |     constructor(
 135 |     |         address _entryPoint,
 136 |     |         address _policyEngine,
 137 |     |         address _mlOracle
 138 |     |     ) {
 139 |     |         require(_entryPoint != address(0), "Invalid entry point");
 140 |     |         require(_policyEngine != address(0), "Invalid policy engine");
 141 |     |         
 142 |     |         entryPoint = IEntryPoint(_entryPoint);
 143 |     |         policyEngine = _policyEngine;
 144 |     |         mlOracleService = _mlOracle;
 145 |     |     }
 146 |     |     
 147 |     |     // ============ Account Registration ============
 148 |     |     
 149 |     |     /**
 150 |     |      * @notice Register a smart account with AMTTP module
 151 |     |      * @param account Address of the Biconomy smart account
 152 |     |      * @param config Initial account configuration
 153 |     |      */
 154 |     |     function registerAccount(
 155 |     |         address account,
 156 |     |         AccountConfig calldata config
 157 |     |     ) external {
 158 |     |         require(!registeredAccounts[account], "Already registered");
 159 |     |         require(account != address(0), "Invalid account");
 160 |     |         
 161 |     |         // Verify caller is account owner or the account itself
 162 |     |         require(
 163 |     |             msg.sender == account || 
 164 |     |             ISmartAccount(account).isModuleEnabled(address(this)),
 165 |     |             "Not authorized"
 166 |     |         );
 167 |     |         
 168 |     |         registeredAccounts[account] = true;
 169 |     |         accountConfigs[account] = AccountConfig({
 170 |     |             enabled: true,
 171 |     |             riskThreshold: config.riskThreshold > 0 ? config.riskThreshold : DEFAULT_RISK_THRESHOLD,
 172 |     |             dailySpendLimit: config.dailySpendLimit,
 173 |     |             singleTxLimit: config.singleTxLimit,
 174 |     |             requireMLValidation: config.requireMLValidation,
 175 |     |             allowSessionKeys: config.allowSessionKeys,
 176 |     |             allowBatching: config.allowBatching
 177 |     |         });
 178 |     |         
 179 |     |         emit AccountRegistered(account, accountConfigs[account]);
 180 |     |     }
 181 |     |     
 182 |     |     /**
 183 |     |      * @notice Update account configuration
 184 |     |      */
 185 |     |     function updateAccountConfig(
 186 |     |         address account,
 187 |     |         AccountConfig calldata config
 188 |     |     ) external {
 189 |     |         require(registeredAccounts[account], "Not registered");
 190 |     |         require(msg.sender == account || _isAccountOwner(account, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 191 |     |         
 192 |     |         accountConfigs[account] = config;
 193 |     |         emit AccountConfigUpdated(account);
 194 |     |     }
 195 |     |     
 196 |     |     // ============ UserOperation Validation ============
 197 |     |     
 198 |     |     /**
 199 |     |      * @notice Validate a UserOperation before execution
 200 |     |      * @dev Called by the smart account during validateUserOp
 201 |     |      * @param userOp The UserOperation to validate
 202 |     |      * @param userOpHash Hash of the UserOperation
 203 |     |      * @return validationData 0 if valid, 1 if invalid
 204 |     |      */
 205 |     |     function validateUserOp(
 206 |     |         UserOperation calldata userOp,
 207 |     |         bytes32 userOpHash
 208 |     |     ) external returns (uint256 validationData) {
 209 |     |         address account = userOp.sender;
 210 |     |         
 211 |     |         if (!registeredAccounts[account]) {
 212 |     |             return 1; // Allow but skip AMTTP validation
 213 |     |         }
 214 |     |         
 215 |     |         AccountConfig memory config = accountConfigs[account];
 216 |     |         if (!config.enabled) {
 217 |     |             return 0; // Module disabled, allow
 218 |     |         }
 219 |     |         
 220 |     |         // Decode call data to determine operation
 221 |     |         (address target, uint256 value, bytes memory data) = _decodeCallData(userOp.callData);
 222 |     |         
 223 |     |         // Check session key if applicable
 224 |     |         if (config.allowSessionKeys) {
 225 |     |             address sessionKey = _extractSessionKey(userOp.signature);
 226 |     |             if (sessionKey != address(0)) {
 227 |     |                 ValidationResult memory result = _validateSessionKey(account, sessionKey, target, value, data);
 228 |     |                 if (!result.valid) {
 229 |     |                     return 1;
 230 |     |                 }
 231 |     |             }
 232 |     |         }
 233 |     |         
 234 |     |         // Check spending limits
 235 |     |         if (!_checkSpendingLimits(account, value)) {
 236 |     |             return 1;
 237 |     |         }
 238 |     |         
 239 |     |         // ML risk assessment
 240 |     |         if (config.requireMLValidation) {
 241 |     |             ValidationResult memory riskResult = _performRiskAssessment(account, target, value, data);
 242 |     |             
 243 |     |             emit TransactionValidated(account, userOpHash, riskResult.riskScore, riskResult.valid);
 244 |     |             
 245 |     |             if (!riskResult.valid) {
 246 |     |                 return 1;
 247 |     |             }
 248 |     |         }
 249 |     |         
 250 |     |         // Update spending tracker
 251 |     |         _updateSpending(account, value);
 252 |     |         
 253 |     |         return 0; // Valid
 254 |     |     }
 255 |     |     
 256 |     |     /**
 257 |     |      * @notice Validate a batch operation
 258 |     |      */
 259 |     |     function validateBatch(
 260 |     |         address account,
 261 |     |         address[] calldata targets,
 262 |     |         uint256[] calldata values,
 263 |     |         bytes[] calldata datas
 264 |     |     ) external returns (bool valid, uint256 totalRiskScore) {
 265 |     |         if (!accountConfigs[account].allowBatching) {
 266 |     |             revert BatchingNotAllowed();
 267 |     |         }
 268 |     |         
 269 |     |         uint256 totalValue = 0;
 270 |     |         totalRiskScore = 0;
 271 |     |         
 272 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
 273 |     |             totalValue += values[i];
 274 |     |             
 275 |     |             ValidationResult memory result = _performRiskAssessment(account, targets[i], values[i], datas[i]);
 276 |     |             totalRiskScore += result.riskScore;
 277 |     |             
 278 |     |             if (!result.valid) {
 279 |     |                 return (false, totalRiskScore);
 280 |     |             }
 281 |     |         }
 282 |     |         
 283 |     |         // Check total spending
 284 |     |         if (!_checkSpendingLimits(account, totalValue)) {
 285 |     |             return (false, totalRiskScore);
 286 |     |         }
 287 |     |         
 288 |     |         return (true, totalRiskScore / targets.length);
 289 |     |     }
 290 |     |     
 291 |     |     // ============ Session Key Management ============
 292 |     |     
 293 |     |     /**
 294 |     |      * @notice Create a new session key for delegated transactions
 295 |     |      */
 296 |     |     function createSessionKey(
 297 |     |         address account,
 298 |     |         address sessionKey,
 299 |     |         uint48 validUntil,
 300 |     |         address[] calldata allowedTargets,
 301 |     |         bytes4[] calldata allowedSelectors,
 302 |     |         uint256 spendingLimit,
 303 |     |         uint256 maxRiskAllowed
 304 |     |     ) external {
 305 |     |         require(registeredAccounts[account], "Not registered");
 306 |     |         require(accountConfigs[account].allowSessionKeys, "Session keys disabled");
 307 |     |         require(msg.sender == account || _isAccountOwner(account, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 308 |     |         require(validUntil > block.timestamp, "Invalid validity");
 309 |     |         
 310 |     |         sessionKeys[account][sessionKey] = SessionKey({
 311 |     |             key: sessionKey,
 312 |     |             validAfter: uint48(block.timestamp),
 313 |     |             validUntil: validUntil,
 314 |     |             allowedTargets: allowedTargets,
 315 |     |             allowedSelectors: allowedSelectors,
 316 |     |             spendingLimit: spendingLimit,
 317 |     |             spentAmount: 0,
 318 |     |             maxRiskAllowed: maxRiskAllowed > 0 ? maxRiskAllowed : accountConfigs[account].riskThreshold,
 319 |     |             active: true
 320 |     |         });
 321 |     |         
 322 |     |         accountSessionKeys[account].push(sessionKey);
 323 |     |         
 324 |     |         emit SessionKeyCreated(account, sessionKey, validUntil);
 325 |     |     }
 326 |     |     
 327 |     |     /**
 328 |     |      * @notice Revoke a session key
 329 |     |      */
 330 |     |     function revokeSessionKey(address account, address sessionKey) external {
 331 |     |         require(msg.sender == account || _isAccountOwner(account, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 332 |     |         
 333 |     |         sessionKeys[account][sessionKey].active = false;
 334 |     |         emit SessionKeyRevoked(account, sessionKey);
 335 |     |     }
 336 |     |     
 337 |     |     /**
 338 |     |      * @notice Get all session keys for an account
 339 |     |      */
 340 |     |     function getSessionKeys(address account) external view returns (address[] memory) {
 341 |     |         return accountSessionKeys[account];
 342 |     |     }
 343 |     |     
 344 |     |     /**
 345 |     |      * @notice Check if session key is valid
 346 |     |      */
 347 |     |     function isSessionKeyValid(address account, address sessionKey) external view returns (bool) {
 348 |     |         SessionKey memory sk = sessionKeys[account][sessionKey];
 349 |     |         return sk.active && 
 350 |     |                block.timestamp >= sk.validAfter && 
 351 |     |                block.timestamp <= sk.validUntil &&
 352 |     |                sk.spentAmount < sk.spendingLimit;
 353 |     |     }

 356 |     |     
 357 |     |     /**
 358 |     |      * @notice Approve a paymaster for sponsored transactions
 359 |     |      */
 360 |     |     function approvePaymaster(address paymaster, bool approved) external onlyOwner {
 361 |     |         approvedPaymasters[paymaster] = approved;
 362 |     |         emit PaymasterApproved(paymaster, approved);
 363 |     |     }
 364 |     |     
 365 |     |     /**
 366 |     |      * @notice Check if paymaster is approved
 367 |     |      */
 368 |     |     function isPaymasterApproved(address paymaster) external view returns (bool) {
 369 |     |         return approvedPaymasters[paymaster];
 370 |     |     }

 374 |     |     function setPolicyEngine(address _policyEngine) external onlyOwner {
 375 |     |         require(_policyEngine != address(0), "Invalid address");
 376 |     |         policyEngine = _policyEngine;
 377 |     |     }

 380 |     |         mlOracleService = _mlOracle;
 381 |     |     }

 383 |     |     function setRiskCacheTTL(uint256 _ttl) external onlyOwner {
 384 |     |         riskCacheTTL = _ttl;
 385 |     |     }

 387 |     |     function setTrustedForwarder(address forwarder, bool trusted) external onlyOwner {
 388 |     |         trustedForwarders[forwarder] = trusted;
 389 |     |         emit ForwarderUpdated(forwarder, trusted);
 390 |     |     }

 392 |     |     function pause() external onlyOwner {
 393 |     |         _pause();
 394 |     |     }

 397 |     |         _unpause();
 398 |     |     }

 400 |     |     // ============ View Functions ============
 401 |     |     
 402 |     |     function getSpendingInfo(address account) external view returns (
 403 |     |         uint256 dailySpent,
 404 |     |         uint256 dailyLimit,
 405 |     |         uint256 remainingToday
 406 |     |     ) {
 407 |     |         SpendingTracker memory tracker = spendingTrackers[account];
 408 |     |         AccountConfig memory config = accountConfigs[account];
 409 |     |         
 410 |     |         dailySpent = tracker.dailySpent;
 411 |     |         dailyLimit = config.dailySpendLimit;
 412 |     |         
 413 |     |         if (dailyLimit > dailySpent) {
 414 |     |             remainingToday = dailyLimit - dailySpent;
 415 |     |         }
 416 |     |     }
 417 |     |     
 418 |     |     function getAccountRisk(address account) external view returns (
 419 |     |         uint256 currentRiskScore,
 420 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold,
 421 |     |         bool withinThreshold
 422 |     |     ) {
 423 |     |         RiskAssessment memory assessment = riskCache[account];
 424 |     |         AccountConfig memory config = accountConfigs[account];
 425 |     |         
 426 |     |         currentRiskScore = assessment.score;
 427 |     |         riskThreshold = config.riskThreshold;
 428 |     |         withinThreshold = currentRiskScore <= riskThreshold;
 429 |     |     }
 430 |     |     
 431 |     |     // ============ Internal Functions ============
 432 |     |     
 433 |     |     function _checkSpendingLimits(address account, uint256 value) internal view returns (bool) {
 434 |     |         AccountConfig memory config = accountConfigs[account];
 435 |     |         SpendingTracker memory tracker = spendingTrackers[account];
 436 |     |         
 437 |     |         // Check single transaction limit
 438 |     |         if (config.singleTxLimit > 0 && value > config.singleTxLimit) {
 439 |     |             return false;
 440 |     |         }
 441 |     |         
 442 |     |         // Check daily limit
 443 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 444 |     |         uint256 dailySpent = tracker.lastDayReset == currentDay ? tracker.dailySpent : 0;
 445 |     |         
 446 |     |         if (config.dailySpendLimit > 0 && dailySpent + value > config.dailySpendLimit) {
 447 |     |             return false;
 448 |     |         }
 449 |     |         
 450 |     |         return true;
 451 |     |     }
 452 |     |     
 453 |     |     function _updateSpending(address account, uint256 value) internal {
 454 |     |         SpendingTracker storage tracker = spendingTrackers[account];
 455 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 456 |     |         uint256 currentWeek = block.timestamp / 1 weeks;
 457 |     |         
 458 |     |         // Reset daily if new day
 459 |     |         if (tracker.lastDayReset < currentDay) {
 460 |     |             tracker.dailySpent = 0;
 461 |     |             tracker.lastDayReset = currentDay;
 462 |     |         }
 463 |     |         
 464 |     |         // Reset weekly if new week
 465 |     |         if (tracker.lastWeekReset < currentWeek) {
 466 |     |             tracker.weeklySpent = 0;
 467 |     |             tracker.lastWeekReset = currentWeek;
 468 |     |         }
 469 |     |         
 470 |     |         tracker.dailySpent += value;
 471 |     |         tracker.weeklySpent += value;
 472 |     |         tracker.transactionCount++;
 473 |     |     }
 474 |     |     
 475 |     |     function _validateSessionKey(
 476 |     |         address account,
 477 |     |         address sessionKey,
 478 |     |         address target,
 479 |     |         uint256 value,
 480 |     |         bytes memory data
 481 |     |     ) internal view returns (ValidationResult memory) {
 482 |     |         SessionKey memory sk = sessionKeys[account][sessionKey];
 483 |     |         
 484 |     |         // Check if session key is active and not expired
 485 |     |         if (!sk.active || block.timestamp < sk.validAfter || block.timestamp > sk.validUntil) {
 486 |     |             return ValidationResult({
 487 |     |                 valid: false,
 488 |     |                 riskScore: 0,
 489 |     |                 reason: "Session key invalid or expired",
 490 |     |                 requiresReview: false
 491 |     |             });
 492 |     |         }
 493 |     |         
 494 |     |         // Check spending limit
 495 |     |         if (sk.spentAmount + value > sk.spendingLimit) {
 496 |     |             return ValidationResult({
 497 |     |                 valid: false,
 498 |     |                 riskScore: 0,
 499 |     |                 reason: "Session key spending limit exceeded",
 500 |     |                 requiresReview: false
 501 |     |             });
 502 |     |         }
 503 |     |         
 504 |     |         // Check allowed targets
 505 |     |         bool targetAllowed = sk.allowedTargets.length == 0; // Empty means all allowed
 506 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < sk.allowedTargets.length; i++) {
 507 |     |             if (sk.allowedTargets[i] == target) {
 508 |     |                 targetAllowed = true;
 509 |     |                 break;
 510 |     |             }
 511 |     |         }
 512 |     |         
 513 |     |         if (!targetAllowed) {
 514 |     |             return ValidationResult({
 515 |     |                 valid: false,
 516 |     |                 riskScore: 0,
 517 |     |                 reason: "Target not allowed for session key",
 518 |     |                 requiresReview: false
 519 |     |             });
 520 |     |         }
 521 |     |         
 522 |     |         // Check allowed selectors if data present
 523 |     |         if (data.length >= 4 && sk.allowedSelectors.length > 0) {
 524 |     |             bytes4 selector = bytes4(data);
 525 |     |             bool selectorAllowed = false;
 526 |     |             
 527 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < sk.allowedSelectors.length; i++) {
 528 |     |                 if (sk.allowedSelectors[i] == selector) {
 529 |     |                     selectorAllowed = true;
 530 |     |                     break;
 531 |     |                 }
 532 |     |             }
 533 |     |             
 534 |     |             if (!selectorAllowed) {
 535 |     |                 return ValidationResult({
 536 |     |                     valid: false,
 537 |     |                     riskScore: 0,
 538 |     |                     reason: "Function selector not allowed",
 539 |     |                     requiresReview: false
 540 |     |                 });
 541 |     |             }
 542 |     |         }
 543 |     |         
 544 |     |         return ValidationResult({
 545 |     |             valid: true,
 546 |     |             riskScore: 0,
 547 |     |             reason: "Session key valid",
 548 |     |             requiresReview: false
 549 |     |         });
 550 |     |     }
 551 |     |     
 552 |     |     function _performRiskAssessment(
 553 |     |         address account,
 554 |     |         address target,
 555 |     |         uint256 value,
 556 |     |         bytes memory /* data */
 557 |     |     ) internal view returns (ValidationResult memory) {
 558 |     |         AccountConfig memory config = accountConfigs[account];
 559 |     |         
 560 |     |         // Check cache first
 561 |     |         RiskAssessment memory cached = riskCache[target];
 562 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
 563 |     |         
 564 |     |         if (cached.timestamp + riskCacheTTL > block.timestamp) {
 565 |     |             riskScore = cached.score;
 566 |     |         } else {
 567 |     |             // Simplified risk calculation (in production, call ML oracle)
 568 |     |             riskScore = _calculateSimpleRisk(target, value);
 569 |     |         }
 570 |     |         
 571 |     |         // Check if sanctioned (would be checked via oracle)
 572 |     |         if (cached.sanctioned) {
 573 |     |             return ValidationResult({
 574 |     |                 valid: false,
 575 |     |                 riskScore: 1000,
 576 |     |                 reason: "Target is sanctioned",
 577 |     |                 requiresReview: false
 578 |     |             });
 579 |     |         }
 580 |     |         
 581 |     |         bool valid = riskScore <= config.riskThreshold;
 582 |     |         
 583 |     |         return ValidationResult({
 584 |     |             valid: valid,
 585 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 586 |     |             reason: valid ? "Risk within threshold" : "Risk exceeds threshold",
 587 |     |             requiresReview: riskScore > config.riskThreshold / 2
 588 |     |         });
 589 |     |     }
 590 |     |     
 591 |     |     function _calculateSimpleRisk(address target, uint256 value) internal view returns (uint256) {
 592 |     |         uint256 risk = 100; // Base risk
 593 |     |         
 594 |     |         // Contract interaction
 595 |     |         uint256 codeSize;
 596 |     |         assembly {
 597 |     |             codeSize := extcodesize(target)
 598 |     |         }
 599 |     |         if (codeSize > 0) {
 600 |     |             risk += 100;
 601 |     |         }
 602 |     |         
 603 |     |         // Value-based risk
 604 |     |         if (value > 10 ether) {
 605 |     |             risk += 300;
 606 |     |         } else if (value > 1 ether) {
 607 |     |             risk += 150;
 608 |     |         } else if (value > 0.1 ether) {
 609 |     |             risk += 50;
 610 |     |         }
 611 |     |         
 612 |     |         return risk;
 613 |     |     }
 614 |     |     
 615 |     |     function _decodeCallData(bytes calldata callData) internal pure returns (
 616 |     |         address target,
 617 |     |         uint256 value,
 618 |     |         bytes memory data
 619 |     |     ) {
 620 |     |         // Decode execute(address,uint256,bytes) call
 621 |     |         if (callData.length >= 68) {
 622 |     |             // Skip selector (4 bytes)
 623 |     |             target = address(bytes20(callData[16:36]));
 624 |     |             value = uint256(bytes32(callData[36:68]));
 625 |     |             
 626 |     |             if (callData.length > 68) {
 627 |     |                 // Has data payload
 628 |     |                 uint256 dataOffset = uint256(bytes32(callData[68:100]));
 629 |     |                 uint256 dataLength = uint256(bytes32(callData[100:132]));
 630 |     |                 data = callData[132:132 + dataLength];
 631 |     |             }
 632 |     |         }
 633 |     |     }
 634 |     |     
 635 |     |     function _extractSessionKey(bytes calldata signature) internal pure returns (address) {
 636 |     |         // Session key is encoded in signature if present
 637 |     |         // Format: [signature (65 bytes)][sessionKey (20 bytes)]
 638 |     |         if (signature.length >= 85) {
 639 |     |             return address(bytes20(signature[65:85]));
 640 |     |         }
 641 |     |         return address(0);
 642 |     |     }
 643 |     |     
 644 |     |     function _isAccountOwner(address account, address caller) internal view returns (bool) {
 645 |     |         // In production, check with the smart account's ownership
 646 |     |         try ISmartAccount(account).isModuleEnabled(caller) returns (bool isEnabled) {
 647 |     |             return isEnabled;
 648 |     |         } catch {
 649 |     |             return false;
 650 |     |         }
 651 |     |     }
 652 |     | }

 287 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 288 |     |     //                     SWAP INITIATION (ETH)
 289 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 290 |     |     
 291 |     |     /**
 292 |     |      * @notice Initiate ETH swap with risk score and oracle signature
 293 |     |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors. Uses nonReentrant guard.
 294 |     |      * Note: _determineStatus calls external policyEngine.validateTransaction but
 295 |     |      * this is safe because: 1) nonReentrant prevents reentrancy, 2) validateTransaction
 296 |     |      * is a view-like call that doesn't modify external state, 3) swapId is unique and
 297 | *   |      * checked for non-existence before the call.
 298 |     |      */
 299 | *r  |     function initiateSwap(
 300 | *   |         address seller,
 301 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 302 | *   |         uint256 timelock,
 303 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 304 | *   |         bytes32 kycHash,
 305 | *r  |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 306 |     |     ) external payable nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 307 |     |         if (msg.value == 0) revert NoETHSent();
 308 |     |         if (seller == address(0)) revert InvalidSeller();
 309 |     |         if (timelock <= block.timestamp) revert InvalidTimelock();
 310 |     |         if (riskScore > RISK_SCALE) revert InvalidRiskScore();
 311 |     |         
 312 |     |         // Verify oracle signature
 313 |     |         if (!_verifyOracleSignature(msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore, kycHash, oracleSignature)) revert InvalidSignature();
 314 |     |         
 315 |     |         // Check user limits
 316 |     |         _checkUserLimits(msg.sender, msg.value);
 317 |     |         
 318 |     |         // Generate unique swap ID before any external calls
 319 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, seller, hashlock, timelock, block.timestamp));
 320 |     |         if (swaps[swapId].buyer != address(0)) revert SwapExists();
 321 |     |         
 322 |     |         // Determine initial status based on risk (may call external policy engine)
 323 |     |         SwapStatus initialStatus = _determineStatus(msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore, kycHash);
 324 |     |         
 325 |     |         // Store swap state
 326 |     |         swaps[swapId] = Swap({
 327 |     |             buyer: msg.sender,
 328 |     |             status: initialStatus,
 329 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
 330 |     |             assetType: AssetType.ETH,
 331 |     |             seller: seller,
 332 |     |             token: address(0),
 333 |     |             amount: msg.value,
 334 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
 335 | *   |             timelock: timelock,
 336 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 337 |     |             kycHash: kycHash
 338 |     |         });
 339 |     |         
 340 |     |         // Update daily spending
 341 |     |         _updateDailySpending(msg.sender, msg.value);
 342 |     |         
 343 |     |         emit SwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore);
 344 |     |         emit RiskScoreSubmitted(swapId, riskScore, activeModelVersion);
 345 |     |         
 346 |     |         if (initialStatus == SwapStatus.Blocked) {
 347 |     |             emit SwapBlocked(swapId, riskScore, "High risk");
 348 |     |         }
 349 |     |         
 350 |     |         return swapId;
 351 |     |     }
 352 |     |     
 353 |     |     /**
 354 |     |      * @notice Initiate ERC20 swap
 355 | *   |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors
 356 | *r  |      */
 357 | *   |     function initiateSwapERC20(
 358 | *r  |         address seller,
 359 | *   |         address token,
 360 | *r  |         uint256 amount,
 361 | *   |         bytes32 hashlock,
 362 | *   |         uint256 timelock,
 363 | *r  |         uint256 riskScore,
 364 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 365 | *   |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 366 | *   |     ) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 367 |     |         if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();
 368 | *   |         if (seller == address(0)) revert InvalidSeller();
 369 | *   |         if (token == address(0)) revert InvalidToken();
 370 | *   |         if (timelock <= block.timestamp) revert InvalidTimelock();
 371 |     |         
 372 | *   |         // Verify oracle signature
 373 | *   |         if (!_verifyOracleSignature(msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash, oracleSignature)) revert InvalidSignature();
 374 |     |         
 375 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, seller, token, hashlock, timelock, block.timestamp));
 376 |     |         if (swaps[swapId].buyer != address(0)) revert SwapExists();
 377 |     |         
 378 |     |         SwapStatus initialStatus = _determineStatus(msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash);
 379 |     |         
 380 |     |         swaps[swapId] = Swap({
 381 |     |             buyer: msg.sender,
 382 |     |             status: initialStatus,
 383 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
 384 | *   |             assetType: AssetType.ERC20,
 385 |     |             seller: seller,
 386 |     |             token: token,
 387 |     |             amount: amount,
 388 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
 389 |     |             timelock: timelock,
 390 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 391 |     |             kycHash: kycHash
 392 |     |         });
 393 |     |         
 394 |     |         // Transfer tokens to escrow (using SafeERC20)
 395 |     |         IERC20(token).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
 396 |     |         
 397 |     |         emit SwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore);
 398 |     |         
 399 |     |         return swapId;
 400 |     |     }
 401 |     |     
 402 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 403 |     |     //                     SWAP COMPLETION / REFUND
 404 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 405 |     |     
 406 |     |     /**
 407 |     |      * @notice Complete swap by revealing preimage
 408 |     |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors
 409 |     |      */
 410 |     |     function completeSwap(bytes32 swapId, bytes32 preimage) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
 411 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 412 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapNotApproved();
 413 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= swap.timelock) revert SwapExpired();
 414 |     |         if (keccak256(abi.encodePacked(preimage)) != swap.hashlock) revert InvalidPreimage();
 415 |     |         
 416 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 417 |     |         
 418 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.seller, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 419 |     |         
 420 |     |         emit SwapCompleted(swapId, swap.seller);
 421 |     |     }
 422 |     |     
 423 |     |     /**
 424 |     |      * @notice Refund expired swap to buyer
 425 |     |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors
 426 |     |      */
 427 |     |     function refundSwap(bytes32 swapId) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
 428 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 429 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending && swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapCannotRefund();
 430 |     |         if (block.timestamp < swap.timelock) revert SwapNotExpired();
 431 |     |         
 432 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 433 |     |         
 434 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 435 |     |         
 436 |     |         emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
 437 |     |     }
 438 |     |     
 439 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 440 |     |     //                     APPROVAL WORKFLOW
 441 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 442 |     |     
 443 |     |     /**
 444 |     |      * @notice Approve a pending swap (for high-risk transactions)
 445 |     |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors and unchecked increment
 446 |     |      */
 447 |     |     function approveSwap(bytes32 swapId) external onlyApprover swapExists(swapId) {
 448 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 449 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending) revert SwapNotPending();
 450 |     |         if (approvals[swapId][msg.sender]) revert AlreadyApproved();
 451 |     |         
 452 |     |         approvals[swapId][msg.sender] = true;
 453 |     |         
 454 |     |         // Safe to use unchecked - approvalCount is uint8, max 255 approvers is reasonable
 455 |     |         unchecked {
 456 |     |             swap.approvalCount++;
 457 |     |         }
 458 |     |         
 459 |     |         emit SwapApproved(swapId, msg.sender);
 460 |     |         
 461 |     |         if (swap.approvalCount >= approvalThreshold) {
 462 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Approved;
 463 |     |         }
 464 |     |     }
 465 |     |     
 466 |     |     /**
 467 |     |      * @notice Reject a swap (refund buyer)
 468 |     |      */
 469 |     |     function rejectSwap(bytes32 swapId, string calldata reason) external onlyApprover swapExists(swapId) {
 470 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 471 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending) revert SwapNotPending();
 472 |     |         
 473 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 474 |     |         
 475 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 476 |     |         
 477 |     |         emit SwapBlocked(swapId, swap.riskScore, reason);
 478 |     |         emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
 479 |     |     }

 485 |     |     /**
 486 |     |      * @notice Raise dispute for a swap (requires Kleros arbitration fee)
 487 |     |      * @dev Gas optimized with custom errors
 488 |     |      */
 489 |     |     function raiseDispute(bytes32 swapId, string calldata evidence) external payable swapExists(swapId) {
 490 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 491 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending && swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapCannotDispute();
 492 |     |         if (msg.sender != swap.buyer && msg.sender != swap.seller) revert NotPartyToSwap();
 493 | *   |         if (address(disputeResolver) == address(0)) revert DisputeResolverNotSet();
 494 |     |         
 495 | *r  |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Disputed;
 496 |     |         
 497 |     |         uint256 disputeId = disputeResolver.createDispute{value: msg.value}(
 498 |     |             swapId,
 499 |     |             msg.sender,
 500 |     |             swap.amount,
 501 |     |             evidence
 502 |     |         );
 503 |     |         
 504 |     |         emit SwapDisputed(swapId, disputeId);
 505 |     |     }
 506 |     |     
 507 |     |     /**
 508 |     |      * @notice Execute dispute ruling (called by DisputeResolver)
 509 |     |      */
 510 |     |     function executeDisputeRuling(bytes32 swapId, bool releaseToSeller) external {
 511 |     |         if (msg.sender != address(disputeResolver)) revert OnlyDisputeResolver();
 512 |     |         
 513 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 514 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Disputed) revert SwapNotDisputed();
 515 |     |         
 516 |     |         if (releaseToSeller) {
 517 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 518 |     |             _transferFunds(swap.seller, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 519 |     |             emit SwapCompleted(swapId, swap.seller);
 520 |     |         } else {
 521 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 522 |     |             _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 523 | *   |             emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
 524 |     |         }
 525 |     |     }

 531 |     |     function _verifyOracleSignature(
 532 |     |         address buyer,
 533 | *   |         address seller,
 534 |     |         uint256 amount,
 535 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 536 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 537 |     |         bytes calldata signature
 538 |     |     ) internal view returns (bool) {
 539 | *   |         if (oracle == address(0)) return true; // Skip if oracle not set
 540 |     |         
 541 |     |         bytes32 messageHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(buyer, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash));
 542 |     |         bytes32 ethSignedHash = messageHash.toEthSignedMessageHash();
 543 |     |         address signer = ethSignedHash.recover(signature);
 544 |     |         
 545 |     |         return signer == oracle;
 546 |     |     }
 547 |     |     
 548 |     |     function _determineStatus(
 549 |     |         address buyer,
 550 |     |         address seller,
 551 |     |         uint256 amount,
 552 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 553 |     |         bytes32 kycHash
 554 |     |     ) internal returns (SwapStatus) {
 555 |     |         // Check policy engine if enabled
 556 |     |         if (policyEngineEnabled && address(policyEngine) != address(0)) {
 557 | *   |             (IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction action, ) = policyEngine.validateTransaction(
 558 | *   |                 buyer, seller, amount, riskScore, activeModelVersion, kycHash
 559 | *   |             );
 560 |     |             
 561 | *   |             if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Block) {
 562 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 563 |     |             } else if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Escrow || 
 564 |     |                        action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Review) {
 565 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Pending;
 566 |     |             }
 567 |     |             return SwapStatus.Approved;
 568 |     |         }
 569 |     |         
 570 |     |         // Fallback to simple threshold
 571 |     |         if (riskScore >= BLOCK_THRESHOLD) {
 572 |     |             return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 573 |     |         } else if (riskScore >= globalRiskThreshold) {
 574 |     |             return SwapStatus.Pending;
 575 |     |         }
 576 |     |         
 577 |     |         // Trusted users auto-approve
 578 |     |         if (userPolicies[buyer].trusted) {
 579 |     |             return SwapStatus.Approved;
 580 |     |         }
 581 |     |         
 582 |     |         return SwapStatus.Approved;
 583 |     |     }
 584 |     |     
 585 |     |     function _checkUserLimits(address user, uint256 amount) internal view {
 586 |     |         UserPolicy storage policy = userPolicies[user];
 587 |     |         
 588 |     |         if (policy.singleTxLimit > 0) {
 589 |     |             if (amount > policy.singleTxLimit) revert ExceedsSingleTxLimit();
 590 |     |         }
 591 |     |         
 592 |     |         if (policy.dailyLimit > 0) {
 593 |     |             uint256 currentDay;
 594 |     |             unchecked {
 595 |     |                 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 596 |     |             }
 597 |     |             uint128 spent = policy.lastResetDay == currentDay ? policy.dailySpent : 0;
 598 |     |             if (spent + amount > policy.dailyLimit) revert ExceedsDailyLimit();
 599 |     |         }
 600 |     |     }
 601 |     |     
 602 |     |     function _updateDailySpending(address user, uint256 amount) internal {
 603 |     |         UserPolicy storage policy = userPolicies[user];
 604 |     |         uint64 currentDay;
 605 |     |         unchecked {
 606 |     |             currentDay = uint64(block.timestamp / 1 days);
 607 |     |         }
 608 |     |         
 609 |     |         if (policy.lastResetDay < currentDay) {
 610 |     |             policy.dailySpent = 0;
 611 |     |             policy.lastResetDay = currentDay;
 612 |     |         }
 613 |     |         
 614 |     |         unchecked {
 615 |     |             policy.dailySpent += uint128(amount);
 616 |     |         }
 617 |     |     }
 618 |     |     
 619 |     |     function _transferFunds(address to, uint256 amount, address token, AssetType assetType) internal {
 620 |     |         if (assetType == AssetType.ETH) {
 621 |     |             (bool sent, ) = to.call{value: amount}("");
 622 |     |             if (!sent) revert ETHTransferFailed();
 623 | *   |         } else {
 624 |     |             IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);
 625 |     |         }
 626 |     |     }

  221 |     |     
  222 |     |     // ═══ ENHANCEMENT 1: Circuit Breaker - Daily Volume Limit ═══
  223 |     |     uint256 public dailyVolumeLimit;      // Max daily volume (0 = unlimited)
  224 |     |     uint256 public dailyVolume;           // Current day's volume
  225 |     |     uint256 public lastVolumeResetDay;    // Day number of last reset
  226 |     |     
  227 |     |     // ═══ ENHANCEMENT 2: Protocol Fees ═══
  228 |     |     uint256 public protocolFeeBps;        // Fee in basis points (100 = 1%)
  229 |     |     address public feeRecipient;          // Where fees are sent
  230 |     |     uint256 public totalFeesCollected;    // Tracking total fees
  231 |     |     
  232 |     |     // ═══ SECURITY ENHANCEMENT 4: Storage Gaps for Upgrades ═══
  233 |     |     uint256[44] private __gap;  // Reduced from 50 to account for new state variables
  234 |     |     
  235 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  236 |     |     //                           EVENTS
  237 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  238 |     |     event SwapInitiated(bytes32 indexed swapId, address indexed buyer, address indexed seller, uint256 amount, uint256 riskScore);
  239 |     |     event SwapApproved(bytes32 indexed swapId, address indexed approver);
  240 |     |     event SwapCompleted(bytes32 indexed swapId, address indexed seller);
  241 |     |     event SwapRefunded(bytes32 indexed swapId, address indexed buyer);
  242 |     |     event SwapDisputed(bytes32 indexed swapId, uint256 disputeId);
  243 |     |     event SwapBlocked(bytes32 indexed swapId, uint256 riskScore, string reason);
  244 |     |     event RiskScoreSubmitted(bytes32 indexed swapId, uint256 riskScore, string modelVersion);
  245 |     |     event PolicyEngineUpdated(address indexed newEngine);
  246 |     |     event DisputeResolverUpdated(address indexed newResolver);
  247 |     |     event UserPolicyUpdated(address indexed user);
  248 |     |     event ApproverAdded(address indexed approver);
  249 |     |     event ApproverRemoved(address indexed approver);
  250 |     |     // New security events
  251 |     |     event OracleAdded(address indexed oracle, uint256 index);
  252 |     |     event OracleRemoved(address indexed oracle);

  254 |     |     event TimelockQueued(bytes32 indexed operationId, TimelockOperation operation, uint256 executeAfter);
  255 |     |     event TimelockExecuted(bytes32 indexed operationId, TimelockOperation operation);
  256 |     |     /// @notice Emitted when a swap is submitted via a MEV-protected channel (e.g., Flashbots Protect)
  257 |     |     event MEVProtectedSwap(address indexed user, bytes32 indexed swapId, string submissionType);
  258 |     |     event TimelockCancelled(bytes32 indexed operationId);
