  260 |     |     // Enhancement events
  261 |     |     event CircuitBreakerTriggered(uint256 dailyVolume, uint256 dailyVolumeLimit);
  262 |     |     event DailyVolumeLimitUpdated(uint256 oldLimit, uint256 newLimit);
  263 |     |     event ProtocolFeeUpdated(uint256 oldFeeBps, uint256 newFeeBps);
  264 |     |     event FeeRecipientUpdated(address indexed oldRecipient, address indexed newRecipient);
  265 |     |     event FeeCollected(bytes32 indexed swapId, uint256 feeAmount, address indexed recipient);
  266 |     |     event DailyVolumeReset(uint256 day, uint256 previousVolume);
  267 |     |     
  268 |     |     // Rich Analytics Events
  269 |     |     event SwapAnalytics(
  270 |     |         bytes32 indexed swapId,
  271 |     |         address indexed buyer,
  272 |     |         address indexed seller,
  273 |     |         uint256 amount,
  274 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
  275 |     |         AssetType assetType,
  276 |     |         string modelVersion,
  277 |     |         uint256 timestamp
  278 |     |     );
  279 |     |     event UserRiskProfile(
  280 |     |         address indexed user,
  281 |     |         uint256 totalVolume,
  282 |     |         uint256 avgRiskScore,
  283 |     |         uint256 swapCount,
  284 |     |         bool kycVerified,
  285 |     |         uint256 timestamp
  286 |     |     );
  287 |     |     event ProtocolMetrics(
  288 |     |         uint256 dailyVolume,
  289 |     |         uint256 dailySwapCount,
  290 |     |         uint256 totalFeesCollected,
  291 |     |         uint256 activeOracleCount,
  292 |     |         uint256 timestamp
  293 |     |     );
  294 |     |     
  295 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  296 |     |     //                           MODIFIERS
  297 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  298 |     |     modifier onlyOracle() {
  299 |     |         if (!isOracle[msg.sender] && msg.sender != owner()) revert NotOracle();
  300 |     |         _;
  301 |     |     }
  302 |     |     
  303 |     |     modifier onlyApprover() {
  304 |     |         if (!isApprover[msg.sender] && msg.sender != owner()) revert NotApprover();
  305 |     |         _;
  306 |     |     }
  307 |     |     
  308 |     |     modifier swapExists(bytes32 swapId) {
  309 |     |         if (swaps[swapId].buyer == address(0)) revert SwapNotFound();
  310 |     |         _;
  311 |     |     }
  312 |     |     
  313 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  314 |     |     //                        INITIALIZATION
  315 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  316 |     |     
  317 |     |     /// @custom:oz-upgrades-unsafe-allow constructor
  318 |     |     constructor() {
  319 |     |         _disableInitializers();
  320 |     |     }
  321 |     |     
  322 |     |     function initialize(address _oracle) public initializer {
  323 |     |         __Ownable_init();
  324 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
  325 |     |         __ReentrancyGuard_init();
  326 |     |         __Pausable_init();
  327 |     |         
  328 |     |         // Set up first oracle
  329 |     |         oracles[0] = _oracle;
  330 |     |         oracleCount = 1;
  331 |     |         oracleThreshold = 1;  // Start with 1-of-1, upgrade to 2-of-3 later
  332 |     |         isOracle[_oracle] = true;
  333 |     |         
  334 |     |         approvalThreshold = 1;
  335 |     |         globalRiskThreshold = HIGH_RISK_THRESHOLD;
  336 |     |         activeModelVersion = "DQN-v1.0";
  337 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = false;
  338 |     |         
  339 |     |         // Add deployer as first approver
  340 |     |         approvers.push(msg.sender);
  341 |     |         isApprover[msg.sender] = true;
  342 |     |         // Set governance to owner by default (can be changed)
  343 |     |         governance = msg.sender;
  344 |     |         isUpgradeApprover[msg.sender] = true;
  345 |     |     }
  346 |     | 
  347 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  348 |     |     //                     UPGRADE TIMELOCK & GOVERNANCE
  349 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  350 |     | 
  351 |     |     /**
  352 |     |      * @notice Set governance address (multisig or DAO)
  353 |     |      */
  354 |     |     function setGovernance(address _governance) external onlyOwner {
  355 |     |         require(_governance != address(0), "Invalid governance");
  356 |     |         governance = _governance;
  357 |     |         isUpgradeApprover[_governance] = true;
  358 |     |     }
  359 |     | 
  360 |     |     /**
  361 |     |      * @notice Add or remove upgrade approvers (multisig/DAO members)
  362 |     |      */
  363 |     |     function setUpgradeApprover(address approver, bool approved) external {
  364 |     |         require(msg.sender == governance || msg.sender == owner(), "Not authorized");
  365 |     |         isUpgradeApprover[approver] = approved;
  366 |     |     }
  367 |     | 
  368 |     |     /**
  369 |     |      * @notice Queue an upgrade (timelocked)
  370 |     |      */
  371 |     |     function queueUpgrade(address newImplementation) external onlyOwner returns (bytes32 upgradeId) {
  372 |     |         require(newImplementation != address(0), "Invalid implementation");
  373 |     |         upgradeId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(newImplementation, block.timestamp));
  374 |     |         upgradeQueue[upgradeId] = UpgradeRequest({
  375 |     |             newImplementation: newImplementation,
  376 |     |             executeAfter: block.timestamp + UPGRADE_TIMELOCK,
  377 |     |             executed: false
  378 |     |         });
  379 |     |     }
  380 |     | 
  381 |     |     /**
  382 |     |      * @notice Approve and execute upgrade after timelock
  383 |     |      */
  384 |     |     function executeUpgrade(bytes32 upgradeId) external {
  385 |     |         require(isUpgradeApprover[msg.sender], "Not upgrade approver");
  386 |     |         UpgradeRequest storage req = upgradeQueue[upgradeId];
  387 |     |         require(req.newImplementation != address(0), "Not queued");
  388 |     |         require(block.timestamp >= req.executeAfter, "Timelock not expired");
  389 |     |         require(!req.executed, "Already executed");
  390 |     |         req.executed = true;
  391 |     |         _upgradeTo(req.newImplementation);
  392 |     |     }
  393 |     |     
  394 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  395 |     |     //              MULTI-ORACLE MANAGEMENT (Security Enhancement 1)
  396 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  397 |     |     
  398 |     |     /**
  399 |     |      * @notice Add a new oracle (HSM-backed address)
  400 |     |      * @param _oracle The oracle address (should be HSM-controlled)
  401 |     |      */
  402 |     |     function addOracle(address _oracle) external onlyOwner {
  403 |     |         require(oracleCount < MAX_ORACLES, "Max oracles reached");
  404 |     |         require(!isOracle[_oracle], "Already oracle");
  405 |     |         
  406 |     |         oracles[oracleCount] = _oracle;
  407 |     |         isOracle[_oracle] = true;
  408 |     |         
  409 |     |         emit OracleAdded(_oracle, oracleCount);
  410 |     |         
  411 |     |         unchecked {
  412 |     |             oracleCount++;
  413 |     |         }
  414 |     |     }
  415 |     |     
  416 |     |     /**
  417 |     |      * @notice Remove an oracle
  418 |     |      * @param _oracle The oracle address to remove
  419 |     |      */
  420 |     |     function removeOracle(address _oracle) external onlyOwner {
  421 |     |         require(isOracle[_oracle], "Not oracle");
  422 |     |         require(oracleCount > oracleThreshold, "Would break threshold");
  423 |     |         
  424 |     |         isOracle[_oracle] = false;
  425 |     |         
  426 |     |         // Compact the array
  427 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < oracleCount; i++) {
  428 |     |             if (oracles[i] == _oracle) {
  429 |     |                 oracles[i] = oracles[oracleCount - 1];
  430 |     |                 oracles[oracleCount - 1] = address(0);
  431 |     |                 break;
  432 |     |             }
  433 |     |         }
  434 |     |         
  435 |     |         unchecked {
  436 |     |             oracleCount--;
  437 |     |         }
  438 |     |         
  439 |     |         emit OracleRemoved(_oracle);
  440 |     |     }
  441 |     |     
  442 |     |     /**
  443 |     |      * @notice Set oracle threshold (e.g., 2-of-3)
  444 |     |      * @param _threshold Number of required signatures
  445 |     |      */
  446 |     |     function setOracleThreshold(uint256 _threshold) external onlyOwner {
  447 |     |         require(_threshold > 0 && _threshold <= oracleCount, "Invalid threshold");
  448 |     |         
  449 |     |         emit OracleThresholdUpdated(oracleThreshold, _threshold);
  450 |     |         oracleThreshold = _threshold;
  451 |     |     }
  452 |     |     
  453 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  454 |     |     //              TIMELOCK FUNCTIONS (Security Enhancement 2)
  455 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  456 |     |     
  457 |     |     /**
  458 |     |      * @notice Queue a timelocked admin operation
  459 |     |      * @param operation The type of operation
  460 |     |      * @param data The encoded operation data
  461 |     |      */
  462 |     |     function queueTimelock(TimelockOperation operation, bytes calldata data) external onlyOwner returns (bytes32 operationId) {
  463 |     |         operationId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(operation, data, block.timestamp));
  464 |     |         
  465 |     |         uint256 executeAfter;
  466 |     |         unchecked {
  467 |     |             executeAfter = block.timestamp + TIMELOCK_DELAY;
  468 |     |         }
  469 |     |         
  470 |     |         timelockQueue[operationId] = TimelockItem({
  471 |     |             executeAfter: executeAfter,
  472 |     |             data: data,
  473 |     |             executed: false
  474 |     |         });
  475 |     |         
  476 |     |         emit TimelockQueued(operationId, operation, executeAfter);
  477 |     |     }
  478 |     |     
  479 |     |     /**
  480 |     |      * @notice Execute a timelocked set oracle operation
  481 |     |      * @param operationId The operation ID from queueTimelock
  482 |     |      * @param newOracle The new oracle address
  483 |     |      */
  484 |     |     function executeSetOracle(bytes32 operationId, address newOracle) external onlyOwner {
  485 |     |         TimelockItem storage item = timelockQueue[operationId];
  486 |     |         if (item.executeAfter == 0) revert TimelockNotQueued();
  487 |     |         if (block.timestamp < item.executeAfter) revert TimelockNotExpired();
  488 |     |         require(!item.executed, "Already executed");
  489 |     |         
  490 |     |         // Verify the data matches
  491 |     |         require(keccak256(item.data) == keccak256(abi.encode(newOracle)), "Data mismatch");
  492 |     |         
  493 |     |         item.executed = true;
  494 |     |         
  495 |     |         // Add new oracle, don't remove old ones (use removeOracle separately)
  496 |     |         if (!isOracle[newOracle] && oracleCount < MAX_ORACLES) {
  497 |     |             oracles[oracleCount] = newOracle;
  498 |     |             isOracle[newOracle] = true;
  499 |     |             unchecked {
  500 |     |                 oracleCount++;
  501 |     |             }
  502 |     |             emit OracleAdded(newOracle, oracleCount - 1);
  503 |     |         }
  504 |     |         
  505 |     |         emit TimelockExecuted(operationId, TimelockOperation.SetOracle);
  506 |     |     }
  507 |     |     
  508 |     |     /**
  509 |     |      * @notice Execute a timelocked set policy engine operation
  510 |     |      */
  511 |     |     function executeSetPolicyEngine(bytes32 operationId, address _policyEngine) external onlyOwner {
  512 |     |         TimelockItem storage item = timelockQueue[operationId];
  513 |     |         if (item.executeAfter == 0) revert TimelockNotQueued();
  514 |     |         if (block.timestamp < item.executeAfter) revert TimelockNotExpired();
  515 |     |         require(!item.executed, "Already executed");
  516 |     |         require(keccak256(item.data) == keccak256(abi.encode(_policyEngine)), "Data mismatch");
  517 |     |         
  518 |     |         item.executed = true;
  519 |     |         policyEngine = IAMTTPPolicyEngine(_policyEngine);
  520 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = _policyEngine != address(0);
  521 |     |         
  522 |     |         emit PolicyEngineUpdated(_policyEngine);
  523 |     |         emit TimelockExecuted(operationId, TimelockOperation.SetPolicyEngine);
  524 |     |     }
  525 |     |     
  526 |     |     /**
  527 |     |      * @notice Execute a timelocked set dispute resolver operation
  528 |     |      */
  529 |     |     function executeSetDisputeResolver(bytes32 operationId, address _disputeResolver) external onlyOwner {
  530 |     |         TimelockItem storage item = timelockQueue[operationId];
  531 |     |         if (item.executeAfter == 0) revert TimelockNotQueued();
  532 |     |         if (block.timestamp < item.executeAfter) revert TimelockNotExpired();
  533 |     |         require(!item.executed, "Already executed");
  534 |     |         require(keccak256(item.data) == keccak256(abi.encode(_disputeResolver)), "Data mismatch");
  535 |     |         
  536 |     |         item.executed = true;
  537 |     |         disputeResolver = IAMTTPDisputeResolver(_disputeResolver);
  538 |     |         
  539 |     |         emit DisputeResolverUpdated(_disputeResolver);
  540 |     |         emit TimelockExecuted(operationId, TimelockOperation.SetDisputeResolver);
  541 |     |     }
  542 |     |     
  543 |     |     /**
  544 |     |      * @notice Cancel a queued timelock operation
  545 |     |      */
  546 |     |     function cancelTimelock(bytes32 operationId) external onlyOwner {
  547 |     |         require(timelockQueue[operationId].executeAfter != 0, "Not queued");
  548 |     |         require(!timelockQueue[operationId].executed, "Already executed");
  549 |     |         
  550 |     |         delete timelockQueue[operationId];
  551 |     |         
  552 |     |         emit TimelockCancelled(operationId);
  553 |     |     }
  554 |     |     
  555 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  556 |     |     //                     CONFIGURATION (Non-critical, no timelock)
  557 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  558 |     |     
  559 |     |     function setGlobalRiskThreshold(uint256 _threshold) external onlyOwner {
  560 |     |         require(_threshold <= RISK_SCALE, "Invalid threshold");
  561 |     |         globalRiskThreshold = _threshold;
  562 |     |     }
  563 |     |     
  564 |     |     function setActiveModelVersion(string calldata _version) external onlyOwner {
  565 |     |         activeModelVersion = _version;
  566 |     |     }
  567 |     |     
  568 |     |     function addApprover(address _approver) external onlyOwner {
  569 |     |         require(!isApprover[_approver], "Already approver");
  570 |     |         approvers.push(_approver);
  571 |     |         isApprover[_approver] = true;
  572 |     |         emit ApproverAdded(_approver);
  573 |     |     }
  574 |     |     
  575 |     |     function removeApprover(address _approver) external onlyOwner {
  576 |     |         require(isApprover[_approver], "Not approver");
  577 |     |         isApprover[_approver] = false;
  578 |     |         emit ApproverRemoved(_approver);
  579 |     |     }
  580 |     |     
  581 |     |     function setApprovalThreshold(uint256 _threshold) external onlyOwner {
  582 |     |         approvalThreshold = _threshold;
  583 |     |     }
  584 |     |     
  585 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  586 |     |     //            ENHANCEMENT 1: CIRCUIT BREAKER - DAILY VOLUME LIMIT
  587 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  588 |     |     
  589 |     |     /**
  590 |     |      * @notice Set daily volume limit (circuit breaker)
  591 |     |      * @param _limit Maximum daily volume in wei (0 = unlimited)
  592 |     |      */
  593 |     |     function setDailyVolumeLimit(uint256 _limit) external onlyOwner {
  594 |     |         emit DailyVolumeLimitUpdated(dailyVolumeLimit, _limit);
  595 |     |         dailyVolumeLimit = _limit;
  596 |     |     }
  597 |     |     
  598 |     |     /**
  599 |     |      * @notice Check and update daily volume, revert if limit exceeded
  600 |     |      * @param amount Transaction amount to add
  601 |     |      */
  602 |     |     function _checkDailyVolume(uint256 amount) internal {
  603 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
  604 |     |         
  605 |     |         // Reset daily volume if new day
  606 |     |         if (currentDay > lastVolumeResetDay) {
  607 |     |             emit DailyVolumeReset(currentDay, dailyVolume);
  608 |     |             dailyVolume = 0;
  609 |     |             lastVolumeResetDay = currentDay;
  610 |     |         }
  611 |     |         
  612 |     |         // Check limit (0 = unlimited)
  613 |     |         if (dailyVolumeLimit > 0) {
  614 |     |             if (dailyVolume + amount > dailyVolumeLimit) {
  615 |     |                 emit CircuitBreakerTriggered(dailyVolume + amount, dailyVolumeLimit);
  616 |     |                 revert DailyVolumeLimitExceeded();
  617 |     |             }
  618 |     |         }
  619 |     |         
  620 |     |         dailyVolume += amount;
  621 |     |     }
  622 |     |     
  623 |     |     /**
  624 |     |      * @notice Get current daily volume status
  625 |     |      */
  626 |     |     function getDailyVolumeStatus() external view returns (
  627 |     |         uint256 currentVolume,
  628 |     |         uint256 limit,
  629 |     |         uint256 remaining,
  630 |     |         uint256 resetTimestamp
  631 |     |     ) {
  632 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
  633 |     |         uint256 effectiveVolume = currentDay > lastVolumeResetDay ? 0 : dailyVolume;
  634 |     |         uint256 effectiveRemaining = dailyVolumeLimit > 0 
  635 |     |             ? (dailyVolumeLimit > effectiveVolume ? dailyVolumeLimit - effectiveVolume : 0)
  636 |     |             : type(uint256).max;
  637 |     |         
  638 |     |         return (
  639 |     |             effectiveVolume,
  640 |     |             dailyVolumeLimit,
  641 |     |             effectiveRemaining,
  642 |     |             (currentDay + 1) * 1 days
  643 |     |         );
  644 |     |     }
  645 |     |     
  646 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  647 |     |     //            ENHANCEMENT 2: PROTOCOL FEES
  648 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  649 |     |     
  650 |     |     /**
  651 |     |      * @notice Set protocol fee in basis points (100 = 1%)
  652 |     |      * @param _feeBps Fee in basis points (max 500 = 5%)
  653 |     |      */
  654 |     |     function setProtocolFee(uint256 _feeBps) external onlyOwner {
  655 |     |         if (_feeBps > 500) revert InvalidFeeConfiguration(); // Max 5%
  656 |     |         emit ProtocolFeeUpdated(protocolFeeBps, _feeBps);
  657 |     |         protocolFeeBps = _feeBps;
  658 |     |     }
  659 |     |     
  660 |     |     /**
  661 |     |      * @notice Set fee recipient address
  662 |     |      * @param _recipient Address to receive fees
  663 |     |      */
  664 |     |     function setFeeRecipient(address _recipient) external onlyOwner {
  665 |     |         require(_recipient != address(0), "Invalid recipient");
  666 |     |         emit FeeRecipientUpdated(feeRecipient, _recipient);
  667 |     |         feeRecipient = _recipient;
  668 |     |     }
  669 |     |     
  670 |     |     /**
  671 |     |      * @notice Calculate and deduct protocol fee
  672 |     |      * @param amount Gross amount
  673 |     |      * @param swapId Swap ID for event
  674 |     |      * @return netAmount Amount after fee deduction
  675 |     |      */
  676 |     |     function _collectFee(uint256 amount, bytes32 swapId) internal returns (uint256 netAmount) {
  677 |     |         if (protocolFeeBps == 0 || feeRecipient == address(0)) {
  678 |     |             return amount;
  679 |     |         }
  680 |     |         
  681 |     |         uint256 fee = (amount * protocolFeeBps) / 10000;
  682 |     |         totalFeesCollected += fee;
  683 |     |         
  684 |     |         emit FeeCollected(swapId, fee, feeRecipient);
  685 |     |         
  686 |     |         return amount - fee;
  687 |     |     }
  688 |     |     
  689 |     |     /**
  690 |     |      * @notice Withdraw collected fees (for ERC20 tokens held)
  691 |     |      */
  692 |     |     function withdrawFees(address token) external onlyOwner {
  693 |     |         require(feeRecipient != address(0), "No fee recipient");
  694 |     |         
  695 |     |         if (token == address(0)) {
  696 |     |             // This function is for ERC20 only; ETH fees are sent directly
  697 |     |             revert("Use feeRecipient for ETH");
  698 |     |         } else {
  699 |     |             uint256 balance = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
  700 |     |             // Note: This requires careful accounting to not withdraw escrowed funds
  701 |     |             // In production, track fees separately per token
  702 |     |             IERC20(token).safeTransfer(feeRecipient, balance);
  703 |     |         }
  704 |     |     }
  705 |     |     
  706 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  707 |     |     //                     USER POLICY FUNCTIONS
  708 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  709 |     |     
  710 |     |     function setUserPolicy(
  711 |     |         address user,
  712 |     |         uint128 dailyLimit,
  713 |     |         uint128 singleTxLimit,
  714 |     |         bool kycVerified,
  715 |     |         bool trusted
  716 |     |     ) external onlyOracle {
  717 |     |         userPolicies[user] = UserPolicy({
  718 |     |             dailyLimit: dailyLimit,
  719 |     |             singleTxLimit: singleTxLimit,
  720 |     |             dailySpent: userPolicies[user].dailySpent,
  721 |     |             lastResetDay: userPolicies[user].lastResetDay,
  722 |     |             kycVerified: kycVerified,
  723 |     |             trusted: trusted
  724 |     |         });
  725 |     |         emit UserPolicyUpdated(user);
  726 |     |     }
  727 |     |     
  728 |     |     function setKYCStatus(address user, bool verified) external onlyOracle {
  729 |     |         userPolicies[user].kycVerified = verified;
  730 |     |         emit UserPolicyUpdated(user);
  731 |     |     }
  732 |     |     
  733 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  734 |     |     //                     SWAP INITIATION (ETH)
  735 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  736 |     |     
  737 |     |     /**
  738 |     |      * @notice Initiate ETH swap with multi-oracle consensus signatures
  739 |     |      * @dev Security: Requires threshold oracle signatures with nonces
  740 |     |      * @param oracleSignatures Array of signatures from different oracles
  741 |     |      * @param nonce Unique nonce to prevent replay attacks
  742 |     |      * @param signatureTimestamp Timestamp when signatures were created
  743 |     |      */
  744 |     |     function initiateSwap(
  745 |     |         address seller,
  746 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
  747 |     |         uint256 timelock,
  748 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
  749 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
  750 |     |         bytes[] calldata oracleSignatures,
  751 |     |         uint256 nonce,
  752 |     |         uint256 signatureTimestamp
  753 |     |     ) external payable nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
  754 |     |         if (msg.value == 0) revert NoETHSent();
  755 |     |         if (seller == address(0)) revert InvalidSeller();
  756 |     |         if (timelock <= block.timestamp) revert InvalidTimelock();
  757 |     |         if (riskScore > RISK_SCALE) revert InvalidRiskScore();
  758 |     |         
  759 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify nonce hasn't been used (includes chainId to prevent cross-chain replay) ═══
  760 |     |         bytes32 nonceHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, nonce, block.chainid));
  761 |     |         if (usedNonces[nonceHash]) revert NonceAlreadyUsed();
  762 |     |         usedNonces[nonceHash] = true;
  763 |     |         emit NonceUsed(nonceHash, msg.sender);
  764 |     |         
  765 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify signature not expired ═══
  766 |     |         if (block.timestamp > signatureTimestamp + SIGNATURE_VALIDITY) revert SignatureExpired();
  767 |     |         
  768 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify multi-oracle signatures ═══
  769 |     |         if (!_verifyMultiOracleSignatures(
  770 |     |             msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore, kycHash, 
  771 |     |             nonce, signatureTimestamp, oracleSignatures
  772 |     |         )) revert InsufficientOracleSignatures();
  773 |     |         
  774 |     |         // Check user limits
  775 |     |         _checkUserLimits(msg.sender, msg.value);
  776 |     |         
  777 |     |         // ═══ ENHANCEMENT: Circuit breaker check ═══
  778 |     |         _checkDailyVolume(msg.value);
  779 |     |         
  780 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, seller, hashlock, timelock, block.timestamp));
  781 |     |         if (swaps[swapId].buyer != address(0)) revert SwapExists();
  782 |     |         
  783 |     |         // Determine initial status based on risk
  784 |     |         SwapStatus initialStatus = _determineStatus(msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore, kycHash);
  785 |     |         
  786 |     |         swaps[swapId] = Swap({
  787 |     |             buyer: msg.sender,
  788 |     |             status: initialStatus,
  789 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
  790 |     |             assetType: AssetType.ETH,
  791 |     |             seller: seller,
  792 |     |             token: address(0),
  793 |     |             amount: msg.value,
  794 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
  795 |     |             timelock: timelock,
  796 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
  797 |     |             kycHash: kycHash
  798 |     |         });
  799 |     |         
  800 |     |         // Update daily spending
  801 |     |         _updateDailySpending(msg.sender, msg.value);
  802 |     |         
  803 |     |         emit SwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, seller, msg.value, riskScore);
  804 |     |         emit RiskScoreSubmitted(swapId, riskScore, activeModelVersion);
  805 |     |         
  806 |     |         if (initialStatus == SwapStatus.Blocked) {
  807 |     |             emit SwapBlocked(swapId, riskScore, "High risk");
  808 |     |         }
  809 |     |         
  810 |     |         return swapId;
  811 |     |     }
  812 |     |     
  813 |     |     /**
  814 |     |      * @notice Initiate ERC20 swap with multi-oracle consensus
  815 |     |      */
  816 |     |     function initiateSwapERC20(
  817 |     |         address seller,
  818 |     |         address token,
  819 |     |         uint256 amount,
  820 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
  821 |     |         uint256 timelock,
  822 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
  823 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
  824 |     |         bytes[] calldata oracleSignatures,
  825 |     |         uint256 nonce,
  826 |     |         uint256 signatureTimestamp
  827 |     |     ) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
  828 |     |         // Emit MEVProtectedSwap for monitoring (off-chain infra can set submissionType to 'flashbots' or 'public')
  829 |     |         // For now, default to 'unknown' (frontend/SDK should pass info in future upgrade)
  830 |     |         emit MEVProtectedSwap(msg.sender, swapId, "unknown");
  831 |     |         if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();
  832 |     |         if (seller == address(0)) revert InvalidSeller();
  833 |     |         if (token == address(0)) revert InvalidToken();
  834 |     |         if (timelock <= block.timestamp) revert InvalidTimelock();
  835 |     |         
  836 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify nonce hasn't been used (includes chainId to prevent cross-chain replay) ═══
  837 |     |         bytes32 nonceHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, nonce, block.chainid));
  838 |     |         if (usedNonces[nonceHash]) revert NonceAlreadyUsed();
  839 |     |         usedNonces[nonceHash] = true;
  840 |     |         emit NonceUsed(nonceHash, msg.sender);
  841 |     |         
  842 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify signature not expired ═══
  843 |     |         if (block.timestamp > signatureTimestamp + SIGNATURE_VALIDITY) revert SignatureExpired();
  844 |     |         
  845 |     |         // ═══ SECURITY: Verify multi-oracle signatures ═══
  846 |     |         if (!_verifyMultiOracleSignatures(
  847 |     |             msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash,
  848 |     |             nonce, signatureTimestamp, oracleSignatures
  849 |     |         )) revert InsufficientOracleSignatures();
  850 |     |         
  851 |     |         // ═══ ENHANCEMENT: Circuit breaker check ═══
  852 |     |         _checkDailyVolume(amount);
  853 |     |         
  854 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, seller, token, hashlock, timelock, block.timestamp));
  855 |     |         if (swaps[swapId].buyer != address(0)) revert SwapExists();
  856 |     |         
  857 |     |         SwapStatus initialStatus = _determineStatus(msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash);
  858 |     |         
  859 |     |         swaps[swapId] = Swap({
  860 |     |             buyer: msg.sender,
  861 |     |             status: initialStatus,
  862 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
  863 |     |             assetType: AssetType.ERC20,
  864 |     |             seller: seller,
  865 |     |             token: token,
  866 |     |             amount: amount,
  867 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
  868 |     |             timelock: timelock,
  869 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
  870 |     |             kycHash: kycHash
  871 |     |         });
  872 |     |         
  873 |     |         // Transfer tokens to escrow (SafeERC20 handles non-standard tokens like USDT)
  874 |     |         IERC20(token).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
  875 |     |         
  876 |     |         emit SwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, seller, amount, riskScore);
  877 |     |         
  878 |     |         return swapId;
  879 |     |     }
  880 |     |     
  881 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  882 |     |     //                     SWAP COMPLETION / REFUND
  883 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  884 |     |     
  885 |     |     function completeSwap(bytes32 swapId, bytes32 preimage) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
  886 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
  887 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapNotApproved();
  888 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= swap.timelock) revert SwapExpired();
  889 |     |         if (keccak256(abi.encodePacked(preimage)) != swap.hashlock) revert InvalidPreimage();
  890 |     |         
  891 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
  892 |     |         
  893 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.seller, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
  894 |     |         
  895 |     |         emit SwapCompleted(swapId, swap.seller);
  896 |     |     }
  897 |     |     
  898 |     |     function refundSwap(bytes32 swapId) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
  899 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
  900 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending && swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapCannotRefund();
  901 |     |         if (block.timestamp < swap.timelock) revert SwapNotExpired();
  902 |     |         
  903 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
  904 |     |         
  905 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
  906 |     |         
  907 |     |         emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
  908 |     |     }
  909 |     |     
  910 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  911 |     |     //                     APPROVAL WORKFLOW
  912 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  913 |     |     
  914 |     |     function approveSwap(bytes32 swapId) external onlyApprover swapExists(swapId) {
  915 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
  916 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending) revert SwapNotPending();
  917 |     |         if (approvals[swapId][msg.sender]) revert AlreadyApproved();
  918 |     |             // Emit MEVProtectedSwap for monitoring (off-chain infra can set submissionType to 'flashbots' or 'public')
  919 |     |             // For now, default to 'unknown' (frontend/SDK should pass info in future upgrade)
  920 |     |             emit MEVProtectedSwap(msg.sender, swapId, "unknown");
  921 |     |         
  922 |     |         approvals[swapId][msg.sender] = true;
  923 |     |         
  924 |     |         unchecked {
  925 |     |             swap.approvalCount++;
  926 |     |         }
  927 |     |         
  928 |     |         emit SwapApproved(swapId, msg.sender);
  929 |     |         
  930 |     |         if (swap.approvalCount >= approvalThreshold) {
  931 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Approved;
  932 |     |         }
  933 |     |     }
  934 |     |     
  935 |     |     function rejectSwap(bytes32 swapId, string calldata reason) external onlyApprover swapExists(swapId) {
  936 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
  937 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending) revert SwapNotPending();
  938 |     |         
  939 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
  940 |     |         
  941 |     |         _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
  942 |     |         
  943 |     |         emit SwapBlocked(swapId, swap.riskScore, reason);
  944 |     |         emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
  945 |     |     }
  946 |     |     
  947 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  948 |     |     //            ENHANCEMENT 4: BATCH OPERATIONS (Gas Savings)
  949 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  950 |     |     
  951 |     |     /**
  952 |     |      * @notice Batch approve multiple swaps in one transaction
  953 |     |      * @param swapIds Array of swap IDs to approve
  954 |     |      */
  955 |     |     function batchApproveSwaps(bytes32[] calldata swapIds) external onlyApprover {
  956 |     |         require(swapIds.length <= 50, "Too many swaps");
  957 |     |         
  958 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < swapIds.length; i++) {
  959 |     |             bytes32 swapId = swapIds[i];
  960 |     |             Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
  961 |     |             
  962 |     |             // Skip if invalid or already processed
  963 |     |             if (swap.buyer == address(0)) continue;
  964 |     |             if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending) continue;
  965 |     |             if (approvals[swapId][msg.sender]) continue;
  966 |     |             
  967 |     |             approvals[swapId][msg.sender] = true;
  968 |     |             
  969 |     |             unchecked {
  970 |     |                 swap.approvalCount++;
  971 |     |             }
  972 |     |             
  973 |     |             emit SwapApproved(swapId, msg.sender);
  974 |     |             
  975 |     |             if (swap.approvalCount >= approvalThreshold) {
  976 |     |                 swap.status = SwapStatus.Approved;
  977 |     |             }
  978 |     |         }
  979 |     |     }
  980 |     |     
  981 |     |     /**
  982 |     |      * @notice Batch set user policies (oracle only)
  983 |     |      * @param users Array of user addresses
  984 |     |      * @param dailyLimits Array of daily limits
  985 |     |      * @param singleTxLimits Array of single transaction limits
  986 |     |      * @param kycStatuses Array of KYC verification statuses
  987 |     |      */
  988 |     |     function batchSetUserPolicies(
  989 |     |         address[] calldata users,
  990 |     |         uint128[] calldata dailyLimits,
  991 |     |         uint128[] calldata singleTxLimits,
  992 |     |         bool[] calldata kycStatuses
  993 |     |     ) external onlyOracle {
  994 |     |         require(users.length <= 100, "Too many users");
  995 |     |         require(
  996 |     |             users.length == dailyLimits.length &&
  997 |     |             users.length == singleTxLimits.length &&
  998 |     |             users.length == kycStatuses.length,
  999 |     |             "Array length mismatch"
 1000 |     |         );
 1001 |     |         
 1002 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < users.length; i++) {
 1003 |     |             userPolicies[users[i]] = UserPolicy({
 1004 |     |                 dailyLimit: dailyLimits[i],
 1005 |     |                 singleTxLimit: singleTxLimits[i],
 1006 |     |                 dailySpent: userPolicies[users[i]].dailySpent,
 1007 |     |                 lastResetDay: userPolicies[users[i]].lastResetDay,
 1008 |     |                 kycVerified: kycStatuses[i],
 1009 |     |                 trusted: false
 1010 |     |             });
 1011 |     |             emit UserPolicyUpdated(users[i]);
 1012 |     |         }
 1013 |     |     }
 1014 |     |     
 1015 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 1016 |     |     //                     DISPUTE RESOLUTION
 1017 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 1018 |     |     
 1019 |     |     function raiseDispute(bytes32 swapId, string calldata evidence) external payable swapExists(swapId) {
 1020 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 1021 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Pending && swap.status != SwapStatus.Approved) revert SwapCannotDispute();
 1022 |     |         if (msg.sender != swap.buyer && msg.sender != swap.seller) revert NotPartyToSwap();
 1023 |     |         if (address(disputeResolver) == address(0)) revert DisputeResolverNotSet();
 1024 |     |         
 1025 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Disputed;
 1026 |     |         
 1027 |     |         uint256 disputeId = disputeResolver.createDispute{value: msg.value}(
 1028 |     |             swapId,
 1029 |     |             msg.sender,
 1030 |     |             swap.amount,
 1031 |     |             evidence
 1032 |     |         );
 1033 |     |         
 1034 |     |         emit SwapDisputed(swapId, disputeId);
 1035 |     |     }
 1036 |     |     
 1037 |     |     function executeDisputeRuling(bytes32 swapId, bool releaseToSeller) external {
 1038 |     |         if (msg.sender != address(disputeResolver)) revert OnlyDisputeResolver();
 1039 |     |         
 1040 |     |         Swap storage swap = swaps[swapId];
 1041 |     |         if (swap.status != SwapStatus.Disputed) revert SwapNotDisputed();
 1042 |     |         
 1043 |     |         if (releaseToSeller) {
 1044 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 1045 |     |             _transferFunds(swap.seller, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 1046 |     |             emit SwapCompleted(swapId, swap.seller);
 1047 |     |         } else {
 1048 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 1049 |     |             _transferFunds(swap.buyer, swap.amount, swap.token, swap.assetType);
 1050 |     |             emit SwapRefunded(swapId, swap.buyer);
 1051 |     |         }
 1052 |     |     }
 1053 |     |     
 1054 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 1055 |     |     //                     INTERNAL FUNCTIONS
 1056 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 1057 |     |     
 1058 |     |     /**
 1059 |     |      * @notice Verify multi-oracle threshold signatures
 1060 |     |      * @dev Security: Requires oracleThreshold unique oracle signatures
 1061 |     |      */
 1062 |     |     function _verifyMultiOracleSignatures(
 1063 |     |         address buyer,
 1064 |     |         address seller,
 1065 |     |         uint256 amount,
 1066 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 1067 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 1068 |     |         uint256 nonce,
 1069 |     |         uint256 timestamp,
 1070 |     |         bytes[] calldata signatures
 1071 |     |     ) internal view returns (bool) {
 1072 |     |         if (oracleCount == 0) return true; // Skip if no oracles set
 1073 |     |         if (signatures.length < oracleThreshold) return false;
 1074 |     |         
 1075 |     |         // Create the message hash including nonce, timestamp, and chainId (HSM-compatible EIP-191)
 1076 |     |         // SECURITY: Including block.chainid prevents cross-chain replay attacks
 1077 |     |         bytes32 messageHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(
 1078 |     |             "\x19Ethereum Signed Message:\n32",
 1079 |     |             keccak256(abi.encodePacked(
 1080 |     |                 buyer, seller, amount, riskScore, kycHash, nonce, timestamp, block.chainid
 1081 |     |             ))
 1082 |     |         ));
 1083 |     |         
 1084 |     |         // Track which oracles have signed to prevent double-counting
 1085 |     |         address[MAX_ORACLES] memory signers;
 1086 |     |         uint256 validSignatures = 0;
 1087 |     |         
 1088 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < signatures.length; i++) {
 1089 |     |             address signer = ECDSA.recover(messageHash, signatures[i]);
 1090 |     |             
 1091 |     |             // Check if signer is a valid oracle
 1092 |     |             if (!isOracle[signer]) continue;
 1093 |     |             
 1094 |     |             // Check if this oracle already signed
 1095 |     |             bool alreadySigned = false;
 1096 |     |             for (uint256 j = 0; j < validSignatures; j++) {
 1097 |     |                 if (signers[j] == signer) {
 1098 |     |                     alreadySigned = true;
 1099 |     |                     break;
 1100 |     |                 }
 1101 |     |             }
 1102 |     |             if (alreadySigned) continue;
 1103 |     |             
 1104 |     |             signers[validSignatures] = signer;
 1105 |     |             unchecked {
 1106 |     |                 validSignatures++;
 1107 |     |             }
 1108 |     |             
 1109 |     |             if (validSignatures >= oracleThreshold) return true;
 1110 |     |         }
 1111 |     |         
 1112 |     |         return validSignatures >= oracleThreshold;
 1113 |     |     }
 1114 |     |     
 1115 |     |     function _determineStatus(
 1116 |     |         address buyer,
 1117 |     |         address seller,
 1118 |     |         uint256 amount,
 1119 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 1120 |     |         bytes32 kycHash
 1121 |     |     ) internal returns (SwapStatus) {
 1122 |     |         if (policyEngineEnabled && address(policyEngine) != address(0)) {
 1123 |     |             (IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction action, ) = policyEngine.validateTransaction(
 1124 |     |                 buyer, seller, amount, riskScore, activeModelVersion, kycHash
 1125 |     |             );
 1126 |     |             
 1127 |     |             if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Block) {
 1128 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 1129 |     |             } else if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Escrow || 
 1130 |     |                        action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Review) {
 1131 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Pending;
 1132 |     |             }
 1133 |     |             return SwapStatus.Approved;
 1134 |     |         }
 1135 |     |         
 1136 |     |         if (riskScore >= BLOCK_THRESHOLD) {
 1137 |     |             return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 1138 |     |         } else if (riskScore >= globalRiskThreshold) {
 1139 |     |             return SwapStatus.Pending;
 1140 |     |         }
 1141 |     |         
 1142 |     |         if (userPolicies[buyer].trusted) {
 1143 |     |             return SwapStatus.Approved;
 1144 |     |         }
 1145 |     |         
 1146 |     |         return SwapStatus.Approved;
 1147 |     |     }
 1148 |     |     
 1149 |     |     function _checkUserLimits(address user, uint256 amount) internal view {
 1150 |     |         UserPolicy storage policy = userPolicies[user];
 1151 |     |         
 1152 |     |         if (policy.singleTxLimit > 0) {
 1153 |     |             if (amount > policy.singleTxLimit) revert ExceedsSingleTxLimit();
 1154 |     |         }
 1155 |     |         
 1156 |     |         if (policy.dailyLimit > 0) {
 1157 |     |             uint256 currentDay;
 1158 |     |             unchecked {
 1159 |     |                 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 1160 |     |             }
 1161 |     |             uint128 spent = policy.lastResetDay == currentDay ? policy.dailySpent : 0;
 1162 |     |             if (spent + amount > policy.dailyLimit) revert ExceedsDailyLimit();
 1163 |     |         }
 1164 |     |     }
 1165 |     |     
 1166 |     |     function _updateDailySpending(address user, uint256 amount) internal {
 1167 |     |         UserPolicy storage policy = userPolicies[user];
 1168 |     |         uint64 currentDay;
 1169 |     |         unchecked {
 1170 |     |             currentDay = uint64(block.timestamp / 1 days);
 1171 |     |         }
 1172 |     |         
 1173 |     |         if (policy.lastResetDay < currentDay) {
 1174 |     |             policy.dailySpent = 0;
 1175 |     |             policy.lastResetDay = currentDay;
 1176 |     |         }
 1177 |     |         
 1178 |     |         unchecked {
 1179 |     |             policy.dailySpent += uint128(amount);
 1180 |     |         }
 1181 |     |     }
 1182 |     |     
 1183 |     |     function _transferFunds(address to, uint256 amount, address token, AssetType assetType) internal {
 1184 |     |         if (assetType == AssetType.ETH) {
 1185 |     |             (bool sent, ) = to.call{value: amount}("");
 1186 |     |             if (!sent) revert ETHTransferFailed();
 1187 |     |         } else {
 1188 |     |             // SafeERC20 handles non-standard tokens like USDT that don't return bool
 1189 |     |             IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);
 1190 |     |         }
 1191 |     |     }

 1199 |     |     }

 1218 |     |         address _policyEngine,
 1219 |     |         address _disputeResolver,
 1220 |     |         uint256 _oracleCount,
 1221 |     |         uint256 _oracleThreshold,
 1222 |     |         bool _policyEnabled,
 1223 |     |         uint256 _riskThreshold,
 1224 |     |         string memory _modelVersion
 1225 |     |     ) {

 1227 |     |             address(policyEngine),
 1228 |     |             address(disputeResolver),
 1229 |     |             oracleCount,
 1230 |     |             oracleThreshold,
 1231 |     |             policyEngineEnabled,
 1232 |     |             globalRiskThreshold,
 1233 |     |             activeModelVersion
 1234 |     |         );

 1236 |     |     
 1237 |     |     /**
 1238 |     |      * @notice Comprehensive health check for monitoring dashboards
 1239 |     |      * @return isPaused Whether contract is paused
 1240 |     |      * @return currentDailyVolume Today's transaction volume
 1241 |     |      * @return volumeLimit Daily volume limit (0 = unlimited)
 1242 |     |      * @return volumeRemaining Remaining volume capacity today
 1243 |     |      * @return activeOracles Number of active oracles
 1244 |     |      * @return requiredOracles Required oracle signatures
 1245 |     |      * @return contractETHBalance ETH held in escrow
 1246 |     |      * @return protocolFee Current protocol fee in basis points
 1247 |     |      * @return totalFees Total fees collected lifetime
 1248 |     |      */
 1249 |     |     function healthCheck() external view returns (
 1250 |     |         bool isPaused,
 1251 |     |         uint256 currentDailyVolume,
 1252 |     |         uint256 volumeLimit,
 1253 |     |         uint256 volumeRemaining,
 1254 |     |         uint256 activeOracles,
 1255 |     |         uint256 requiredOracles,
 1256 |     |         uint256 contractETHBalance,
 1257 |     |         uint256 protocolFee,
 1258 |     |         uint256 totalFees
 1259 |     |     ) {
 1260 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 1261 |     |         uint256 effectiveVolume = currentDay > lastVolumeResetDay ? 0 : dailyVolume;
 1262 |     |         uint256 remaining = dailyVolumeLimit > 0 
 1263 |     |             ? (dailyVolumeLimit > effectiveVolume ? dailyVolumeLimit - effectiveVolume : 0)
 1264 |     |             : type(uint256).max;
 1265 |     |         
 1266 |     |         return (
 1267 |     |             paused(),
 1268 |     |             effectiveVolume,
 1269 |     |             dailyVolumeLimit,
 1270 |     |             remaining,
 1271 |     |             oracleCount,
 1272 |     |             oracleThreshold,
 1273 |     |             address(this).balance,
 1274 |     |             protocolFeeBps,
 1275 |     |             totalFeesCollected
 1276 |     |         );
 1277 |     |     }

  11 |     | /**

  14 |     |  * @notice Enables AMTTP to propagate risk scores and block malicious addresses across chains
  15 |     |  * 
  16 |     |  * Architecture:
  17 |     |  * 
  18 |     |  *   Ethereum (Source)              Polygon (Destination)
  19 |     |  *   ┌─────────────────┐            ┌─────────────────┐

  21 |     |  *   │                 │            │                 │

  24 |     |  *   │ • Policy Sync   │            │ • Emit Events   │
  25 |     |  *   └─────────────────┘            └─────────────────┘
  26 |     |  *          │                              │
  27 |     |  *          ▼                              ▼
  28 |     |  *   ┌─────────────────┐            ┌─────────────────┐

  30 |     |  *   └─────────────────┘            └─────────────────┘
  31 |     |  */

  33 |     |     Initializable,
  34 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable,
  35 |     |     ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable,
  36 |     |     PausableUpgradeable,

  87 |     |

  89 |     |     mapping(uint16 => mapping(bytes => mapping(uint64 => bytes32))) public failedMessages;
  90 |     |     
  91 |     |     // ═══ PER-CHAIN RATE LIMITING ═══
  92 |     |     // chainId => blockNumber => count

  94 |     |     // chainId => max messages per block

  96 |     |

 184 |     |     /**
 185 |     |      * @notice Initialize the cross-chain contract
 186 |     |      * @param _lzEndpoint LayerZero endpoint address
 187 |     |      * @param _localChainId Local chain identifier
 188 |     |      * @param _policyEngine Policy engine contract address
 189 |     |      */
 190 |     |     function initialize(
 191 |     |         address _lzEndpoint,
 192 |     |         uint16 _localChainId,
 193 |     |         address _policyEngine
 194 |     |     ) external initializer {
 195 |     |         __Ownable_init();
 196 |     |         __ReentrancyGuard_init();
 197 |     |         __Pausable_init();
 198 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
 199 |     |         
 200 |     |         if (_lzEndpoint == address(0)) revert InvalidEndpoint();
 201 |     |         
 202 |     |         lzEndpoint = ILayerZeroEndpoint(_lzEndpoint);
 203 |     |         localChainId = _localChainId;
 204 |     |         policyEngine = _policyEngine;
 205 |     |         minDstGas = 200000; // Default minimum gas
 206 |     |         
 207 |     |         // Set default adapter params (version 1, 200k gas)
 208 |     |         defaultAdapterParams = abi.encodePacked(uint16(1), uint256(200000));
 209 |     |         
 210 |     |         // Initialize chain names
 211 |     |         _initializeChainNames();
 212 |     |     }
 213 |     |     
 214 |     |     // ============ External Functions ============
 215 |     |     
 216 |     |     /**
 217 |     |      * @notice Send risk score to another chain
 218 |     |      * @param _dstChainId Destination chain ID
 219 |     |      * @param _targetAddress Address being scored
 220 |     |      * @param _riskScore Risk score (0-1000)
 221 |     |      * @param _adapterParams LayerZero adapter parameters
 222 |     |      */
 223 |     |     function sendRiskScore(
 224 |     |         uint16 _dstChainId,
 225 |     |         address _targetAddress,
 226 |     |         uint256 _riskScore,
 227 |     |         bytes calldata _adapterParams
 228 |     |     ) external payable whenNotPaused nonReentrant {
 229 |     |         require(msg.sender == policyEngine || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 230 |     |         require(_riskScore <= 1000, "Invalid risk score");
 231 |     |         
 232 |     |         bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[_dstChainId];
 233 |     |         require(trustedRemote.length > 0, "Untrusted destination");
 234 |     |         
 235 |     |         // Encode payload
 236 |     |         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(
 237 |     |             MSG_RISK_SCORE,
 238 |     |             _targetAddress,
 239 |     |             _riskScore,
 240 |     |             block.timestamp
 241 |     |         );
 242 |     |         
 243 |     |         bytes memory adapterParams;
 244 |     |         if (_adapterParams.length > 0) {
 245 |     |             adapterParams = _adapterParams;
 246 |     |         } else {
 247 |     |             adapterParams = defaultAdapterParams;
 248 |     |         }
 249 |     |         
 250 |     |         // ═══ PER-CHAIN RATE LIMITING ═══
 251 |     |         // Call this before sending a cross-chain message
 252 |     |         _enforceChainRateLimit(_dstChainId);
 253 |     |         
 254 |     |         // ═══ PER-CHAIN PAUSE CHECK ═══
 255 |     |         _enforceChainNotPaused(_dstChainId);
 256 |     |         
 257 |     |         // Send via LayerZero
 258 |     |         lzEndpoint.send{value: msg.value}(
 259 |     |             _dstChainId,
 260 |     |             trustedRemote,
 261 |     |             payload,
 262 |     |             payable(msg.sender),
 263 |     |             address(0), // No ZRO payment
 264 |     |             adapterParams
 265 |     |         );
 266 |     |         
 267 |     |         bytes32 messageId = keccak256(abi.encode(
 268 |     |             _dstChainId,
 269 |     |             _targetAddress,
 270 |     |             _riskScore,
 271 |     |             block.timestamp
 272 |     |         ));
 273 |     |         
 274 |     |         emit RiskScoreSent(_dstChainId, _targetAddress, _riskScore, messageId);
 275 |     |     }
 276 |     |     
 277 |     |     /**
 278 |     |      * @notice Block an address globally across all chains
 279 |     |      * @param _dstChainIds Array of destination chain IDs
 280 |     |      * @param _targetAddress Address to block
 281 |     |      * @param _reason Reason for blocking
 282 |     |      */
 283 |     |     function blockAddressGlobally(
 284 |     |         uint16[] calldata _dstChainIds,
 285 |     |         address _targetAddress,
 286 |     |         string calldata _reason
 287 |     |     ) external payable whenNotPaused nonReentrant {
 288 |     |         require(msg.sender == policyEngine || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 289 |     |         // SECURITY: Prevent DoS via unbounded array
 290 |     |         require(_dstChainIds.length <= MAX_BATCH_CHAINS, "Too many chains");
 291 |     |         
 292 |     |         // Block locally first
 293 |     |         globallyBlocked[_targetAddress] = true;
 294 |     |         emit AddressBlockedGlobally(_targetAddress, localChainId, _reason);
 295 |     |         
 296 |     |         // Propagate to other chains
 297 |     |         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(
 298 |     |             MSG_BLOCK_ADDRESS,
 299 |     |             _targetAddress,
 300 |     |             _reason,
 301 |     |             block.timestamp
 302 |     |         );
 303 |     |         
 304 |     |         uint256 totalFee = 0;
 305 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < _dstChainIds.length; i++) {
 306 |     |             uint16 dstChainId = _dstChainIds[i];
 307 |     |             bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[dstChainId];
 308 |     |             
 309 |     |             if (trustedRemote.length > 0) {
 310 |     |                 (uint256 nativeFee, ) = lzEndpoint.estimateFees(
 311 |     |                     dstChainId,
 312 |     |                     address(this),
 313 |     |                     payload,
 314 |     |                     false,
 315 |     |                     defaultAdapterParams
 316 |     |                 );
 317 |     |                 totalFee += nativeFee;
 318 |     |             }
 319 |     |         }
 320 |     |         
 321 |     |         require(msg.value >= totalFee, "Insufficient fee");
 322 |     |         
 323 |     |         // Send to all chains
 324 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < _dstChainIds.length; i++) {
 325 |     |             uint16 dstChainId = _dstChainIds[i];
 326 |     |             bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[dstChainId];
 327 |     |             
 328 |     |             if (trustedRemote.length > 0 && !chainPaused[dstChainId]) {
 329 |     |                 (uint256 nativeFee, ) = lzEndpoint.estimateFees(
 330 |     |                     dstChainId,
 331 |     |                     address(this),
 332 |     |                     payload,
 333 |     |                     false,
 334 |     |                     defaultAdapterParams
 335 |     |                 );
 336 |     |                 
 337 |     |                 lzEndpoint.send{value: nativeFee}(
 338 |     |                     dstChainId,
 339 |     |                     trustedRemote,
 340 |     |                     payload,
 341 |     |                     payable(msg.sender),
 342 |     |                     address(0),
 343 |     |                     defaultAdapterParams
 344 |     |                 );
 345 |     |             }
 346 |     |         }
 347 |     |     }
 348 |     |     
 349 |     |     /**
 350 |     |     /**
 351 |     |      * @notice Unblock an address globally
 352 |     |      * @param _dstChainIds Array of destination chain IDs
 353 |     |      * @param _targetAddress Address to unblock
 354 |     |      */
 355 |     |     function unblockAddressGlobally(
 356 |     |         uint16[] calldata _dstChainIds,
 357 |     |         address _targetAddress
 358 |     |     ) external payable whenNotPaused nonReentrant {
 359 |     |         require(msg.sender == policyEngine || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 360 |     |         // SECURITY: Prevent DoS via unbounded array
 361 |     |         require(_dstChainIds.length <= MAX_BATCH_CHAINS, "Too many chains");
 362 |     |         
 363 |     |         // Unblock locally
 364 |     |         globallyBlocked[_targetAddress] = false;
 365 |     |         emit AddressUnblockedGlobally(_targetAddress, localChainId);
 366 |     |         
 367 |     |         // Propagate to other chains
 368 |     |         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(
 369 |     |             MSG_UNBLOCK_ADDRESS,
 370 |     |             _targetAddress,
 371 |     |             block.timestamp
 372 |     |         );
 373 |     |         
 374 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < _dstChainIds.length; i++) {
 375 |     |             uint16 dstChainId = _dstChainIds[i];
 376 |     |             bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[dstChainId];
 377 |     |             
 378 |     |             if (trustedRemote.length > 0 && !chainPaused[dstChainId]) {
 379 |     |                 (uint256 nativeFee, ) = lzEndpoint.estimateFees(
 380 |     |                     dstChainId,
 381 |     |                     address(this),
 382 |     |                     payload,
 383 |     |                     false,
 384 |     |                     defaultAdapterParams
 385 |     |                 );
 386 |     |                 
 387 |     |                 lzEndpoint.send{value: nativeFee}(
 388 |     |                     dstChainId,
 389 |     |                     trustedRemote,
 390 |     |                     payload,
 391 |     |                     payable(msg.sender),
 392 |     |                     address(0),
 393 |     |                     defaultAdapterParams
 394 |     |                 );
 395 |     |             }
 396 |     |         }
 397 |     |     }
 398 |     |     
 399 |     |     /**
 400 |     |      * @notice Propagate dispute result to other chains
 401 |     |      * @param _dstChainId Destination chain
 402 |     |      * @param _disputeId Dispute identifier
 403 |     |      * @param _approved Whether the transaction was approved
 404 |     |      */
 405 |     |     function propagateDisputeResult(
 406 |     |         uint16 _dstChainId,
 407 |     |         bytes32 _disputeId,
 408 |     |         bool _approved
 409 |     |     ) external payable whenNotPaused {
 410 |     |         require(msg.sender == policyEngine || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 411 |     |         
 412 |     |         bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[_dstChainId];
 413 |     |         require(trustedRemote.length > 0, "Untrusted destination");
 414 |     |         
 415 |     |         // ═══ PER-CHAIN PAUSE CHECK ═══
 416 |     |         _enforceChainNotPaused(_dstChainId);
 417 |     |         
 418 |     |         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(
 419 |     |             MSG_DISPUTE_RESULT,
 420 |     |             _disputeId,
 421 |     |             _approved,
 422 |     |             block.timestamp
 423 |     |         );
 424 |     |         
 425 |     |         lzEndpoint.send{value: msg.value}(
 426 |     |             _dstChainId,
 427 |     |             trustedRemote,
 428 |     |             payload,
 429 |     |             payable(msg.sender),
 430 |     |             address(0),
 431 |     |             defaultAdapterParams
 432 |     |         );
 433 |     |     }
 434 |     |     
 435 |     |     // ============ LayerZero Receiver ============
 436 |     |     
 437 |     |     /**
 438 |     |      * @notice Receive LayerZero message (called by endpoint)
 439 |     |      * @param _srcChainId Source chain ID
 440 |     |      * @param _srcAddress Source address in bytes
 441 |     |      * @param _nonce Message nonce
 442 |     |      * @param _payload Encoded message payload
 443 |     |      */
 444 |     |     function lzReceive(
 445 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 446 |     |         bytes calldata _srcAddress,
 447 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 448 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 449 |     |     ) external override {
 450 |     |         // Verify caller is the LayerZero endpoint
 451 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(lzEndpoint), "Invalid endpoint caller");
 452 |     |         
 453 |     |         // ═══ PER-CHAIN PAUSE CHECK ═══
 454 |     |         // Reject messages from paused chains
 455 |     |         _enforceChainNotPaused(_srcChainId);
 456 |     |         
 457 |     |         // Verify the source is trusted
 458 |     |         bytes memory trustedRemote = trustedRemotes[_srcChainId];
 459 |     |         require(
 460 |     |             trustedRemote.length == _srcAddress.length &&
 461 |     |             keccak256(trustedRemote) == keccak256(_srcAddress),
 462 |     |             "Untrusted source"
 463 |     |         );
 464 |     |         
 465 |     |         // Check nonce hasn't been processed
 466 |     |         require(
 467 |     |             receivedNonces[_srcChainId][_srcAddress] < _nonce,
 468 |     |             "Nonce already processed"
 469 |     |         );
 470 |     |         
 471 |     |         // Try to process, store on failure for retry
 472 |     |         try this.processMessage(_srcChainId, _srcAddress, _nonce, _payload) {
 473 |     |             receivedNonces[_srcChainId][_srcAddress] = _nonce;
 474 |     |         } catch (bytes memory reason) {
 475 |     |             // Store failed message for retry
 476 |     |             failedMessages[_srcChainId][_srcAddress][_nonce] = keccak256(_payload);
 477 |     |             emit MessageFailed(_srcChainId, _srcAddress, _nonce, _payload, reason);
 478 |     |         }
 479 |     |     }
 480 |     |     
 481 |     |     /**
 482 |     |      * @notice Process incoming cross-chain message
 483 |     |      * @dev External for try/catch, only callable by this contract
 484 |     |      */
 485 |     |     function processMessage(
 486 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 487 |     |         bytes calldata _srcAddress,
 488 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 489 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 490 |     |     ) external {
 491 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(this), "Only self");
 492 |     |         
 493 |     |         // Decode message type
 494 |     |         uint8 msgType = abi.decode(_payload, (uint8));
 495 |     |         
 496 |     |         if (msgType == MSG_RISK_SCORE) {
 497 |     |             _handleRiskScore(_srcChainId, _nonce, _payload);
 498 |     |         } else if (msgType == MSG_BLOCK_ADDRESS) {
 499 |     |             _handleBlockAddress(_srcChainId, _payload);
 500 |     |         } else if (msgType == MSG_UNBLOCK_ADDRESS) {
 501 |     |             _handleUnblockAddress(_srcChainId, _payload);
 502 |     |         } else if (msgType == MSG_POLICY_UPDATE) {
 503 |     |             _handlePolicyUpdate(_srcChainId, _nonce, _payload);
 504 |     |         } else if (msgType == MSG_DISPUTE_RESULT) {
 505 |     |             _handleDisputeResult(_srcChainId, _nonce, _payload);
 506 |     |         } else {
 507 |     |             revert InvalidPayload();
 508 |     |         }
 509 |     |     }
 510 |     |     
 511 |     |     /**
 512 |     |      * @notice Retry a failed message
 513 |     |      * @param _srcChainId Source chain ID
 514 |     |      * @param _srcAddress Source address
 515 |     |      * @param _nonce Message nonce
 516 |     |      * @param _payload Original payload
 517 |     |      */
 518 |     |     function retryMessage(
 519 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 520 |     |         bytes calldata _srcAddress,
 521 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 522 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 523 |     |     ) external nonReentrant {
 524 |     |         bytes32 payloadHash = failedMessages[_srcChainId][_srcAddress][_nonce];
 525 |     |         require(payloadHash != bytes32(0), "No failed message");
 526 |     |         require(keccak256(_payload) == payloadHash, "Payload mismatch");
 527 |     |         
 528 |     |         // Clear failed message
 529 |     |         delete failedMessages[_srcChainId][_srcAddress][_nonce];
 530 |     |         
 531 |     |         // Reprocess
 532 |     |         this.processMessage(_srcChainId, _srcAddress, _nonce, _payload);
 533 |     |         receivedNonces[_srcChainId][_srcAddress] = _nonce;
 534 |     |         
 535 |     |         emit RetryMessageSuccess(_srcChainId, _srcAddress, _nonce, payloadHash);
 536 |     |     }
 537 |     |     
 538 |     |     // ============ Internal Handlers ============
 539 |     |     
 540 |     |     function _handleRiskScore(
 541 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 542 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 543 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 544 |     |     ) internal {
 545 |     |         (, address targetAddress, uint256 riskScore, ) = abi.decode(
 546 |     |             _payload,
 547 |     |             (uint8, address, uint256, uint256)
 548 |     |         );
 549 |     |         
 550 |     |         // Store cross-chain risk score
 551 |     |         crossChainRiskScores[targetAddress][_srcChainId] = riskScore;
 552 |     |         lastRiskUpdate[targetAddress] = block.timestamp;
 553 |     |         
 554 |     |         emit RiskScoreReceived(_srcChainId, targetAddress, riskScore, _nonce);
 555 |     |         
 556 |     |         // If high risk, auto-block locally
 557 |     |         if (riskScore >= 700) {
 558 |     |             globallyBlocked[targetAddress] = true;
 559 |     |             emit AddressBlockedGlobally(
 560 |     |                 targetAddress,
 561 |     |                 _srcChainId,
 562 |     |                 "High cross-chain risk score"
 563 |     |             );
 564 |     |         }
 565 |     |     }
 566 |     |     
 567 |     |     function _handleBlockAddress(
 568 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 569 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 570 |     |     ) internal {
 571 |     |         (, address targetAddress, string memory reason, ) = abi.decode(
 572 |     |             _payload,
 573 |     |             (uint8, address, string, uint256)
 574 |     |         );
 575 |     |         
 576 |     |         globallyBlocked[targetAddress] = true;
 577 |     |         emit AddressBlockedGlobally(targetAddress, _srcChainId, reason);
 578 |     |     }
 579 |     |     
 580 |     |     function _handleUnblockAddress(
 581 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 582 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 583 |     |     ) internal {
 584 |     |         (, address targetAddress, ) = abi.decode(
 585 |     |             _payload,
 586 |     |             (uint8, address, uint256)
 587 |     |         );
 588 |     |         
 589 |     |         globallyBlocked[targetAddress] = false;
 590 |     |         emit AddressUnblockedGlobally(targetAddress, _srcChainId);
 591 |     |     }
 592 |     |     
 593 |     |     function _handlePolicyUpdate(
 594 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 595 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 596 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 597 |     |     ) internal {
 598 |     |         (, bytes32 policyHash, ) = abi.decode(
 599 |     |             _payload,
 600 |     |             (uint8, bytes32, uint256)
 601 |     |         );
 602 |     |         
 603 |     |         // Emit event for off-chain indexers to process
 604 |     |         emit PolicySynced(_srcChainId, policyHash, _nonce);
 605 |     |     }
 606 |     |     
 607 |     |     function _handleDisputeResult(
 608 |     |         uint16 _srcChainId,
 609 |     |         uint64 _nonce,
 610 |     |         bytes calldata _payload
 611 |     |     ) internal {
 612 |     |         (, bytes32 disputeId, bool approved, ) = abi.decode(
 613 |     |             _payload,
 614 |     |             (uint8, bytes32, bool, uint256)
 615 |     |         );
 616 |     |         
 617 |     |         emit DisputeResultReceived(_srcChainId, disputeId, approved, _nonce);
 618 |     |     }
 619 |     |     
 620 |     |     // ============ View Functions ============
 621 |     |     
 622 |     |     /**
 623 |     |      * @notice Get aggregated risk score from all chains
 624 |     |      * @param _address Address to check
 625 |     |      * @return maxScore Highest risk score across all chains
 626 |     |      * @return sourceChain Chain with highest score
 627 |     |      */
 628 |     |     function getAggregatedRiskScore(address _address) 
 629 |     |         external 
 630 |     |         view 
 631 |     |         returns (uint256 maxScore, uint16 sourceChain) 
 632 |     |     {
 633 |     |         uint16[] memory chains = getSupportedChains();
 634 |     |         
 635 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < chains.length; i++) {
 636 |     |             uint256 score = crossChainRiskScores[_address][chains[i]];
 637 |     |             if (score > maxScore) {
 638 |     |                 maxScore = score;
 639 |     |                 sourceChain = chains[i];
 640 |     |             }
 641 |     |         }
 642 |     |     }
 643 |     |     
 644 |     |     /**
 645 |     |      * @notice Check if an address is blocked globally
 646 |     |      * @param _address Address to check
 647 |     |      * @return True if blocked
 648 |     |      */
 649 |     |     function isGloballyBlocked(address _address) external view returns (bool) {
 650 |     |         return globallyBlocked[_address];
 651 |     |     }
 652 |     |     
 653 |     |     /**
 654 |     |      * @notice Estimate fee for sending risk score
 655 |     |      * @param _dstChainId Destination chain
 656 |     |      * @param _targetAddress Target address
 657 |     |      * @param _riskScore Risk score
 658 |     |      * @return nativeFee Fee in native token
 659 |     |      */
 660 |     |     function estimateRiskScoreFee(
 661 |     |         uint16 _dstChainId,
 662 |     |         address _targetAddress,
 663 |     |         uint256 _riskScore
 664 |     |     ) external view returns (uint256 nativeFee) {
 665 |     |         bytes memory payload = abi.encode(
 666 |     |             MSG_RISK_SCORE,
 667 |     |             _targetAddress,
 668 |     |             _riskScore,
 669 |     |             block.timestamp
 670 |     |         );
 671 |     |         
 672 |     |         (nativeFee, ) = lzEndpoint.estimateFees(
 673 |     |             _dstChainId,
 674 |     |             address(this),
 675 |     |             payload,
 676 |     |             false,
 677 |     |             defaultAdapterParams
 678 |     |         );
 679 |     |     }
 680 |     |     
 681 |     |     /**
 682 |     |      * @notice Get list of supported chains
 683 |     |      * @return Array of chain IDs
 684 |     |      */
 685 |     |     function getSupportedChains() public pure returns (uint16[] memory) {
 686 |     |         uint16[] memory chains = new uint16[](5);
 687 |     |         chains[0] = 101;  // Ethereum
 688 |     |         chains[1] = 109;  // Polygon
 689 |     |         chains[2] = 110;  // Arbitrum
 690 |     |         chains[3] = 184;  // Base
 691 |     |         chains[4] = 111;  // Optimism
 692 |     |         return chains;
 693 |     |     }
