 747 |     |     /**
 748 |     |      * @notice Set per-chain rate limit (max messages per block)
 749 |     |      */
 750 |     |     function setChainRateLimit(uint16 chainId, uint256 maxPerBlock) external onlyOwner {
 751 |     |         require(maxPerBlock > 0 && maxPerBlock < 1000, "Unreasonable limit");
 752 |     |         chainRateLimit[chainId] = maxPerBlock;
 753 |     |     }
 754 |     | 
 755 |     |     /**
 756 |     |      * @notice Internal function to enforce per-chain rate limiting
 757 |     |      * @param chainId The chain to rate limit for
 758 |     |      */
 759 |     |     function _enforceChainRateLimit(uint16 chainId) internal {
 760 |     |         uint256 limit = chainRateLimit[chainId];
 761 |     |         // If no limit is set, allow unlimited
 762 |     |         if (limit == 0) return;
 763 |     |         uint256 currentBlock = block.number;
 764 |     |         uint256 count = chainBlockMessageCount[chainId][currentBlock];
 765 |     |         if (count >= limit) {
 766 |     |             emit ChainRateLimitExceeded(chainId, currentBlock);
 767 |     |             revert("Rate limit exceeded");
 768 |     |         }
 769 |     |         chainBlockMessageCount[chainId][currentBlock] = count + 1;
 770 |     |     }

C:/amttp/contracts/AMTTPDisputeResolver.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.24;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
   5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   6 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
   7 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
   8 |     | import "./interfaces/IKleros.sol";
   9 |     | 
  10 |     | /**
  11 |     |  * @title AMTTPDisputeResolver
  12 |     |  * @notice Decentralized dispute resolution for high-risk transactions using Kleros
  13 |     |  * @dev Implements IArbitrable to receive rulings from Kleros Court
  14 |     |  * 
  15 |     |  * Flow:
  16 |     |  * 1. ML/Oracle flags transaction as HIGH_RISK
  17 |     |  * 2. Funds are escrowed in this contract
  18 |     |  * 3. Challenge window opens (e.g., 24 hours)
  19 |     |  * 4. Anyone can challenge → Creates Kleros dispute
  20 |     |  * 5. Kleros jurors vote on evidence
  21 |     |  * 6. Ruling executed: APPROVE (release funds) or REJECT (return to sender)
  22 |     |  */
  23 |     | contract AMTTPDisputeResolver is IArbitrable, IEvidence, Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {
  24 |     |     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  25 |     |     
  26 |     |     // ============================================================
  27 |     |     // CONSTANTS
  28 |     |     // ============================================================
  29 |     |     
  30 |     |     uint256 public constant RULING_OPTIONS = 2;  // Approve or Reject
  31 |     |     uint256 public constant RULING_APPROVE = 1;
  32 |     |     uint256 public constant RULING_REJECT = 2;
  33 |     |     
  34 |     |     // Kleros Court IDs (Sepolia testnet)
  35 |     |     // See: https://docs.kleros.io/products/court/kleros-court-v2
  36 |     |     bytes public constant EXTRA_DATA = abi.encodePacked(
  37 |     |         uint96(0),      // General Court
  38 |     |         uint96(3),      // Min jurors
  39 |     |         uint96(1)       // Dispute kit
  40 |     |     );
  41 |     |     
  42 |     |     // ============================================================
  43 |     |     // STATE
  44 |     |     // ============================================================
  45 |     |     
  46 |     |     IArbitrator public immutable arbitrator;
  47 |     |     
  48 |     |     uint256 public challengeWindow = 24 hours;
  49 |     |     uint256 public metaEvidenceID;
  50 |     |     
  51 |     |     enum EscrowStatus {
  52 |     |         None,
  53 |     |         Pending,        // Waiting for challenge window
  54 |     |         Challenged,     // Dispute created in Kleros
  55 |     |         Resolved,       // Ruling received
  56 |     |         Executed        // Funds transferred
  57 |     |     }
  58 |     |     
  59 |     |     struct EscrowedTransaction {
  60 |     |         bytes32 txId;
  61 |     |         address sender;
  62 |     |         address recipient;
  63 |     |         address token;          // address(0) for ETH, token address for ERC20
  64 |     |         uint256 amount;
  65 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
  66 |     |         uint256 createdAt;
  67 |     |         uint256 challengeDeadline;
  68 |     |         EscrowStatus status;
  69 |     |         uint256 disputeID;
  70 |     |         uint256 ruling;
  71 |     |         uint8 partialPct;       // percentage given to recipient when partial ruling is used (0-100)
  72 |     |         string evidenceURI;     // IPFS link to ML evidence
  73 |     |     }
  74 |     |     
  75 |     |     // txId => EscrowedTransaction
  76 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => EscrowedTransaction) public escrows;
  77 |     |     
  78 |     |     // disputeID => txId (for Kleros callback)
  79 |     |     mapping(uint256 => bytes32) public disputeToTx;
  80 |     |     
  81 |     |     // Evidence group counter
  82 |     |     uint256 public evidenceGroupCounter;
  83 |     |     
  84 |     |     // txId => evidenceGroupID
  85 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => uint256) public txToEvidenceGroup;
  86 |     |     
  87 |     |     // ============================================================
  88 |     |     // EVENTS
  89 |     |     // ============================================================
  90 |     |     
  91 |     |     event TransactionEscrowed(
  92 |     |         bytes32 indexed txId,
  93 |     |         address indexed sender,
  94 |     |         address indexed recipient,
  95 |     |         address token,
  96 |     |         uint256 amount,
  97 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
  98 |     |         uint256 challengeDeadline
  99 |     |     );
 100 |     | 
 101 |     |     event AppealRequested(bytes32 indexed txId, address indexed requester, uint256 timestamp);
 102 |     |     
 103 |     |     event TransactionChallenged(
 104 |     |         bytes32 indexed txId,
 105 |     |         address indexed challenger,
 106 |     |         uint256 disputeID
 107 |     |     );
 108 |     |     
 109 |     |     event TransactionApproved(
 110 |     |         bytes32 indexed txId,
 111 |     |         address indexed recipient,
 112 |     |         uint256 amount
 113 |     |     );
 114 |     |     
 115 |     |     event TransactionRejected(
 116 |     |         bytes32 indexed txId,
 117 |     |         address indexed sender,
 118 |     |         uint256 amount
 119 |     |     );
 120 |     |     
 121 |     |     event ChallengeWindowExpired(
 122 |     |         bytes32 indexed txId,
 123 |     |         address indexed recipient,
 124 |     |         uint256 amount
 125 |     |     );
 126 |     |     
 127 |     |     // ============================================================
 128 |     |     // CONSTRUCTOR
 129 |     |     // ============================================================
 130 |     |     
 131 |     |     /**
 132 |     |      * @param _arbitrator Address of Kleros Arbitrator contract
 133 |     |      * @param _metaEvidenceURI IPFS URI containing dispute metadata/rules
 134 |     |      */
 135 |     |     constructor(
 136 |     |         address _arbitrator,
 137 |     |         string memory _metaEvidenceURI
 138 |     |     ) {
 139 |     |         require(_arbitrator != address(0), "Invalid arbitrator");
 140 |     |         arbitrator = IArbitrator(_arbitrator);
 141 |     |         
 142 |     |         // Emit MetaEvidence for this dispute type
 143 |     |         emit MetaEvidence(metaEvidenceID, _metaEvidenceURI);
 144 |     |     }
 145 |     |     
 146 |     |     // ============================================================
 147 |     |     // ESCROW FUNCTIONS
 148 |     |     // ============================================================
 149 |     |     
 150 |     |     /**
 151 |     |      * @notice Escrow a high-risk transaction for potential dispute
 152 |     |      * @param _txId Unique transaction identifier
 153 |     |      * @param _recipient Intended recipient of funds
 154 |     |      * @param _riskScore Risk score from ML model (0-1000)
 155 |     |      * @param _evidenceURI IPFS URI with ML evidence (features, signals, etc.)
 156 |     |      */
 157 |     |     function escrowTransaction(
 158 |     |         bytes32 _txId,
 159 |     |         address _recipient,
 160 |     |         uint256 _riskScore,
 161 |     |         string calldata _evidenceURI
 162 |     |     ) external payable nonReentrant {
 163 |     |         require(msg.value > 0, "No funds sent");
 164 |     |         require(_recipient != address(0), "Invalid recipient");
 165 |     |         require(escrows[_txId].status == EscrowStatus.None, "TX already exists");
 166 |     |         
 167 |     |         uint256 deadline = block.timestamp + challengeWindow;
 168 |     |         uint256 evidenceGroup = ++evidenceGroupCounter;
 169 |     |         
 170 |     |         escrows[_txId] = EscrowedTransaction({
 171 |     |             txId: _txId,
 172 |     |             sender: msg.sender,
 173 |     |             recipient: _recipient,
 174 |     |             token: address(0),
 175 |     |             amount: msg.value,
 176 |     |             riskScore: _riskScore,
 177 |     |             createdAt: block.timestamp,
 178 |     |             challengeDeadline: deadline,
 179 |     |             status: EscrowStatus.Pending,
 180 |     |             disputeID: 0,
 181 |     |             ruling: 0,
 182 |     |             partialPct: 0,
 183 |     |             evidenceURI: _evidenceURI
 184 |     |         });
 185 |     |         
 186 |     |         txToEvidenceGroup[_txId] = evidenceGroup;
 187 |     |         
 188 |     |         emit TransactionEscrowed(
 189 |     |             _txId,
 190 |     |             msg.sender,
 191 |     |             _recipient,
 192 |     |             address(0),
 193 |     |             msg.value,
 194 |     |             _riskScore,
 195 |     |             deadline
 196 |     |         );
 197 |     |         
 198 |     |         // Auto-submit ML evidence
 199 |     |         if (bytes(_evidenceURI).length > 0) {
 200 |     |             emit Evidence(arbitrator, evidenceGroup, msg.sender, _evidenceURI);
 201 |     |         }
 202 |     |     }
 203 |     |     
 204 |     |     /**
 205 |     |      * @notice Challenge an escrowed transaction - creates Kleros dispute
 206 |     |      * @param _txId Transaction to challenge
 207 |     |      */
 208 |     |     function challengeTransaction(bytes32 _txId) external payable nonReentrant {
 209 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 210 |     |         
 211 |     |         require(escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Pending, "Not challengeable");
 212 |     |         require(block.timestamp < escrow.challengeDeadline, "Challenge window closed");
 213 |     |         
 214 |     |         // Get arbitration cost
 215 |     |         uint256 arbitrationCost = arbitrator.arbitrationCost(EXTRA_DATA);
 216 |     |         require(msg.value >= arbitrationCost, "Insufficient arbitration fee");
 217 |     |         
 218 |     |         // CEI: Update state BEFORE external call to prevent reentrancy
 219 |     |         escrow.status = EscrowStatus.Challenged;
 220 |     |         
 221 |     |         // Create dispute in Kleros
 222 |     |         uint256 disputeID = arbitrator.createDispute{value: arbitrationCost}(
 223 |     |             RULING_OPTIONS,
 224 |     |             EXTRA_DATA
 225 |     |         );
 226 |     |         
 227 |     |         // Update remaining state after getting disputeID
 228 |     |         escrow.disputeID = disputeID;
 229 |     |         disputeToTx[disputeID] = _txId;
 230 |     |         
 231 |     |         // Emit dispute event for Kleros
 232 |     |         emit Dispute(
 233 |     |             arbitrator,
 234 |     |             disputeID,
 235 |     |             metaEvidenceID,
 236 |     |             txToEvidenceGroup[_txId]
 237 |     |         );
 238 |     |         
 239 |     |         emit TransactionChallenged(_txId, msg.sender, disputeID);
 240 |     |         
 241 |     |         // Refund excess
 242 |     |         if (msg.value > arbitrationCost) {
 243 |     |             payable(msg.sender).transfer(msg.value - arbitrationCost);
 244 |     |         }
 245 |     |     }
 246 |     | 
 247 |     |     /**
 248 |     |      * @notice Escrow an ERC20 token for potential dispute
 249 |     |      * @param _txId Unique transaction identifier
 250 |     |      * @param _recipient Intended recipient of funds
 251 |     |      * @param _token ERC20 token address
 252 |     |      * @param _amount Amount of tokens to escrow
 253 |     |      * @param _riskScore Risk score from ML model (0-1000)
 254 |     |      * @param _evidenceURI IPFS URI with ML evidence
 255 |     |      */
 256 |     |     function escrowTransactionERC20(
 257 |     |         bytes32 _txId,
 258 |     |         address _recipient,
 259 |     |         address _token,
 260 |     |         uint256 _amount,
 261 |     |         uint256 _riskScore,
 262 |     |         string calldata _evidenceURI
 263 |     |     ) external nonReentrant {
 264 |     |         require(_amount > 0, "No funds sent");
 265 |     |         require(_recipient != address(0), "Invalid recipient");
 266 |     |         require(_token != address(0), "Invalid token");
 267 |     |         require(escrows[_txId].status == EscrowStatus.None, "TX already exists");
 268 |     | 
 269 |     |         uint256 deadline = block.timestamp + challengeWindow;
 270 |     |         uint256 evidenceGroup = ++evidenceGroupCounter;
 271 |     | 
 272 |     |         // Transfer tokens to contract
 273 |     |         IERC20(_token).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), _amount);
 274 |     | 
 275 |     |         escrows[_txId] = EscrowedTransaction({
 276 |     |             txId: _txId,
 277 |     |             sender: msg.sender,
 278 |     |             recipient: _recipient,
 279 |     |             token: _token,
 280 |     |             amount: _amount,
 281 |     |             riskScore: _riskScore,
 282 |     |             createdAt: block.timestamp,
 283 |     |             challengeDeadline: deadline,
 284 |     |             status: EscrowStatus.Pending,
 285 |     |             disputeID: 0,
 286 |     |             ruling: 0,
 287 |     |             partialPct: 0,
 288 |     |             evidenceURI: _evidenceURI
 289 |     |         });
 290 |     | 
 291 |     |         txToEvidenceGroup[_txId] = evidenceGroup;
 292 |     | 
 293 |     |         emit TransactionEscrowed(
 294 |     |             _txId,
 295 |     |             msg.sender,
 296 |     |             _recipient,
 297 |     |             _token,
 298 |     |             _amount,
 299 |     |             _riskScore,
 300 |     |             deadline
 301 |     |         );
 302 |     | 
 303 |     |         if (bytes(_evidenceURI).length > 0) {
 304 |     |             emit Evidence(arbitrator, evidenceGroup, msg.sender, _evidenceURI);
 305 |     |         }
 306 |     |     }
 307 |     |     
 308 |     |     /**
 309 |     |      * @notice Submit additional evidence for a dispute
 310 |     |      * @param _txId Transaction ID
 311 |     |      * @param _evidenceURI IPFS URI with evidence
 312 |     |      */
 313 |     |     function submitEvidence(bytes32 _txId, string calldata _evidenceURI) external {
 314 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 315 |     |         require(escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Challenged, "No active dispute");
 316 |     |         require(
 317 |     |             msg.sender == escrow.sender || msg.sender == escrow.recipient,
 318 |     |             "Not a party"
 319 |     |         );
 320 |     |         
 321 |     |         emit Evidence(
 322 |     |             arbitrator,
 323 |     |             txToEvidenceGroup[_txId],
 324 |     |             msg.sender,
 325 |     |             _evidenceURI
 326 |     |         );
 327 |     |     }
 328 |     |     
 329 |     |     // ============================================================
 330 |     |     // KLEROS CALLBACK
 331 |     |     // ============================================================
 332 |     |     
 333 |     |     /**
 334 |     |      * @notice Called by Kleros when jurors reach a ruling
 335 |     |      * @param _disputeID The dispute ID
 336 |     |      * @param _ruling 1 = Approve (release to recipient), 2 = Reject (return to sender)
 337 |     |      */
 338 |     |     function rule(uint256 _disputeID, uint256 _ruling) external override {
 339 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(arbitrator), "Only arbitrator");
 340 |     |         
 341 |     |         bytes32 txId = disputeToTx[_disputeID];
 342 |     |         require(txId != bytes32(0), "Unknown dispute");
 343 |     |         
 344 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[txId];
 345 |     |         require(escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Challenged, "Not challenged");
 346 |     |         
 347 |     |         escrow.status = EscrowStatus.Resolved;
 348 |     |         escrow.ruling = _ruling;
 349 |     |         
 350 |     |     // If arbitrator provided extra metadata indicating a partial pct, we don't have it here.
 351 |     |     // Owners/operators can set defaultPartialPct per-escrow in advance if needed via setEscrowPartialPct.
 352 |     |         
 353 |     |         emit Ruling(arbitrator, _disputeID, _ruling);
 354 |     |         
 355 |     |         // Execute ruling
 356 |     |         _executeRuling(txId);
 357 |     |     }
 358 |     |     
 359 |     |     // ============================================================
 360 |     |     // EXECUTION
 361 |     |     // ============================================================
 362 |     |     
 363 |     |     /**
 364 |     |      * @notice Execute the ruling or release after challenge window
 365 |     |      * @param _txId Transaction to execute
 366 |     |      */
 367 |     |     function executeTransaction(bytes32 _txId) external nonReentrant {
 368 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 369 |     |         
 370 |     |         if (escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Pending) {
 371 |     |             // No challenge - release after window expires
 372 |     |             require(
 373 |     |                 block.timestamp >= escrow.challengeDeadline,
 374 |     |                 "Challenge window active"
 375 |     |             );
 376 |     |             
 377 |     |             // Cache values before state change (CEI pattern)
 378 |     |             address recipient = escrow.recipient;
 379 |     |             uint256 amount = escrow.amount;
 380 |     |             
 381 |     |             escrow.status = EscrowStatus.Executed;
 382 |     |             
 383 |     |             // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 384 |     |             emit ChallengeWindowExpired(_txId, recipient, amount);
 385 |     |             
 386 |     |             payable(recipient).transfer(amount);
 387 |     |             
 388 |     |         } else if (escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Resolved) {
 389 |     |             _executeRuling(_txId);
 390 |     |         } else {
 391 |     |             revert("Cannot execute");
 392 |     |         }
 393 |     |     }
 394 |     | 
 395 |     |     /**
 396 |     |      * @notice Request an appeal (off-chain coordination required to submit evidence to Kleros)
 397 |     |      * @param _txId Transaction ID to appeal
 398 |     |      */
 399 |     |     function requestAppeal(bytes32 _txId) external {
 400 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 401 |     |         require(escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Resolved, "Not resolved");
 402 |     |         require(msg.sender == escrow.sender || msg.sender == escrow.recipient, "Not a party");
 403 |     | 
 404 |     |         emit AppealRequested(_txId, msg.sender, block.timestamp);
 405 |     |     }
 406 |     | 
 407 |     |     /**
 408 |     |      * @notice Set partial percentage for an escrow (only owner/operator). Useful when arbitrator indicates partial split off-chain.
 409 |     |      */
 410 |     |     function setEscrowPartialPct(bytes32 _txId, uint8 _pct) external onlyOwner {
 411 |     |         require(_pct <= 100, "Invalid pct");
 412 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 413 |     |         escrow.partialPct = _pct;
 414 |     |     }
 415 |     |     
 416 |     |     function _executeRuling(bytes32 _txId) internal {
 417 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage escrow = escrows[_txId];
 418 |     |         
 419 |     |         if (escrow.status == EscrowStatus.Executed) return;
 420 |     |         
 421 |     |         escrow.status = EscrowStatus.Executed;
 422 |     |         
 423 |     |         // Cache values to prevent reentrancy issues
 424 |     |         address recipient = escrow.recipient;
 425 |     |         address sender = escrow.sender;
 426 |     |         uint256 amount = escrow.amount;
 427 |     |         address token = escrow.token;
 428 |     |         uint256 ruling = escrow.ruling;
 429 |     |         uint256 pct = escrow.partialPct;
 430 |     |         
 431 |     |         if (ruling == RULING_APPROVE) {
 432 |     |             // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 433 |     |             emit TransactionApproved(_txId, recipient, amount);
 434 |     |             // Explicitly approved by arbitrator - release to recipient
 435 |     |             if (token == address(0)) {
 436 |     |                 payable(recipient).transfer(amount);
 437 |     |             } else {
 438 |     |                 IERC20(token).safeTransfer(recipient, amount);
 439 |     |             }
 440 |     |         } else if (ruling == RULING_REJECT) {
 441 |     |             // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 442 |     |             emit TransactionRejected(_txId, sender, amount);
 443 |     |             // Reject - return to sender
 444 |     |             if (token == address(0)) {
 445 |     |                 payable(sender).transfer(amount);
 446 |     |             } else {
 447 |     |                 IERC20(token).safeTransfer(sender, amount);
 448 |     |             }
 449 |     |         } else if (ruling == 3) {
 450 |     |             // Partial ruling - use partialPct stored on escrow (default 0 means fully reject)
 451 |     |             if (pct == 0) {
 452 |     |                 // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 453 |     |                 emit TransactionRejected(_txId, sender, amount);
 454 |     |                 // treat as reject
 455 |     |                 if (token == address(0)) {
 456 |     |                     payable(sender).transfer(amount);
 457 |     |                 } else {
 458 |     |                     IERC20(token).safeTransfer(sender, amount);
 459 |     |                 }
 460 |     |             } else {
 461 |     |                 uint256 toRecipient = (amount * pct) / 100;
 462 |     |                 uint256 toSender = amount - toRecipient;
 463 |     |                 // Emit events BEFORE external calls (CEI pattern)
 464 |     |                 emit TransactionApproved(_txId, recipient, toRecipient);
 465 |     |                 emit TransactionRejected(_txId, sender, toSender);
 466 |     |                 if (token == address(0)) {
 467 |     |                     if (toRecipient > 0) payable(recipient).transfer(toRecipient);
 468 |     |                     if (toSender > 0) payable(sender).transfer(toSender);
 469 |     |                 } else {
 470 |     |                     if (toRecipient > 0) IERC20(token).safeTransfer(recipient, toRecipient);
 471 |     |                     if (toSender > 0) IERC20(token).safeTransfer(sender, toSender);
 472 |     |                 }
 473 |     |             }
 474 |     |         } else {
 475 |     |             // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 476 |     |             emit TransactionRejected(_txId, sender, amount);
 477 |     |             // Unknown ruling - default to reject
 478 |     |             if (token == address(0)) {
 479 |     |                 payable(sender).transfer(amount);
 480 |     |             } else {
 481 |     |                 IERC20(token).safeTransfer(sender, amount);
 482 |     |             }
 483 |     |         }
 484 |     |     }
 485 |     |     
 486 |     |     // ============================================================
 487 |     |     // VIEW FUNCTIONS
 488 |     |     // ============================================================
 489 |     |     
 490 |     |     /**
 491 |     |      * @notice Get the cost to challenge a transaction
 492 |     |      */
 493 |     |     function getChallengeCost() external view returns (uint256) {
 494 |     |         return arbitrator.arbitrationCost(EXTRA_DATA);
 495 |     |     }
 496 |     |     
 497 |     |     /**
 498 |     |      * @notice Get escrow details
 499 |     |      */
 500 |     |     function getEscrow(bytes32 _txId) external view returns (
 501 |     |         address sender,
 502 |     |         address recipient,
 503 |     |         address token,
 504 |     |         uint256 amount,
 505 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 506 |     |         uint256 challengeDeadline,
 507 |     |         EscrowStatus status,
 508 |     |         uint256 disputeID,
 509 |     |         uint256 ruling
 510 |     |     ) {
 511 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage e = escrows[_txId];
 512 |     |         return (
 513 |     |             e.sender,
 514 |     |             e.recipient,
 515 |     |             e.token,
 516 |     |             e.amount,
 517 |     |             e.riskScore,
 518 |     |             e.challengeDeadline,
 519 |     |             e.status,
 520 |     |             e.disputeID,
 521 |     |             e.ruling
 522 |     |         );
 523 |     |     }
 524 |     |     
 525 |     |     /**
 526 |     |      * @notice Check if transaction can be executed
 527 |     |      */
 528 |     |     function canExecute(bytes32 _txId) external view returns (bool) {
 529 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage e = escrows[_txId];
 530 |     |         
 531 |     |         if (e.status == EscrowStatus.Pending) {
 532 |     |             return block.timestamp >= e.challengeDeadline;
 533 |     |         } else if (e.status == EscrowStatus.Resolved) {
 534 |     |             return true;
 535 |     |         }
 536 |     |         return false;
 537 |     |     }
 538 |     |     
 539 |     |     // ============================================================
 540 |     |     // ADMIN
 541 |     |     // ============================================================
 542 |     |     
 543 |     |     /**
 544 |     |      * @notice Update challenge window (owner only)
 545 |     |      */
 546 |     |     function setChallengeWindow(uint256 _window) external onlyOwner {
 547 |     |         require(_window >= 1 hours && _window <= 7 days, "Invalid window");
 548 |     |         challengeWindow = _window;
 549 |     |     }
 550 |     |     
 551 |     |     /**
 552 |     |      * @notice Update meta evidence for new dispute types
 553 |     |      */
 554 |     |     function updateMetaEvidence(string calldata _uri) external onlyOwner {
 555 |     |         metaEvidenceID++;
 556 |     |         emit MetaEvidence(metaEvidenceID, _uri);
 557 |     |     }
 558 |     |     
 559 |     |     /**
 560 |     |      * @notice Emergency withdraw (owner only, for stuck funds)
 561 |     |      * @dev Returns funds to original sender after 30 days past deadline
 562 |     |      */
 563 |     |     function emergencyWithdraw(bytes32 _txId) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
 564 |     |         EscrowedTransaction storage e = escrows[_txId];
 565 |     |         require(e.status != EscrowStatus.Executed, "Already executed");
 566 |     |         require(
 567 |     |             block.timestamp > e.challengeDeadline + 30 days,
 568 |     |             "Too early"
 569 |     |         );
 570 |     |         
 571 |     |         // Cache values before state change (CEI pattern)
 572 |     |         address sender = e.sender;
 573 |     |         uint256 amount = e.amount;
 574 |     |         address token = e.token;
 575 |     |         
 576 |     |         e.status = EscrowStatus.Executed;
 577 |     |         
 578 |     |         // Emit event BEFORE external call (CEI pattern)
 579 |     |         emit TransactionRejected(_txId, sender, amount);
 580 |     |         
 581 |     |         // SECURITY FIX: Return funds to original sender, not owner
 582 |     |         if (token == address(0)) {
 583 |     |             payable(sender).transfer(amount);
 584 |     |         } else {
 585 |     |             IERC20(token).safeTransfer(sender, amount);
 586 |     |         }
 587 |     |     }
 588 |     |     
 589 |     |     // ============================================================
 590 |     |     // HEALTH CHECK & ANALYTICS
 591 |     |     // ============================================================
 592 |     |     
 593 |     |     /**
 594 |     |      * @notice Get contract health status for monitoring
 595 |     |      * @return arbitratorAddress The Kleros arbitrator address
 596 |     |      * @return currentChallengeWindow Current challenge window duration
 597 |     |      * @return currentMetaEvidenceID Current meta evidence version
 598 |     |      * @return contractBalance ETH balance held in contract
 599 |     |      * @return isOperational Whether the contract can accept new escrows
 600 |     |      */
 601 |     |     function healthCheck() external view returns (
 602 |     |         address arbitratorAddress,
 603 |     |         uint256 currentChallengeWindow,
 604 |     |         uint256 currentMetaEvidenceID,
 605 |     |         uint256 contractBalance,
 606 |     |         bool isOperational
 607 |     |     ) {
 608 |     |         return (
 609 |     |             address(arbitrator),
 610 |     |             challengeWindow,
 611 |     |             metaEvidenceID,
 612 |     |             address(this).balance,
 613 |     |             address(arbitrator) != address(0)
 614 |     |         );
 615 |     |     }
 616 |     |     
 617 |     |     /**
 618 |     |      * @notice Get token balance held in contract (for ERC20 escrows)
 619 |     |      * @param _token Token address to check
 620 |     |      */
 621 |     |     function getTokenBalance(address _token) external view returns (uint256) {
 622 |     |         return IERC20(_token).balanceOf(address(this));
 623 |     |     }
 624 |     | }

  27 |     | /**
  28 |     |  * @title AMTTPNFT - ERC721 NFT Escrow Contract
  29 |     |  * @notice Handles NFT-to-NFT and NFT-to-ETH swaps with HTLC
  30 |     |  * @dev Part of the unified AMTTP protocol - works with AMTTPRouter
  31 |     |  * 
  32 |     |  * Features:
  33 |     |  * - ERC721 escrow with HTLC (Hash Time-Locked Contracts)
  34 |     |  * - NFT-to-NFT atomic swaps
  35 |     |  * - NFT-to-ETH trades
  36 |     |  * - Oracle signature verification for ML risk scores
  37 |     |  * - Policy Engine integration for risk-based actions
  38 |     |  * - Dispute resolution via Kleros
  39 |     |  * - Multi-approver workflow for high-risk transactions
  40 |     |  */
  41 |     | contract AMTTPNFT is 
  42 |     |     Initializable, 
  43 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable, 
  44 |     |     UUPSUpgradeable, 
  45 |     |     ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable,
  46 |     |     PausableUpgradeable,
  47 |     |     IERC721Receiver,
  48 |     |     IAMTTPNFT
  49 |     | {
  50 |     |     using ECDSA for bytes32;
  51 |     | 
  52 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  53 |     |     //                           CONSTANTS
  54 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  55 |     |     uint256 public constant RISK_SCALE = 1000;
  56 |     |     uint256 public constant HIGH_RISK_THRESHOLD = 700;
  57 |     |     uint256 public constant BLOCK_THRESHOLD = 900;
  58 |     |     
  59 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  62 |     |     enum SwapStatus { Pending, Approved, Completed, Refunded, Disputed, Blocked }
  63 |     |     enum SwapType { NFT_TO_ETH, NFT_TO_NFT }

  69 |     |     /**
  70 |     |      * @notice NFT Swap structure for NFT-to-ETH swaps
  71 |     |      */
  72 |     |     struct NFTSwap {
  73 |     |         address seller;          // NFT owner (selling NFT for ETH)
  74 |     |         address buyer;           // ETH sender (buying NFT)
  75 |     |         address nftContract;     // ERC721 contract address
  76 |     |         uint256 tokenId;         // NFT token ID
  77 |     |         uint256 ethAmount;       // ETH amount for the NFT
  78 |     |         bytes32 hashlock;        // HTLC hashlock
  79 |     |         uint256 timelock;        // Expiration timestamp
  80 |     |         uint256 riskScore;       // ML risk score (0-1000)
  81 |     |         bytes32 kycHash;         // KYC verification hash
  82 |     |         SwapStatus status;       // Current status
  83 |     |         uint8 approvalCount;     // Number of approvals
  84 |     |         SwapType swapType;       // Type of swap
  85 |     |     }
  86 |     |     
  87 |     |     /**
  88 |     |      * @notice NFT-to-NFT atomic swap structure
  89 |     |      */
  90 |     |     struct NFTtoNFTSwap {
  91 |     |         address partyA;          // First NFT owner
  92 |     |         address partyB;          // Second NFT owner
  93 |     |         address nftContractA;    // First NFT contract
  94 |     |         address nftContractB;    // Second NFT contract
  95 |     |         uint256 tokenIdA;        // First NFT token ID
  96 |     |         uint256 tokenIdB;        // Second NFT token ID
  97 |     |         bytes32 hashlock;        // HTLC hashlock
  98 |     |         uint256 timelock;        // Expiration timestamp
  99 |     |         uint256 riskScore;       // ML risk score
 100 |     |         SwapStatus status;       // Current status
 101 |     |         bool partyADeposited;    // Party A deposited NFT
 102 |     |         bool partyBDeposited;    // Party B deposited NFT
 103 |     |     }

 163 |     |         _;
 164 |     |     }

 167 |     |         require(nftSwaps[swapId].seller != address(0), "Swap not found");
 168 |     |         _;

 180 |     |     function initialize(address _oracle) public initializer {
 181 |     |         __Ownable_init();
 182 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
 183 |     |         __ReentrancyGuard_init();
 184 |     |         __Pausable_init();
 185 |     |         
 186 |     |         oracle = _oracle;
 187 |     |         approvalThreshold = 1;
 188 |     |         globalRiskThreshold = HIGH_RISK_THRESHOLD;
 189 |     |         activeModelVersion = "DQN-v1.0";
 190 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = false;
 191 |     |         
 192 |     |         // Add deployer as first approver
 193 |     |         approvers.push(msg.sender);
 194 |     |         isApprover[msg.sender] = true;
 195 |     |     }

 197 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

 201 |     |     function setRouter(address _router) external onlyOwner {
 202 |     |         router = _router;

 204 |     |     }

 206 |     |     function setPolicyEngine(address _policyEngine) external onlyOwner {
 207 |     |         policyEngine = IAMTTPPolicyEngine(_policyEngine);
 208 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = _policyEngine != address(0);
 209 |     |         emit PolicyEngineUpdated(_policyEngine);
 210 |     |     }

 213 |     |         disputeResolver = IAMTTPDisputeResolverLocal(_disputeResolver);
 214 |     |     }

 216 |     |     function setOracle(address _oracle) external onlyOwner {
 217 |     |         oracle = _oracle;
 218 |     |     }
 219 |     |     
 220 |     |     function addApprover(address _approver) external onlyOwner {
 221 |     |         require(!isApprover[_approver], "Already approver");
 222 |     |         approvers.push(_approver);
 223 |     |         isApprover[_approver] = true;
 224 |     |     }
 225 |     |     
 226 |     |     function removeApprover(address _approver) external onlyOwner {
 227 |     |         require(isApprover[_approver], "Not approver");
 228 |     |         isApprover[_approver] = false;
 229 |     |     }
 230 |     |     
 231 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 232 |     |     //                     NFT-TO-ETH SWAP
 233 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 234 |     |     
 235 |     |     /**
 236 |     |      * @notice Seller initiates NFT-to-ETH swap by depositing NFT
 237 |     |      * @param buyer Address of the ETH buyer
 238 |     |      * @param nftContract ERC721 contract address
 239 |     |      * @param tokenId Token ID of the NFT
 240 |     |      * @param ethAmount Expected ETH amount
 241 |     |      * @param hashlock HTLC hashlock
 242 |     |      * @param timelock Expiration timestamp
 243 |     |      * @param riskScore ML risk score from oracle
 244 |     |      * @param kycHash KYC verification hash
 245 |     |      * @param oracleSignature Oracle's signature
 246 |     |      */
 247 |     |     function initiateNFTtoETHSwap(
 248 |     |         address buyer,
 249 |     |         address nftContract,
 250 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 251 |     |         uint256 ethAmount,
 252 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 253 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 254 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 255 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 256 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 257 |     |     ) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 258 |     |         require(buyer != address(0), "Invalid buyer");
 259 |     |         require(nftContract != address(0), "Invalid NFT contract");
 260 |     |         require(ethAmount > 0, "Zero ETH amount");
 261 |     |         require(timelock > block.timestamp, "Invalid timelock");
 262 |     |         require(riskScore <= RISK_SCALE, "Invalid risk score");
 263 |     |         
 264 |     |         // Verify oracle signature
 265 |     |         require(_verifyNFTOracleSignature(msg.sender, buyer, nftContract, tokenId, ethAmount, riskScore, kycHash, oracleSignature), "Invalid signature");
 266 |     |         
 267 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, buyer, nftContract, tokenId, hashlock, timelock));
 268 |     |         require(nftSwaps[swapId].seller == address(0), "Swap exists");
 269 |     |         
 270 |     |         // Determine status based on risk
 271 |     |         SwapStatus initialStatus = _determineStatus(msg.sender, buyer, ethAmount, riskScore, kycHash);
 272 |     |         
 273 |     |         nftSwaps[swapId] = NFTSwap({
 274 |     |             seller: msg.sender,
 275 |     |             buyer: buyer,
 276 |     |             nftContract: nftContract,
 277 |     |             tokenId: tokenId,
 278 |     |             ethAmount: ethAmount,
 279 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
 280 |     |             timelock: timelock,
 281 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 282 |     |             kycHash: kycHash,
 283 |     |             status: initialStatus,
 284 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
 285 |     |             swapType: SwapType.NFT_TO_ETH
 286 |     |         });
 287 |     |         
 288 |     |         // Transfer NFT to escrow
 289 |     |         IERC721(nftContract).transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), tokenId);
 290 |     |         
 291 |     |         emit NFTSwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, buyer, nftContract, tokenId, ethAmount);
 292 |     |         emit NFTDeposited(swapId, msg.sender, nftContract, tokenId);
 293 |     |         
 294 |     |         if (initialStatus == SwapStatus.Blocked) {
 295 |     |             emit SwapBlocked(swapId, riskScore, "High risk transaction");
 296 |     |         }
 297 |     |         
 298 |     |         return swapId;
 299 |     |     }
 300 |     |     
 301 |     |     /**
 302 |     |      * @notice Buyer deposits ETH to complete the swap
 303 |     |      */
 304 |     |     function depositETHForNFT(bytes32 swapId) external payable nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
 305 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 306 |     |         require(msg.sender == swap.buyer, "Not buyer");
 307 |     |         require(msg.value == swap.ethAmount, "Wrong ETH amount");
 308 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Approved, "Not approved");
 309 |     |         
 310 |     |         // ETH is now in escrow, waiting for preimage reveal
 311 |     |     }
 312 |     |     
 313 |     |     /**
 314 |     |      * @notice Complete NFT-to-ETH swap with preimage
 315 |     |      */
 316 |     |     function completeNFTSwap(bytes32 swapId, bytes32 preimage) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
 317 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 318 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Approved, "Not approved");
 319 |     |         require(block.timestamp < swap.timelock, "Expired");
 320 |     |         require(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(preimage)) == swap.hashlock, "Invalid preimage");
 321 |     |         
 322 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 323 |     |         
 324 |     |         // Transfer NFT to buyer
 325 |     |         IERC721(swap.nftContract).transferFrom(address(this), swap.buyer, swap.tokenId);
 326 |     |         
 327 |     |         // Transfer ETH to seller
 328 |     |         (bool sent, ) = swap.seller.call{value: swap.ethAmount}("");
 329 |     |         require(sent, "ETH transfer failed");
 330 |     |         
 331 |     |         emit SwapCompleted(swapId);
 332 |     |     }
 333 |     |     
 334 |     |     /**
 335 |     |      * @notice Refund expired NFT swap
 336 |     |      */
 337 |     |     function refundNFTSwap(bytes32 swapId) external nonReentrant swapExists(swapId) {
 338 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 339 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Pending || swap.status == SwapStatus.Approved, "Cannot refund");
 340 |     |         require(block.timestamp >= swap.timelock, "Not expired");
 341 |     |         
 342 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 343 |     |         
 344 |     |         // Return NFT to seller
 345 |     |         IERC721(swap.nftContract).transferFrom(address(this), swap.seller, swap.tokenId);
 346 |     |         
 347 |     |         // Return ETH to buyer if deposited
 348 |     |         if (address(this).balance >= swap.ethAmount) {
 349 |     |             (bool sent, ) = swap.buyer.call{value: swap.ethAmount}("");
 350 |     |             require(sent, "ETH refund failed");
 351 |     |         }
 352 |     |         
 353 |     |         emit SwapRefunded(swapId);
 354 |     |     }
 355 |     |     
 356 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 357 |     |     //                     NFT-TO-NFT ATOMIC SWAP
 358 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 359 |     |     
 360 |     |     /**
 361 |     |      * @notice Initiate NFT-to-NFT atomic swap
 362 |     |      */
 363 |     |     function initiateNFTtoNFTSwap(
 364 |     |         address partyB,
 365 |     |         address nftContractA,
 366 |     |         uint256 tokenIdA,
 367 |     |         address nftContractB,
 368 |     |         uint256 tokenIdB,
 369 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 370 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 371 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 372 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 373 |     |     ) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 374 |     |         require(partyB != address(0), "Invalid party B");
 375 |     |         require(timelock > block.timestamp, "Invalid timelock");
 376 |     |         
 377 |     |         swapId = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, partyB, nftContractA, nftContractB, tokenIdA, tokenIdB, hashlock));
 378 |     |         
 379 |     |         nftToNftSwaps[swapId] = NFTtoNFTSwap({
 380 |     |             partyA: msg.sender,
 381 |     |             partyB: partyB,
 382 |     |             nftContractA: nftContractA,
 383 |     |             nftContractB: nftContractB,
 384 |     |             tokenIdA: tokenIdA,
 385 |     |             tokenIdB: tokenIdB,
 386 |     |             hashlock: hashlock,
 387 |     |             timelock: timelock,
 388 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 389 |     |             status: SwapStatus.Pending,
 390 |     |             partyADeposited: false,
 391 |     |             partyBDeposited: false
 392 |     |         });
 393 |     |         
 394 |     |         emit NFTtoNFTSwapInitiated(swapId, msg.sender, partyB);
 395 |     |         
 396 |     |         return swapId;
 397 |     |     }
 398 |     |     
 399 |     |     /**
 400 |     |      * @notice Deposit NFT for NFT-to-NFT swap
 401 |     |      * @dev Uses CEI pattern: state changes before external NFT transfer calls
 402 |     |      */
 403 |     |     function depositNFTForSwap(bytes32 swapId) external nonReentrant {
 404 |     |         NFTtoNFTSwap storage swap = nftToNftSwaps[swapId];
 405 |     |         require(swap.partyA != address(0), "Swap not found");
 406 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Pending, "Wrong status");
 407 |     |         
 408 |     |         if (msg.sender == swap.partyA) {
 409 |     |             require(!swap.partyADeposited, "Already deposited");
 410 |     |             // CEI: Update state BEFORE external call
 411 |     |             swap.partyADeposited = true;
 412 |     |             IERC721(swap.nftContractA).transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), swap.tokenIdA);
 413 |     |             emit NFTDeposited(swapId, msg.sender, swap.nftContractA, swap.tokenIdA);
 414 |     |         } else if (msg.sender == swap.partyB) {
 415 |     |             require(!swap.partyBDeposited, "Already deposited");
 416 |     |             // CEI: Update state BEFORE external call
 417 |     |             swap.partyBDeposited = true;
 418 |     |             IERC721(swap.nftContractB).transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), swap.tokenIdB);
 419 |     |             emit NFTDeposited(swapId, msg.sender, swap.nftContractB, swap.tokenIdB);
 420 |     |         } else {
 421 |     |             revert("Not party to swap");
 422 |     |         }
 423 |     |         
 424 |     |         // Auto-approve when both NFTs deposited
 425 |     |         if (swap.partyADeposited && swap.partyBDeposited) {
 426 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Approved;
 427 |     |         }
 428 |     |     }
 429 |     |     
 430 |     |     /**
 431 |     |      * @notice Complete NFT-to-NFT swap
 432 |     |      */
 433 |     |     function completeNFTtoNFTSwap(bytes32 swapId, bytes32 preimage) external nonReentrant {
 434 |     |         NFTtoNFTSwap storage swap = nftToNftSwaps[swapId];
 435 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Approved, "Not approved");
 436 |     |         require(block.timestamp < swap.timelock, "Expired");
 437 |     |         require(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(preimage)) == swap.hashlock, "Invalid preimage");
 438 |     |         
 439 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 440 |     |         
 441 |     |         // Cross-transfer NFTs
 442 |     |         IERC721(swap.nftContractA).transferFrom(address(this), swap.partyB, swap.tokenIdA);
 443 |     |         IERC721(swap.nftContractB).transferFrom(address(this), swap.partyA, swap.tokenIdB);
 444 |     |         
 445 |     |         emit SwapCompleted(swapId);
 446 |     |     }
 447 |     |     
 448 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 449 |     |     //                     APPROVAL WORKFLOW
 450 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 451 |     |     
 452 |     |     function approveSwap(bytes32 swapId) external onlyApprover swapExists(swapId) {
 453 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 454 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Pending, "Not pending");
 455 |     |         require(!approvals[swapId][msg.sender], "Already approved");
 456 |     |         
 457 |     |         approvals[swapId][msg.sender] = true;
 458 |     |         swap.approvalCount++;
 459 |     |         
 460 |     |         emit SwapApproved(swapId, msg.sender);
 461 |     |         
 462 |     |         if (swap.approvalCount >= approvalThreshold) {
 463 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Approved;
 464 |     |         }
 465 |     |     }
 466 |     |     
 467 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 468 |     |     //                     DISPUTE RESOLUTION
 469 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 470 |     |     
 471 |     |     function raiseDispute(bytes32 swapId, string calldata evidence) external payable swapExists(swapId) {
 472 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 473 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Pending || swap.status == SwapStatus.Approved, "Cannot dispute");
 474 |     |         require(msg.sender == swap.buyer || msg.sender == swap.seller, "Not party to swap");
 475 |     |         require(address(disputeResolver) != address(0), "Dispute resolver not set");
 476 |     |         
 477 |     |         swap.status = SwapStatus.Disputed;
 478 |     |         
 479 |     |         uint256 disputeId = disputeResolver.createDispute{value: msg.value}(
 480 |     |             swapId,
 481 |     |             msg.sender,
 482 |     |             swap.ethAmount,
 483 |     |             evidence
 484 |     |         );
 485 |     |         
 486 |     |         emit SwapDisputed(swapId, disputeId);
 487 |     |     }
 488 |     |     
 489 |     |     function executeDisputeRuling(bytes32 swapId, bool releaseTobuyer) external {
 490 |     |         require(msg.sender == address(disputeResolver), "Only dispute resolver");
 491 |     |         
 492 |     |         NFTSwap storage swap = nftSwaps[swapId];
 493 |     |         require(swap.status == SwapStatus.Disputed, "Not disputed");
 494 |     |         
 495 |     |         if (releaseTobuyer) {
 496 |     |             // Buyer wins: gets NFT
 497 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Completed;
 498 |     |             IERC721(swap.nftContract).transferFrom(address(this), swap.buyer, swap.tokenId);
 499 |     |             emit SwapCompleted(swapId);
 500 |     |         } else {
 501 |     |             // Seller wins: gets NFT back
 502 |     |             swap.status = SwapStatus.Refunded;
 503 |     |             IERC721(swap.nftContract).transferFrom(address(this), swap.seller, swap.tokenId);
 504 |     |             emit SwapRefunded(swapId);
 505 |     |         }
 506 |     |     }
 507 |     |     
 508 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 509 |     |     //                     RESCUE FUNCTIONS
 510 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 511 |     |     
 512 |     |     /**
 513 |     |      * @notice Rescue stuck NFT (admin only)
 514 |     |      */
 515 |     |     function rescueNFT(address nftContract, address to, uint256 tokenId) external onlyOwner {
 516 |     |         IERC721(nftContract).transferFrom(address(this), to, tokenId);
 517 |     |     }
 518 |     |     
 519 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 520 |     |     //                     INTERNAL FUNCTIONS
 521 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 522 |     |     
 523 |     |     function _verifyNFTOracleSignature(
 524 |     |         address seller,
 525 |     |         address buyer,
 526 |     |         address nftContract,
 527 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 528 |     |         uint256 ethAmount,
 529 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 530 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 531 |     |         bytes calldata signature
 532 |     |     ) internal view returns (bool) {
 533 |     |         if (oracle == address(0)) return true;
 534 |     |         
 535 |     |         bytes32 messageHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(seller, buyer, nftContract, tokenId, ethAmount, riskScore, kycHash));
 536 |     |         bytes32 ethSignedHash = messageHash.toEthSignedMessageHash();
 537 |     |         address signer = ethSignedHash.recover(signature);
 538 |     |         
 539 |     |         return signer == oracle;
 540 |     |     }
 541 |     |     
 542 |     |     function _determineStatus(
 543 |     |         address seller,
 544 |     |         address buyer,
 545 |     |         uint256 amount,
 546 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 547 |     |         bytes32 kycHash
 548 |     |     ) internal returns (SwapStatus) {
 549 |     |         if (policyEngineEnabled && address(policyEngine) != address(0)) {
 550 |     |             (IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction action, ) = policyEngine.validateTransaction(
 551 |     |                 seller, buyer, amount, riskScore, activeModelVersion, kycHash
 552 |     |             );
 553 |     |             
 554 |     |             if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Block) {
 555 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 556 |     |             } else if (action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Escrow || 
 557 |     |                        action == IAMTTPPolicyEngine.PolicyAction.Review) {
 558 |     |                 return SwapStatus.Pending;
 559 |     |             }
 560 |     |             return SwapStatus.Approved;
 561 |     |         }
 562 |     |         
 563 |     |         if (riskScore >= BLOCK_THRESHOLD) {
 564 |     |             return SwapStatus.Blocked;
 565 |     |         } else if (riskScore >= globalRiskThreshold) {
 566 |     |             return SwapStatus.Pending;
 567 |     |         }
 568 |     |         
 569 |     |         return SwapStatus.Approved;
 570 |     |     }
 571 |     |

 575 |     |

 577 |     |         return nftSwaps[swapId];
 578 |     |     }

 580 |     |     function getNFTtoNFTSwap(bytes32 swapId) external view returns (NFTtoNFTSwap memory) {
 581 |     |         return nftToNftSwaps[swapId];
 582 |     |     }
 583 |     |

 586 |     |     }

   9 |     | /**
  10 |     |  * @title AMTTPPolicyEngine - Advanced policy management for AMTTP
  11 |     |  * @dev Manages user-defined transaction policies, risk thresholds, and approval workflows
  12 |     |  * @notice This contract has a richer implementation than the minimal IAMTTPPolicyEngine interface
  13 |     |  * used by AMTTPRouter. The Router uses a minimal interface for gas efficiency.
  14 |     |  */
  15 |     | contract AMTTPPolicyEngine is 
  16 |     |     Initializable, 
  17 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable, 
  18 |     |     UUPSUpgradeable, 
  19 |     |     ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable
  20 |     | {
  21 |     |     
  22 |     |     // ---------------- Enums ----------------
  23 |     |     enum RiskLevel { Minimal, Low, Medium, High }
  24 |     |     enum PolicyAction { Approve, Review, Escrow, Block }
  25 |     |     enum VelocityWindow { Hour, Day, Week, Month }
  26 |     |     
  27 |     |     // ---------------- Structs ----------------
  28 |     |     struct TransactionPolicy {
  29 |     |         uint256 maxAmount;                    // Maximum transaction amount
  30 |     |         uint256 dailyLimit;                   // 24-hour transaction limit
  31 |     |         uint256 weeklyLimit;                  // 7-day transaction limit
  32 |     |         uint256 monthlyLimit;                 // 30-day transaction limit
  33 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold;                // Risk threshold (0-1000, representing 0-1.0)
  34 |     |         address[] allowedCounterparties;      // Whitelist of addresses
  35 |     |         address[] blockedCounterparties;      // Blacklist of addresses
  36 |     |         bool autoApprove;                     // Auto-approve low risk transactions
  37 |     |         bool enabled;                         // Policy is active
  38 |     |         uint256 cooldownPeriod;               // Time between large transactions
  39 |     |         uint256 lastLargeTransaction;         // Timestamp of last large transaction
  40 |     |     }
  41 |     |     
  42 |     |     struct RiskPolicy {
  43 |     |         uint256 minimalThreshold;             // 0-250 (0-0.25)
  44 |     |         uint256 lowThreshold;                 // 250-400 (0.25-0.40)  
  45 |     |         uint256 mediumThreshold;              // 400-700 (0.40-0.70)
  46 |     |         uint256 highThreshold;                // 700-1000 (0.70-1.0)
  47 |     |         PolicyAction minimalAction;
  48 |     |         PolicyAction lowAction;
  49 |     |         PolicyAction mediumAction;
  50 |     |         PolicyAction highAction;
  51 |     |         bool adaptiveThresholds;              // Adjust thresholds based on user behavior
  52 |     |     }
  53 |     |     
  54 |     |     struct VelocityLimit {
  55 |     |         uint256 maxTransactions;              // Max transactions in window
  56 |     |         uint256 maxVolume;                    // Max volume in window
  57 |     |         VelocityWindow window;                // Time window
  58 |     |         bool enabled;
  59 |     |     }
  60 |     |     
  61 |     |     struct ComplianceRule {
  62 |     |         bool requireKYC;                      // KYC required for transactions
  63 |     |         bool requireApproval;                 // Manual approval required
  64 |     |         uint256 approvalThreshold;            // Amount requiring approval
  65 |     |         address[] approvers;                  // List of approved addresses
  66 |     |         uint256 approvalTimeout;              // Timeout for approvals
  67 |     |         bool geofencing;                      // Geographic restrictions
  68 |     |         string[] allowedCountries;            // Allowed country codes
  69 |     |     }
  70 |     |     
  71 |     |     struct UserActivity {
  72 |     |         uint256 dailyVolume;                  // Today's transaction volume
  73 |     |         uint256 weeklyVolume;                 // This week's volume
  74 |     |         uint256 monthlyVolume;                // This month's volume
  75 |     |         uint256 dailyCount;                   // Today's transaction count
  76 |     |         uint256 lastTransactionTime;         // Last transaction timestamp
  77 |     |         uint256 lastResetTime;                // Last reset timestamp
  78 |     |         uint256 suspiciousScore;              // Cumulative suspicious activity score
  79 |     |         bool frozen;                          // Account frozen status
  80 |     |     }
  81 |     |     
  82 |     |     // ---------------- State Variables ----------------
  83 |     |     mapping(address => TransactionPolicy) public userPolicies;
  84 |     |     mapping(address => RiskPolicy) public userRiskPolicies;
  85 |     |     mapping(address => VelocityLimit[]) public userVelocityLimits;
  86 |     |     mapping(address => ComplianceRule) public userComplianceRules;
  87 |     |     mapping(address => UserActivity) public userActivity;
  88 |     |     mapping(address => mapping(address => bool)) public trustedCounterparties;
  89 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public globalApprovers;
  90 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public trustedUsers;  // Global trusted users list
  91 |     |     
  92 |     |     // Global settings
  93 |     |     uint256 public globalRiskThreshold;
  94 |     |     uint256 public globalMaxAmount;
  95 |     |     bool public emergencyPause;
  96 |     |     address public amttpContract;
  97 |     |     address public oracleService;
  98 |     |     
  99 |     |     // Kleros Dispute Resolution
 100 |     |     address public disputeResolver;  // AMTTPDisputeResolver contract
 101 |     |     uint256 public escrowThreshold;  // Risk score threshold for escrow (default: 700)
 102 |     |     
 103 |     |     // DQN Model integration
 104 |     |     mapping(string => uint256) public modelVersionScores; // Model version -> F1 score
 105 |     |     string public activeModelVersion;
 106 |     |     uint256 public minimumModelScore;                     // Minimum F1 score (e.g., 669 for 0.669)
 107 |     |     
 108 |     |     // ---------------- Events ----------------
 109 |     |     event PolicyUpdated(address indexed user, string policyType);
 110 |     |     event TransactionValidated(address indexed user, address indexed counterparty, uint256 amount, PolicyAction action);
 111 |     |     event RiskThresholdExceeded(address indexed user, uint256 riskScore, uint256 threshold);
 112 |     |     event VelocityLimitExceeded(address indexed user, VelocityWindow window, uint256 amount);
 113 |     |     event AccountFrozen(address indexed user, string reason);
 114 |     |     event AccountUnfrozen(address indexed user);

 120 |     |     event MediumRiskFlagged(address indexed user, address indexed counterparty, uint256 amount, uint256 riskScore);
 121 |     |     event LowRiskAutoApproved(address indexed user, address indexed counterparty, uint256 amount, uint256 riskScore);
 122 |     |

 126 |     |         _;

 131 |     |         _;

 136 |     |         _;

 144 |     |     // ---------------- Initialization ----------------
 145 |     |     function initialize(address _amttpContract, address _oracleService) public initializer {
 146 |     |         __Ownable_init();
 147 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
 148 |     |         __ReentrancyGuard_init();
 149 |     |         
 150 |     |         amttpContract = _amttpContract;
 151 |     |         oracleService = _oracleService;
 152 |     |         globalRiskThreshold = 700; // 0.70 default
 153 |     |         globalMaxAmount = 100 ether; // 100 ETH default
 154 |     |         activeModelVersion = "DQN-v1.0-real-fraud";
 155 |     |         minimumModelScore = 669; // 0.669 F1 score minimum
 156 |     |         
 157 |     |         // Set default model score
 158 |     |         modelVersionScores["DQN-v1.0-real-fraud"] = 669;
 159 |     |     }
