 161 |     |     // ---------------- Policy Management ----------------
 162 |     |     
 163 |     |     /**
 164 |     |      * @dev Set transaction policy for a user
 165 |     |      */
 166 |     |     function setTransactionPolicy(
 167 |     |         address user,
 168 |     |         uint256 maxAmount,
 169 |     |         uint256 dailyLimit,
 170 |     |         uint256 weeklyLimit,
 171 |     |         uint256 monthlyLimit,
 172 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold,
 173 |     |         bool autoApprove,
 174 |     |         uint256 cooldownPeriod
 175 |     |     ) external {
 176 |     |         require(msg.sender == user || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 177 |     |         require(maxAmount <= globalMaxAmount, "Exceeds global limit");
 178 |     |         require(riskThreshold <= 1000, "Invalid risk threshold");
 179 |     |         
 180 |     |         userPolicies[user] = TransactionPolicy({
 181 |     |             maxAmount: maxAmount,
 182 |     |             dailyLimit: dailyLimit,
 183 |     |             weeklyLimit: weeklyLimit,
 184 |     |             monthlyLimit: monthlyLimit,
 185 |     |             riskThreshold: riskThreshold,
 186 |     |             allowedCounterparties: userPolicies[user].allowedCounterparties,
 187 |     |             blockedCounterparties: userPolicies[user].blockedCounterparties,
 188 |     |             autoApprove: autoApprove,
 189 |     |             enabled: true,
 190 |     |             cooldownPeriod: cooldownPeriod,
 191 |     |             lastLargeTransaction: userPolicies[user].lastLargeTransaction
 192 |     |         });
 193 |     |         
 194 |     |         emit PolicyUpdated(user, "transaction");
 195 |     |     }
 196 |     |     
 197 |     |     /**
 198 |     |      * @dev Set risk policy for a user  
 199 |     |      */
 200 |     |     function setRiskPolicy(
 201 |     |         address user,
 202 |     |         uint256[4] memory thresholds, // [minimal, low, medium, high]
 203 |     |         PolicyAction[4] memory actions,
 204 |     |         bool adaptiveThresholds
 205 |     |     ) external {
 206 |     |         require(msg.sender == user || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 207 |     |         require(thresholds[0] < thresholds[1] && thresholds[1] < thresholds[2] && thresholds[2] < thresholds[3], "Invalid thresholds");
 208 |     |         
 209 |     |         userRiskPolicies[user] = RiskPolicy({
 210 |     |             minimalThreshold: thresholds[0],
 211 |     |             lowThreshold: thresholds[1],
 212 |     |             mediumThreshold: thresholds[2],
 213 |     |             highThreshold: thresholds[3],
 214 |     |             minimalAction: actions[0],
 215 |     |             lowAction: actions[1],
 216 |     |             mediumAction: actions[2],
 217 |     |             highAction: actions[3],
 218 |     |             adaptiveThresholds: adaptiveThresholds
 219 |     |         });
 220 |     |         
 221 |     |         emit PolicyUpdated(user, "risk");
 222 |     |     }
 223 |     |     
 224 |     |     /**
 225 |     |      * @dev Add velocity limit for a user
 226 |     |      */
 227 |     |     function addVelocityLimit(
 228 |     |         address user,
 229 |     |         uint256 maxTransactions,
 230 |     |         uint256 maxVolume,
 231 |     |         VelocityWindow window
 232 |     |     ) external {
 233 |     |         require(msg.sender == user || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 234 |     |         
 235 |     |         userVelocityLimits[user].push(VelocityLimit({
 236 |     |             maxTransactions: maxTransactions,
 237 |     |             maxVolume: maxVolume,
 238 |     |             window: window,
 239 |     |             enabled: true
 240 |     |         }));
 241 |     |         
 242 |     |         emit PolicyUpdated(user, "velocity");
 243 |     |     }
 244 |     |     
 245 |     |     /**
 246 |     |      * @dev Set compliance rules for a user
 247 |     |      */
 248 |     |     function setComplianceRules(
 249 |     |         address user,
 250 |     |         bool requireKYC,
 251 |     |         bool requireApproval,
 252 |     |         uint256 approvalThreshold,
 253 |     |         uint256 approvalTimeout,
 254 |     |         bool geofencing
 255 |     |     ) external {
 256 |     |         require(msg.sender == user || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 257 |     |         
 258 |     |         userComplianceRules[user].requireKYC = requireKYC;
 259 |     |         userComplianceRules[user].requireApproval = requireApproval;
 260 |     |         userComplianceRules[user].approvalThreshold = approvalThreshold;
 261 |     |         userComplianceRules[user].approvalTimeout = approvalTimeout;
 262 |     |         userComplianceRules[user].geofencing = geofencing;
 263 |     |         
 264 |     |         emit PolicyUpdated(user, "compliance");
 265 |     |     }
 266 |     |     
 267 |     |     // ---------------- Transaction Validation ----------------
 268 |     |     
 269 |     |     /**
 270 |     |      * @dev Validate transaction against user policies and DQN risk score
 271 |     |      */
 272 |     |     function validateTransaction(
 273 |     |         address user,
 274 |     |         address counterparty,
 275 |     |         uint256 amount,
 276 |     |         uint256 dqnRiskScore,        // Risk score from your DQN model (0-1000)
 277 |     |         string memory modelVersion,
 278 |     |         bytes32 kycHash
 279 |     |     ) external onlyAMTTP notPaused notFrozen(user) returns (PolicyAction action, string memory reason) {
 280 |     |         
 281 |     |         // Update user activity
 282 |     |         _updateUserActivity(user, amount);
 283 |     |         
 284 |     |         // Check model version
 285 |     |         require(modelVersionScores[modelVersion] >= minimumModelScore, "Model version not approved");
 286 |     |         
 287 |     |         // Get user policies (with defaults if not set)
 288 |     |         TransactionPolicy memory policy = _getUserPolicyWithDefaults(user);
 289 |     |         RiskPolicy memory riskPolicy = _getUserRiskPolicyWithDefaults(user);
 290 |     |         ComplianceRule memory compliance = userComplianceRules[user];
 291 |     |         
 292 |     |         // 1. Basic policy checks
 293 |     |         if (amount > policy.maxAmount) {
 294 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Block, "Exceeds maximum amount");
 295 |     |         }
 296 |     |         
 297 |     |         // 2. Velocity checks
 298 |     |         if (!_checkVelocityLimits(user, amount)) {
 299 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Block, "Velocity limit exceeded");
 300 |     |         }
 301 |     |         
 302 |     |         // 3. Counterparty checks
 303 |     |         if (_isBlockedCounterparty(user, counterparty)) {
 304 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Block, "Blocked counterparty");
 305 |     |         }
 306 |     |         
 307 |     |         // 4. KYC compliance
 308 |     |         if (compliance.requireKYC && kycHash == bytes32(0)) {
 309 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Block, "KYC required");
 310 |     |         }
 311 |     |         
 312 |     |         // 5. DQN Risk Assessment - Your trained model integration
 313 |     |         PolicyAction riskAction = _assessDQNRisk(dqnRiskScore, riskPolicy);
 314 |     |         
 315 |     |         // 6. HIGH RISK: Auto-block and flag for Kleros dispute
 316 |     |         // Transactions with risk score >= 700 are auto-blocked
 317 |     |         if (riskAction == PolicyAction.Block) {
 318 |     |             emit HighRiskAutoBlocked(user, counterparty, amount, dqnRiskScore);
 319 |     |             emit RiskThresholdExceeded(user, dqnRiskScore, riskPolicy.mediumThreshold);
 320 |     |             // Caller should route to Kleros escrow via routeToKlerosEscrow()
 321 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Block, "HIGH_RISK: Auto-blocked - route to Kleros for dispute");
 322 |     |         }
 323 |     |         
 324 |     |         // 7. MEDIUM RISK: Flag for human review
 325 |     |         // Transactions with risk score 400-699 need manual verification
 326 |     |         if (riskAction == PolicyAction.Review) {
 327 |     |             emit MediumRiskFlagged(user, counterparty, amount, dqnRiskScore);
 328 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Review, "MEDIUM_RISK: Flagged for human review");
 329 |     |         }
 330 |     |         
 331 |     |         // 8. Special approval requirements
 332 |     |         if (compliance.requireApproval && amount >= compliance.approvalThreshold) {
 333 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Review, "Manual approval required");
 334 |     |         }
 335 |     |         
 336 |     |         // 9. Cooldown period for large transactions
 337 |     |         if (amount >= policy.maxAmount / 2 && 
 338 |     |             block.timestamp < policy.lastLargeTransaction + policy.cooldownPeriod) {
 339 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Review, "Cooldown period active");
 340 |     |         }
 341 |     |         
 342 |     |         // 10. Trusted counterparty override (only affects Review → Approve)
 343 |     |         if (trustedCounterparties[user][counterparty] && riskAction == PolicyAction.Review) {
 344 |     |             riskAction = PolicyAction.Approve;
 345 |     |         }
 346 |     |         
 347 |     |         // 11. LOW RISK: Auto-approval for low/minimal risk scores (< 400)
 348 |     |         if (riskAction == PolicyAction.Approve) {
 349 |     |             _recordSuccessfulTransaction(user, amount);
 350 |     |             emit LowRiskAutoApproved(user, counterparty, amount, dqnRiskScore);
 351 |     |             emit TransactionValidated(user, counterparty, amount, PolicyAction.Approve);
 352 |     |             return (PolicyAction.Approve, "LOW_RISK: Auto-approved");
 353 |     |         }
 354 |     |         
 355 |     |         emit TransactionValidated(user, counterparty, amount, riskAction);
 356 |     |         return (riskAction, _getRiskActionReason(dqnRiskScore, riskAction));
 357 |     |     }
 358 |     |     
 359 |     |     /**
 360 |     |      * @dev Assess risk using your DQN model scores
 361 |     |      */
 362 |     |     function _assessDQNRisk(uint256 riskScore, RiskPolicy memory policy) internal pure returns (PolicyAction) {
 363 |     |         if (riskScore <= policy.minimalThreshold) {
 364 |     |             return policy.minimalAction;
 365 |     |         } else if (riskScore <= policy.lowThreshold) {
 366 |     |             return policy.lowAction;
 367 |     |         } else if (riskScore <= policy.mediumThreshold) {
 368 |     |             return policy.mediumAction;
 369 |     |         } else {
 370 |     |             return policy.highAction;
 371 |     |         }
 372 |     |     }
 373 |     |     
 374 |     |     /**
 375 |     |      * @dev Update user activity tracking
 376 |     |      */
 377 |     |     function _updateUserActivity(address user, uint256 amount) internal {
 378 |     |         UserActivity storage activity = userActivity[user];
 379 |     |         
 380 |     |         // Reset counters if new day/week/month
 381 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= activity.lastResetTime + 1 days) {
 382 |     |             activity.dailyVolume = 0;
 383 |     |             activity.dailyCount = 0;
 384 |     |             activity.lastResetTime = block.timestamp;
 385 |     |         }
 386 |     |         
 387 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= activity.lastResetTime + 7 days) {
 388 |     |             activity.weeklyVolume = 0;
 389 |     |         }
 390 |     |         
 391 |     |         if (block.timestamp >= activity.lastResetTime + 30 days) {
 392 |     |             activity.monthlyVolume = 0;
 393 |     |         }
 394 |     |         
 395 |     |         // Update counters
 396 |     |         activity.dailyVolume += amount;
 397 |     |         activity.weeklyVolume += amount;
 398 |     |         activity.monthlyVolume += amount;
 399 |     |         activity.dailyCount += 1;
 400 |     |         activity.lastTransactionTime = block.timestamp;
 401 |     |     }
 402 |     |     
 403 |     |     /**
 404 |     |      * @dev Check velocity limits
 405 |     |      */
 406 |     |     function _checkVelocityLimits(address user, uint256 amount) internal view returns (bool) {
 407 |     |         VelocityLimit[] memory limits = userVelocityLimits[user];
 408 |     |         UserActivity memory activity = userActivity[user];
 409 |     |         
 410 |     |         for (uint i = 0; i < limits.length; i++) {
 411 |     |             if (!limits[i].enabled) continue;
 412 |     |             
 413 |     |             if (limits[i].window == VelocityWindow.Day) {
 414 |     |                 if (activity.dailyVolume + amount > limits[i].maxVolume ||
 415 |     |                     activity.dailyCount + 1 > limits[i].maxTransactions) {
 416 |     |                     return false;
 417 |     |                 }
 418 |     |             }
 419 |     |             // Add other window checks as needed
 420 |     |         }
 421 |     |         
 422 |     |         return true;
 423 |     |     }
 424 |     |     
 425 |     |     /**
 426 |     |      * @dev Get user policy with defaults
 427 |     |      */
 428 |     |     function _getUserPolicyWithDefaults(address user) internal view returns (TransactionPolicy memory) {
 429 |     |         TransactionPolicy memory policy = userPolicies[user];
 430 |     |         
 431 |     |         if (!policy.enabled) {
 432 |     |             // Return default policy
 433 |     |             policy = TransactionPolicy({
 434 |     |                 maxAmount: 10 ether,
 435 |     |                 dailyLimit: 50 ether,
 436 |     |                 weeklyLimit: 200 ether,
 437 |     |                 monthlyLimit: 500 ether,
 438 |     |                 riskThreshold: 700, // 0.70
 439 |     |                 allowedCounterparties: new address[](0),
 440 |     |                 blockedCounterparties: new address[](0),
 441 |     |                 autoApprove: true,
 442 |     |                 enabled: true,
 443 |     |                 cooldownPeriod: 1 hours,
 444 |     |                 lastLargeTransaction: 0
 445 |     |             });
 446 |     |         }
 447 |     |         
 448 |     |         return policy;
 449 |     |     }
 450 |     |     
 451 |     |     /**
 452 |     |      * @dev Get user risk policy with defaults
 453 |     |      * 
 454 |     |      * Default policy thresholds:
 455 |     |      * - LOW (0-399): Auto-approve - trusted transactions
 456 |     |      * - MEDIUM (400-699): Review - requires human verification  
 457 |     |      * - HIGH (700-1000): Block - auto-blocked, routed to Kleros escrow
 458 |     |      */
 459 |     |     function _getUserRiskPolicyWithDefaults(address user) internal view returns (RiskPolicy memory) {
 460 |     |         RiskPolicy memory policy = userRiskPolicies[user];
 461 |     |         
 462 |     |         if (policy.highThreshold == 0) {
 463 |     |             // Default risk policy: HIGH = Block, MEDIUM = Review, LOW = Approve
 464 |     |             policy = RiskPolicy({
 465 |     |                 minimalThreshold: 200,    // 0-200: Very low risk
 466 |     |                 lowThreshold: 400,        // 200-400: Low risk (auto-approve)
 467 |     |                 mediumThreshold: 700,     // 400-700: Medium risk (review)
 468 |     |                 highThreshold: 1000,      // 700-1000: High risk (auto-block)
 469 |     |                 minimalAction: PolicyAction.Approve,  // Very low → approve
 470 |     |                 lowAction: PolicyAction.Approve,      // Low → approve
 471 |     |                 mediumAction: PolicyAction.Review,    // Medium → human review
 472 |     |                 highAction: PolicyAction.Block,       // High → auto-block + Kleros
 473 |     |                 adaptiveThresholds: false
 474 |     |             });
 475 |     |         }
 476 |     |         
 477 |     |         return policy;
 478 |     |     }
 479 |     |     
 480 |     |     function _isBlockedCounterparty(address user, address counterparty) internal view returns (bool) {
 481 |     |         address[] memory blocked = userPolicies[user].blockedCounterparties;
 482 |     |         for (uint i = 0; i < blocked.length; i++) {
 483 |     |             if (blocked[i] == counterparty) return true;
 484 |     |         }
 485 |     |         return false;
 486 |     |     }
 487 |     |     
 488 |     |     function _recordSuccessfulTransaction(address user, uint256 amount) internal {
 489 |     |         if (amount >= userPolicies[user].maxAmount / 2) {
 490 |     |             userPolicies[user].lastLargeTransaction = block.timestamp;
 491 |     |         }
 492 |     |     }
 493 |     |     
 494 |     |     function _getRiskActionReason(uint256 riskScore, PolicyAction action) internal pure returns (string memory) {
 495 |     |         if (action == PolicyAction.Approve) return "Low risk - approved";
 496 |     |         if (action == PolicyAction.Review) return "Medium risk - review required";
 497 |     |         if (action == PolicyAction.Escrow) return "High risk - escrow required";
 498 |     |         return "Very high risk - blocked";
 499 |     |     }

 507 |     |         require(msg.sender == oracleService || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
 508 |     |         require(f1Score >= minimumModelScore, "Model performance too low");
 509 |     |         
 510 |     |         modelVersionScores[version] = f1Score;
 511 |     |         activeModelVersion = version;
 512 |     |         
 513 |     |         emit ModelVersionUpdated(version, f1Score);
 514 |     |     }

 521 |     |         minimumModelScore = score;
 522 |     |     }

 593 |     |      * @dev Get policy engine status for tests and UI
 594 |     |      */
 595 |     |     struct PolicyEngineStatus {
 596 |     |         address policyEngineAddress;
 597 |     |         bool enabled;
 598 |     |         uint256 globalThreshold;
 599 |     |         string defaultModel;
 600 |     |     }

 602 |     |     function getPolicyEngineStatus() external view returns (PolicyEngineStatus memory) {
 603 |     |         return PolicyEngineStatus({
 604 |     |             policyEngineAddress: address(this),
 605 |     |             enabled: !emergencyPause,
 606 |     |             globalThreshold: globalRiskThreshold,
 607 |     |             defaultModel: activeModelVersion
 608 |     |         });
 609 |     |     }
 610 |     |     
 611 |     |     function isTransactionAllowed(
 612 |     |         address user,
 613 |     |         address counterparty,
 614 |     |         uint256 amount,
 615 |     |         uint256 riskScore
 616 |     |     ) external view returns (bool allowed, string memory reason) {
 617 |     |         if (emergencyPause) return (false, "System paused");
 618 |     |         if (userActivity[user].frozen) return (false, "Account frozen");
 619 |     |         
 620 |     |         TransactionPolicy memory policy = _getUserPolicyWithDefaults(user);
 621 |     |         
 622 |     |         if (amount > policy.maxAmount) return (false, "Exceeds maximum amount");
 623 |     |         if (_isBlockedCounterparty(user, counterparty)) return (false, "Blocked counterparty");
 624 |     |         
 625 |     |         RiskPolicy memory riskPolicy = _getUserRiskPolicyWithDefaults(user);
 626 |     |         PolicyAction action = _assessDQNRisk(riskScore, riskPolicy);
 627 |     |         
 628 |     |         if (action == PolicyAction.Block) return (false, "Risk too high");
 629 |     |         
 630 |     |         return (true, "Transaction allowed");
 631 |     |     }

 633 |     |     // ============================================================
 634 |     |     // KLEROS DISPUTE RESOLUTION INTEGRATION
 635 |     |     // ============================================================
 636 |     |     
 637 |     |     /**
 638 |     |      * @dev Set the Kleros dispute resolver contract address
 639 |     |      * @param _disputeResolver Address of AMTTPDisputeResolver contract
 640 |     |      */
 641 |     |     function setDisputeResolver(address _disputeResolver) external onlyOwner {
 642 |     |         require(_disputeResolver != address(0), "Invalid address");
 643 |     |         disputeResolver = _disputeResolver;
 644 |     |     }
 645 |     |     
 646 |     |     /**
 647 |     |      * @dev Set the risk score threshold for escrow routing
 648 |     |      * @param _threshold Risk score (0-1000) above which transactions go to escrow
 649 |     |      */
 650 |     |     function setEscrowThreshold(uint256 _threshold) external onlyOwner {
 651 |     |         require(_threshold <= 1000, "Invalid threshold");
 652 |     |         escrowThreshold = _threshold;
 653 |     |     }
 654 |     |     
 655 |     |     /**
 656 |     |      * @dev Check if a transaction should be routed to Kleros escrow
 657 |     |      * @param riskScore The risk score from ML model (0-1000)
 658 |     |      * @return shouldEscrow Whether transaction should go to dispute resolver
 659 |     |      */
 660 |     |     function shouldRouteToKleros(uint256 riskScore) public view returns (bool shouldEscrow) {
 661 |     |         if (disputeResolver == address(0)) return false;
 662 |     |         if (escrowThreshold == 0) return false;
 663 |     |         return riskScore >= escrowThreshold;
 664 |     |     }
 665 |     |     
 666 |     |     /**
 667 |     |      * @dev Get dispute resolver address
 668 |     |      */
 669 |     |     function getDisputeResolver() external view returns (address) {
 670 |     |         return disputeResolver;
 671 |     |     }
 672 |     |     
 673 |     |     /**
 674 |     |      * @dev Route a high-risk transaction to Kleros escrow for potential dispute
 675 |     |      * @param txId Unique transaction identifier
 676 |     |      * @param recipient Intended recipient
 677 |     |      * @param riskScore Risk score from ML model
 678 |     |      * @param evidenceURI IPFS URI containing ML evidence
 679 |     |      * @return success Whether the escrow was created
 680 |     |      */
 681 |     |     function routeToKlerosEscrow(
 682 |     |         bytes32 txId,
 683 |     |         address recipient,
 684 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 685 |     |         string calldata evidenceURI
 686 |     |     ) external payable nonReentrant returns (bool success) {
 687 |     |         require(disputeResolver != address(0), "Dispute resolver not set");
 688 |     |         require(msg.value > 0, "No funds sent");
 689 |     |         require(riskScore >= escrowThreshold, "Risk below threshold");
 690 |     |         
 691 |     |         // Call the dispute resolver to escrow funds
 692 |     |         (bool sent, ) = disputeResolver.call{value: msg.value}(
 693 |     |             abi.encodeWithSignature(
 694 |     |                 "escrowTransaction(bytes32,address,uint256,string)",
 695 |     |                 txId,
 696 |     |                 recipient,
 697 |     |                 riskScore,
 698 |     |                 evidenceURI
 699 |     |             )
 700 |     |         );
 701 |     |         
 702 |     |         require(sent, "Escrow failed");
 703 |     |         
 704 |     |         emit TransactionEscrowedForDispute(txId, msg.sender, msg.value, riskScore);
 705 |     |         
 706 |     |         return true;
 707 |     |     }
 708 |     |     
 709 |     |     // ---------------- Upgrade Authorization ----------------
 710 |     |     function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImplementation) internal override onlyOwner {}
 711 |     |

 713 |     |     //              STORAGE GAP (Security Enhancement for Upgrades)
 714 |     |     // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 715 |     |     
 716 |     |     /**
 717 |     |      * @dev Reserved storage space for future upgrades.
 718 |     |      * This allows adding new state variables without shifting storage layout.
 719 |     |      */
 720 |     |     uint256[50] private __gap;
 721 |     | }

   9 |     | /**
  10 |     |  * @title IAMTTPPolicyLocal - Minimal interface for policy validation
  11 |     |  * @dev Lightweight interface to integrate with AMTTP without bloating the main contract
  12 |     |  */
  13 |     | interface IAMTTPPolicyLocal {
  14 |     |     function validateTransaction(
  15 |     |         address user,
  16 |     |         address counterparty,
  17 |     |         uint256 amount,
  18 |     |         uint256 riskScore
  19 |     |     ) external view returns (bool allowed, uint8 recommendedRiskLevel);
  20 |     | }
  21 |     | 
  22 |     | /**
  23 |     |  * @title AMTTPPolicyManager - Lightweight policy integration
  24 |     |  * @dev Manages policy validation without adding bulk to main contract
  25 |     |  */
  26 |     | contract AMTTPPolicyManager is 
  27 |     |     Initializable, 
  28 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable, 
  29 |     |     UUPSUpgradeable,
  30 |     |     IAMTTPPolicy 
  31 |     | {
  32 |     |     
  33 |     |     // Policy engine address
  34 |     |     IAMTTPPolicyLocal public policyEngine;
  35 |     |     
  36 |     |     // Simple user policies
  37 |     |     mapping(address => uint256) public userMaxAmounts;
  38 |     |     mapping(address => uint256) public userRiskThresholds;
  39 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public trustedUsers;
  40 |     |     
  41 |     |     // Global settings
  42 |     |     uint256 public globalRiskThreshold;
  43 |     |     bool public policyEngineEnabled;
  44 |     |     
  45 |     |     // Events
  46 |     |     event PolicyEngineUpdated(address indexed newEngine);
  47 |     |     event UserPolicyUpdated(address indexed user, uint256 maxAmount, uint256 riskThreshold);
  48 |     |     event TrustedUserUpdated(address indexed user, bool trusted);
  49 |     |     
  50 |     |     /// @custom:oz-upgrades-unsafe-allow constructor
  51 |     |     constructor() {
  52 |     |         _disableInitializers();
  53 |     |     }
  54 |     |     
  55 |     |     function initialize() public initializer {
  56 |     |         __Ownable_init();
  57 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
  58 |     |         
  59 |     |         globalRiskThreshold = 700; // 0.70 default
  60 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = false;
  61 |     |     }
  62 |     |     
  63 |     |     /**
  64 |     |      * @dev Set policy engine
  65 |     |      */
  66 |     |     function setPolicyEngine(address _policyEngine) external onlyOwner {
  67 |     |         policyEngine = IAMTTPPolicyLocal(_policyEngine);
  68 |     |         policyEngineEnabled = _policyEngine != address(0);
  69 |     |         emit PolicyEngineUpdated(_policyEngine);
  70 |     |     }
  71 |     |     
  72 |     |     /**
  73 |     |      * @dev Set user policy (simplified)
  74 |     |      */
  75 |     |     function setUserPolicy(
  76 |     |         address user,
  77 |     |         uint256 maxAmount,
  78 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold
  79 |     |     ) external override {
  80 |     |         require(msg.sender == user || msg.sender == owner(), "Unauthorized");
  81 |     |         require(riskThreshold <= 1000, "Invalid threshold");
  82 |     |         
  83 |     |         userMaxAmounts[user] = maxAmount;
  84 |     |         userRiskThresholds[user] = riskThreshold;
  85 |     |         
  86 |     |         emit UserPolicyUpdated(user, maxAmount, riskThreshold);
  87 |     |     }
  88 |     |     
  89 |     |     /**
  90 |     |      * @dev Set trusted user status
  91 |     |      */
  92 |     |     function setTrustedUser(address user, bool trusted) external onlyOwner {
  93 |     |         trustedUsers[user] = trusted;
  94 |     |         emit TrustedUserUpdated(user, trusted);
  95 |     |     }
  96 |     |     
  97 |     |     /**
  98 |     |      * @dev Validate transaction (main integration point)
  99 |     |      */
 100 |     |     function validateTransaction(
 101 |     |         address user,
 102 |     |         address counterparty,
 103 |     |         uint256 amount,
 104 |     |         uint256 riskScore
 105 |     |     ) external view override returns (bool allowed, uint8 recommendedRiskLevel, string memory reason) {
 106 |     |         
 107 |     |         // Check user limits
 108 |     |         uint256 userMaxAmount = userMaxAmounts[user];
 109 |     |         if (userMaxAmount > 0 && amount > userMaxAmount) {
 110 |     |             return (false, 3, "Exceeds user limit");
 111 |     |         }
 112 |     |         
 113 |     |         // Check risk threshold
 114 |     |         uint256 userThreshold = userRiskThresholds[user];
 115 |     |         if (userThreshold == 0) userThreshold = globalRiskThreshold;
 116 |     |         
 117 |     |         // Determine risk level and actions
 118 |     |         if (riskScore >= 800) {
 119 |     |             return (false, 3, "Risk too high");
 120 |     |         } else if (riskScore >= userThreshold) {
 121 |     |             if (trustedUsers[user]) {
 122 |     |                 return (true, 2, "High risk - trusted user");
 123 |     |             } else {
 124 |     |                 return (true, 3, "High risk - escrow required");
 125 |     |             }
 126 |     |         } else if (riskScore >= 400) {
 127 |     |             return (true, 2, "Medium risk - review recommended");
 128 |     |         } else {
 129 |     |             return (true, 1, "Low risk - approved");
 130 |     |         }
 131 |     |     }
 132 |     |     
 133 |     |     /**
 134 |     |      * @dev Check if transaction would be allowed
 135 |     |      */
 136 |     |     function isTransactionAllowed(
 137 |     |         address user,
 138 |     |         address counterparty,
 139 |     |         uint256 amount,
 140 |     |         uint256 riskScore
 141 |     |     ) external view override returns (bool) {
 142 |     |         (bool allowed,,) = this.validateTransaction(user, counterparty, amount, riskScore);
 143 |     |         return allowed;
 144 |     |     }
 145 |     |     
 146 |     |     /**
 147 |     |      * @dev Get user policy
 148 |     |      */
 149 |     |     function getUserPolicy(address user) external view override returns (
 150 |     |         uint256 maxAmount,
 151 |     |         uint256 riskThreshold,
 152 |     |         bool trusted
 153 |     |     ) {
 154 |     |         maxAmount = userMaxAmounts[user];
 155 |     |         riskThreshold = userRiskThresholds[user];
 156 |     |         if (riskThreshold == 0) riskThreshold = globalRiskThreshold;
 157 |     |         trusted = trustedUsers[user];
 158 |     |     }
 159 |     |     
 160 |     |     function _authorizeUpgrade(address newImplementation) internal override onlyOwner {}
 161 |     | }

 101 |     | /**
 102 |     |  * @title AMTTPRouter - Unified Entry Point for AMTTP Protocol
 103 |     |  * @notice Single interface for all AMTTP operations across ETH, ERC20, and NFT swaps
 104 |     |  * @dev Routes calls to appropriate sub-contracts based on asset type
 105 |     |  * 
 106 |     |  * Architecture:
 107 |     |  * ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 108 |     |  * │                        AMTTPRouter                               │
 109 |     |  * │  • Unified API for all swap types                                │
 110 |     |  * │  • Analytics aggregation                                         │
 111 |     |  * │  • Cross-contract coordination                                   │
 112 |     |  * └────────────┬─────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
 113 |     |  *              │                     │
 114 |     |  *    ┌─────────▼─────────┐ ┌─────────▼─────────┐
 115 |     |  *    │    AMTTPCore      │ │     AMTTPNFT      │
 116 |     |  *    │   ETH / ERC20     │ │   ERC721 / NFTs   │
 117 |     |  *    └─────────┬─────────┘ └─────────┬─────────┘
 118 |     |  *              │                     │
 119 |     |  *    ┌─────────▼─────────────────────▼─────────┐
 120 |     |  *    │           PolicyEngine                   │
 121 |     |  *    │   Risk Assessment / User Policies        │
 122 |     |  *    └─────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
 123 |     |  *                      │
 124 |     |  *    ┌─────────────────▼───────────────────────┐
 125 |     |  *    │         DisputeResolver (Kleros)         │
 126 |     |  *    └─────────────────────────────────────────┘
 127 |     |  */
 128 |     | contract AMTTPRouter is 
 129 |     |     Initializable, 
 130 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable, 
 131 |     |     UUPSUpgradeable,
 132 |     |     ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable

 213 |     |     }

 216 |     |     //                     ETH SWAP (via Core)

 219 |     |     /**
 220 |     |      * @notice Initiate ETH swap through unified router
 221 |     |      */
 222 |     |     function swapETH(
 223 |     |         address seller,
 224 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 225 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 226 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 227 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 228 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 229 |     |     ) external payable nonReentrant returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 230 |     |         require(address(coreContract) != address(0), "Core not set");
 231 |     |         
 232 |     |         swapId = coreContract.initiateSwap{value: msg.value}(
 233 |     |             seller,
 234 |     |             hashlock,
 235 |     |             timelock,
 236 |     |             riskScore,
 237 |     |             kycHash,
 238 |     |             oracleSignature
 239 |     |         );
 240 |     |         
 241 |     |         _recordSwap(swapId, SwapType.ETH, msg.sender, msg.value);
 242 |     |         totalVolumeETH += msg.value;
 243 |     |         
 244 |     |         return swapId;
 245 |     |     }

 247 |     |     /**
 248 |     |      * @notice Initiate ERC20 swap through unified router
 249 |     |      */
 250 |     |     function swapERC20(
 251 |     |         address seller,
 252 |     |         address token,
 253 |     |         uint256 amount,
 254 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 255 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 256 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 257 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 258 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 259 |     |     ) external nonReentrant returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 260 |     |         require(address(coreContract) != address(0), "Core not set");
 261 |     |

 263 |     |             seller,
 264 |     |             token,
 265 |     |             amount,
 266 |     |             hashlock,
 267 |     |             timelock,
 268 |     |             riskScore,
 269 |     |             kycHash,
 270 |     |             oracleSignature
 271 |     |         );
 272 |     |         
 273 |     |         _recordSwap(swapId, SwapType.ERC20, msg.sender, amount);
 274 |     |         totalVolumeERC20 += amount;
 275 |     |         
 276 |     |         return swapId;
 277 |     |     }

 284 |     |      * @notice Initiate NFT-to-ETH swap (seller deposits NFT)

 286 |     |     function swapNFTforETH(
 287 |     |         address buyer,
 288 |     |         address nftContract_,
 289 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 290 |     |         uint256 ethAmount,
 291 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 292 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 293 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 294 |     |         bytes32 kycHash,
 295 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 296 |     |     ) external nonReentrant returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 297 |     |         require(address(nftContract) != address(0), "NFT contract not set");

 302 |     |             tokenId,
 303 |     |             ethAmount,
 304 |     |             hashlock,
 305 |     |             timelock,
 306 |     |             riskScore,
 307 |     |             kycHash,
 308 |     |             oracleSignature

 310 |     |

 312 |     |         totalNFTSwaps++;
 313 |     |         
 314 |     |         return swapId;
 315 |     |     }

 317 |     |     /**
 318 |     |      * @notice Initiate NFT-to-NFT atomic swap
 319 |     |      */
 320 |     |     function swapNFTforNFT(
 321 |     |         address partyB,
 322 |     |         address nftContractA,
 323 |     |         uint256 tokenIdA,
 324 |     |         address nftContractB,
 325 |     |         uint256 tokenIdB,
 326 |     |         bytes32 hashlock,
 327 |     |         uint256 timelock,
 328 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 329 |     |         bytes calldata oracleSignature
 330 |     |     ) external nonReentrant returns (bytes32 swapId) {
 331 |     |         require(address(nftContract) != address(0), "NFT contract not set");
 332 |     |         
 333 |     |         swapId = nftContract.initiateNFTtoNFTSwap(
 334 |     |             partyB,
 335 |     |             nftContractA,
 336 |     |             tokenIdA,
 337 |     |             nftContractB,
 338 |     |             tokenIdB,
 339 |     |             hashlock,
 340 |     |             timelock,
 341 |     |             riskScore,
 342 |     |             oracleSignature
 343 |     |         );
 344 |     |         
 345 |     |         _recordSwap(swapId, SwapType.NFT_TO_NFT, msg.sender, 0);
 346 |     |         totalNFTSwaps++;
 347 |     |         
 348 |     |         return swapId;
 349 |     |     }
 350 |     |     
 351 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 352 |     |     //                     SWAP COMPLETION / REFUND
 353 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 354 |     |     
 355 |     |     /**
 356 |     |      * @notice Complete any swap type with preimage
 357 |     |      */
 358 |     |     function completeSwap(bytes32 swapId, bytes32 preimage) external nonReentrant {
 359 |     |         SwapType sType = swapTypes[swapId];
 360 |     |         require(sType != SwapType.NONE, "Unknown swap");
 361 |     |         
 362 |     |         if (sType == SwapType.ETH || sType == SwapType.ERC20) {
 363 |     |             coreContract.completeSwap(swapId, preimage);
 364 |     |         } else if (sType == SwapType.NFT_TO_ETH) {
 365 |     |             nftContract.completeNFTSwap(swapId, preimage);
 366 |     |         } else if (sType == SwapType.NFT_TO_NFT) {
 367 |     |             nftContract.completeNFTtoNFTSwap(swapId, preimage);
 368 |     |         }
 369 |     |         
 370 |     |         totalSwapsCompleted++;
 371 |     |     }
 372 |     |     
 373 |     |     /**
 374 |     |      * @notice Refund any swap type
 375 |     |      */
 376 |     |     function refundSwap(bytes32 swapId) external nonReentrant {
 377 |     |         SwapType sType = swapTypes[swapId];
 378 |     |         require(sType != SwapType.NONE, "Unknown swap");
 379 |     |         
 380 |     |         if (sType == SwapType.ETH || sType == SwapType.ERC20) {
 381 |     |             coreContract.refundSwap(swapId);
 382 |     |         } else if (sType == SwapType.NFT_TO_ETH || sType == SwapType.NFT_TO_NFT) {
 383 |     |             nftContract.refundNFTSwap(swapId);
 384 |     |         }
 385 |     |     }
 386 |     |     
 387 |     |     /**
 388 |     |      * @notice Approve any swap type (approvers only)
 389 |     |      */
 390 |     |     function approveSwap(bytes32 swapId) external {
 391 |     |         SwapType sType = swapTypes[swapId];
 392 |     |         require(sType != SwapType.NONE, "Unknown swap");
 393 |     |         
 394 |     |         if (sType == SwapType.ETH || sType == SwapType.ERC20) {
 395 |     |             coreContract.approveSwap(swapId);
 396 |     |         } else {
 397 |     |             nftContract.approveSwap(swapId);
 398 |     |         }
 399 |     |     }
 400 |     |     
 401 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 402 |     |     //                     DISPUTE RESOLUTION
 403 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 404 |     |     
 405 |     |     /**
 406 |     |      * @notice Raise dispute for any swap type
 407 |     |      */
 408 |     |     function raiseDispute(bytes32 swapId, string calldata evidence) external payable {
 409 |     |         SwapType sType = swapTypes[swapId];
 410 |     |         require(sType != SwapType.NONE, "Unknown swap");
 411 |     |         
 412 |     |         if (sType == SwapType.ETH || sType == SwapType.ERC20) {
 413 |     |             coreContract.raiseDispute{value: msg.value}(swapId, evidence);
 414 |     |         } else {
 415 |     |             nftContract.raiseDispute{value: msg.value}(swapId, evidence);
 416 |     |         }
 417 |     |     }
 418 |     |     
 419 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 420 |     |     //                     CROSS-CHAIN OPERATIONS
 421 |     |     // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
 422 |     |     
 423 |     |     /**
 424 |     |      * @notice Sync risk score to another chain
 425 |     |      */
 426 |     |     function syncRiskToChain(
 427 |     |         uint16 dstChainId,
 428 |     |         address user,
 429 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 430 |     |         bytes calldata adapterParams
 431 |     |     ) external payable {
 432 |     |         require(address(crossChain) != address(0), "CrossChain not set");
 433 |     |         crossChain.syncRiskScore{value: msg.value}(dstChainId, user, riskScore, adapterParams);
 434 |     |     }
 435 |     |     
 436 |     |     /**
 437 |     |      * @notice Get risk score from another chain
 438 |     |      */
 439 |     |     function getChainRiskScore(uint16 chainId, address user) external view returns (uint256) {
 440 |     |         require(address(crossChain) != address(0), "CrossChain not set");
 441 |     |         return crossChain.getChainRiskScore(chainId, user);
 442 |     |     }

C:/amttp/contracts/AMTTPSafeModule.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.24;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
   5 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/ReentrancyGuard.sol";
   6 |     | import "@openzeppelin/contracts/security/Pausable.sol";
   7 |     | import "./interfaces/ISafe.sol";
   8 |     | 
   9 |     | /**
  10 |     |  * @title AMTTPSafeModule
  11 |     |  * @notice Safe (Gnosis) module for AMTTP transaction risk assessment
  12 |     |  * @dev Integrates AMTTP PolicyEngine with Safe multisig wallets for institutional compliance
  13 |     |  * 
  14 |     |  * Features:
  15 |     |  * - Pre-transaction risk assessment via AMTTP ML engine
  16 |     |  * - Configurable risk thresholds per Safe
  17 |     |  * - Transaction queuing for high-risk operations
  18 |     |  * - Multi-signature override for blocked transactions
  19 |     |  * - Compliance audit logging
  20 |     |  */
  21 |     | contract AMTTPSafeModule is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, IGuard, IModule {
  22 |     |     
  23 |     |     // ============ Constants ============
  24 |     |     uint256 public constant RISK_PRECISION = 1000; // Risk scores 0-1000
  25 |     |     uint256 public constant DEFAULT_HIGH_RISK_THRESHOLD = 700;
  26 |     |     uint256 public constant DEFAULT_BLOCK_THRESHOLD = 900;
  27 |     |     uint256 public constant MAX_QUEUE_TIME = 7 days;
  28 |     |     
  29 |     |     // ============ Enums ============
  30 |     |     enum TransactionStatus { Pending, Approved, Blocked, Executed, Expired }
  31 |     |     enum RiskAction { Allow, Queue, Block }
  32 |     |     
  33 |     |     // ============ Structs ============
  34 |     |     struct SafeConfig {
  35 |     |         bool enabled;
  36 |     |         uint256 highRiskThreshold;      // Queue for review
  37 |     |         uint256 blockThreshold;         // Auto-block
  38 |     |         uint256 dailyLimit;             // Daily volume limit
  39 |     |         uint256 singleTxLimit;          // Single transaction limit
  40 |     |         bool requiresMLCheck;           // Require ML risk assessment
  41 |     |         bool autoApproveWhitelisted;    // Auto-approve whitelisted addresses
  42 |     |         address[] operators;            // Authorized operators
  43 |     |     }
  44 |     |     
  45 |     |     struct QueuedTransaction {
  46 |     |         bytes32 txHash;
  47 |     |         address safe;
  48 |     |         address to;
  49 |     |         uint256 value;
  50 |     |         bytes data;
  51 |     |         uint8 operation;
  52 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
  53 |     |         uint256 queuedAt;
  54 |     |         uint256 approvalCount;
  55 |     |         TransactionStatus status;
  56 |     |         string riskReason;
  57 |     |     }
  58 |     |     
  59 |     |     struct DailyVolume {
  60 |     |         uint256 volume;
  61 |     |         uint256 lastResetDay;
  62 |     |     }
  63 |     |     
  64 |     |     struct AuditLog {
  65 |     |         bytes32 txHash;
  66 |     |         address safe;
  67 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
  68 |     |         RiskAction action;
  69 |     |         uint256 timestamp;
  70 |     |         string reason;
  71 |     |     }
  72 |     |     
  73 |     |     // ============ State Variables ============
  74 |     |     
  75 |     |     // AMTTP Integration
  76 |     |     address public policyEngine;
  77 |     |     address public mlOracleService;
  78 |     |     
  79 |     |     // Safe configurations
  80 |     |     mapping(address => SafeConfig) public safeConfigs;
  81 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public registeredSafes;
  82 |     |     
  83 |     |     // Transaction queue
  84 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => QueuedTransaction) public queuedTransactions;
  85 |     |     mapping(address => bytes32[]) public safeQueuedTxs;
  86 |     |     
  87 |     |     // Volume tracking
  88 |     |     mapping(address => DailyVolume) public dailyVolumes;
  89 |     |     
  90 |     |     // Whitelist/Blacklist per Safe
  91 |     |     mapping(address => mapping(address => bool)) public whitelist;
  92 |     |     mapping(address => mapping(address => bool)) public blacklist;
  93 |     |     
  94 |     |     // Approvals for queued transactions
  95 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => mapping(address => bool)) public txApprovals;
  96 |     |     
  97 |     |     // Audit trail
  98 |     |     AuditLog[] public auditLogs;
  99 |     |     mapping(address => uint256[]) public safeAuditIndexes;
 100 |     |     
 101 |     |     // ============ Events ============
 102 |     |     event SafeRegistered(address indexed safe, address[] operators);
 103 |     |     event SafeConfigUpdated(address indexed safe, uint256 highRiskThreshold, uint256 blockThreshold);
 104 |     |     event TransactionQueued(bytes32 indexed txHash, address indexed safe, uint256 riskScore, string reason);
 105 |     |     event TransactionApproved(bytes32 indexed txHash, address indexed approver);
 106 |     |     event TransactionBlocked(bytes32 indexed txHash, address indexed safe, uint256 riskScore, string reason);
 107 |     |     event TransactionExecuted(bytes32 indexed txHash, address indexed safe, bool success);
 108 |     |     event WhitelistUpdated(address indexed safe, address indexed target, bool status);
 109 |     |     event BlacklistUpdated(address indexed safe, address indexed target, bool status);
 110 |     |     event PolicyEngineUpdated(address indexed newEngine);
 111 |     |     event MLOracleUpdated(address indexed newOracle);
 112 |     |     
 113 |     |     // ============ Modifiers ============
 114 |     |     modifier onlySafeOperator(address safe) {
 115 |     |         require(isOperator(safe, msg.sender), "Not authorized operator");
 116 |     |         _;
 117 |     |     }
 118 |     |     
 119 |     |     modifier onlyRegisteredSafe(address safe) {
 120 |     |         require(registeredSafes[safe], "Safe not registered");
 121 |     |         _;
 122 |     |     }
 123 |     |     
 124 |     |     // ============ Constructor ============
 125 |     |     constructor(address _policyEngine, address _mlOracle) {
 126 |     |         require(_policyEngine != address(0), "Invalid policy engine");
 127 |     |         policyEngine = _policyEngine;
 128 |     |         mlOracleService = _mlOracle;
 129 |     |     }
 130 |     |     
 131 |     |     // ============ Safe Registration ============
 132 |     |     
 133 |     |     /**
 134 |     |      * @notice Register a Safe with AMTTP module
 135 |     |      * @param safe Address of the Safe wallet
 136 |     |      * @param operators Array of authorized operators
 137 |     |      * @param config Initial configuration
 138 |     |      */
 139 |     |     function registerSafe(
 140 |     |         address safe,
 141 |     |         address[] calldata operators,
 142 |     |         SafeConfig calldata config
 143 |     |     ) external {
 144 |     |         require(!registeredSafes[safe], "Safe already registered");
 145 |     |         require(safe != address(0), "Invalid safe address");
 146 |     |         require(operators.length > 0, "Need at least one operator");
 147 |     |         
 148 |     |         // Verify caller is Safe owner or module is enabled
 149 |     |         require(
 150 |     |             IGnosisSafe(safe).isOwner(msg.sender) || 
 151 |     |             IGnosisSafe(safe).isModuleEnabled(address(this)),
 152 |     |             "Not authorized"
 153 |     |         );
 154 |     |         
 155 |     |         registeredSafes[safe] = true;
 156 |     |         safeConfigs[safe] = SafeConfig({
 157 |     |             enabled: config.enabled,
 158 |     |             highRiskThreshold: config.highRiskThreshold > 0 ? config.highRiskThreshold : DEFAULT_HIGH_RISK_THRESHOLD,
 159 |     |             blockThreshold: config.blockThreshold > 0 ? config.blockThreshold : DEFAULT_BLOCK_THRESHOLD,
 160 |     |             dailyLimit: config.dailyLimit,
 161 |     |             singleTxLimit: config.singleTxLimit,
 162 |     |             requiresMLCheck: config.requiresMLCheck,
 163 |     |             autoApproveWhitelisted: config.autoApproveWhitelisted,
 164 |     |             operators: operators
 165 |     |         });
 166 |     |         
 167 |     |         emit SafeRegistered(safe, operators);
 168 |     |     }
 169 |     |     
 170 |     |     /**
 171 |     |      * @notice Update Safe configuration
 172 |     |      */
 173 |     |     function updateSafeConfig(
 174 |     |         address safe,
 175 |     |         SafeConfig calldata config
 176 |     |     ) external onlySafeOperator(safe) onlyRegisteredSafe(safe) {
 177 |     |         safeConfigs[safe] = config;
 178 |     |         emit SafeConfigUpdated(safe, config.highRiskThreshold, config.blockThreshold);
 179 |     |     }
 180 |     |     
 181 |     |     // ============ IGuard Implementation ============
 182 |     |     
 183 |     |     /**
 184 |     |      * @notice Pre-transaction guard check (called by Safe before execution)
 185 |     |      */
 186 |     |     function checkTransaction(
 187 |     |         address to,
 188 |     |         uint256 value,
 189 |     |         bytes memory data,
 190 |     |         uint8 operation,
 191 |     |         uint256 safeTxGas,
 192 |     |         uint256 baseGas,
 193 |     |         uint256 gasPrice,
 194 |     |         address gasToken,
 195 |     |         address refundReceiver,
 196 |     |         bytes memory signatures,
 197 |     |         address msgSender
 198 |     |     ) external override {
 199 |     |         address safe = msg.sender;
 200 |     |         
 201 |     |         if (!registeredSafes[safe] || !safeConfigs[safe].enabled) {
 202 |     |             return; // Skip if not registered or disabled
 203 |     |         }
 204 |     |         
 205 |     |         // Check blacklist
 206 |     |         require(!blacklist[safe][to], "Recipient blacklisted");
 207 |     |         
 208 |     |         // Check daily volume
 209 |     |         _checkDailyVolume(safe, value);
 210 |     |         
 211 |     |         // Check single transaction limit
 212 |     |         SafeConfig memory config = safeConfigs[safe];
 213 |     |         if (config.singleTxLimit > 0) {
 214 |     |             require(value <= config.singleTxLimit, "Exceeds single tx limit");
 215 |     |         }
 216 |     |         
 217 |     |         // Auto-approve whitelisted if enabled
 218 |     |         if (config.autoApproveWhitelisted && whitelist[safe][to]) {
 219 |     |             _logAudit(bytes32(0), safe, 0, RiskAction.Allow, "Whitelisted recipient");
 220 |     |             return;
 221 |     |         }
 222 |     |         
 223 |     |         // Get risk assessment
 224 |     |         if (config.requiresMLCheck && mlOracleService != address(0)) {
 225 |     |             (uint256 riskScore, string memory reason) = _getRiskAssessment(safe, to, value, data);
 226 |     |             
 227 |     |             bytes32 txHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(safe, to, value, data, block.timestamp));
 228 |     |             
 229 |     |             if (riskScore >= config.blockThreshold) {
 230 |     |                 // Block high-risk transaction
 231 |     |                 _logAudit(txHash, safe, riskScore, RiskAction.Block, reason);
 232 |     |                 emit TransactionBlocked(txHash, safe, riskScore, reason);
 233 |     |                 revert("Transaction blocked: high risk");
 234 |     |             } else if (riskScore >= config.highRiskThreshold) {
 235 |     |                 // Queue for review
 236 |     |                 _queueTransaction(txHash, safe, to, value, data, operation, riskScore, reason);
 237 |     |                 revert("Transaction queued: elevated risk");
 238 |     |             }
 239 |     |             
 240 |     |             _logAudit(txHash, safe, riskScore, RiskAction.Allow, reason);
 241 |     |         }
 242 |     |     }
 243 |     |     
 244 |     |     /**
 245 |     |      * @notice Post-transaction check
 246 |     |      */
 247 |     |     function checkAfterExecution(bytes32 txHash, bool success) external override {
 248 |     |         // Log execution result
 249 |     |         emit TransactionExecuted(txHash, msg.sender, success);
 250 |     |     }
 251 |     |     
 252 |     |     // ============ IModule Implementation ============
 253 |     |     
 254 |     |     /**
 255 |     |      * @notice Execute an approved queued transaction
 256 |     |      */
 257 |     |     function execute(
 258 |     |         address to,
 259 |     |         uint256 value,
 260 |     |         bytes memory data,
 261 |     |         uint8 operation
 262 |     |     ) external override onlyRegisteredSafe(msg.sender) returns (bool success) {
 263 |     |         address safe = msg.sender;
 264 |     |         bytes32 txHash = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(safe, to, value, data, block.timestamp));
 265 |     |         
 266 |     |         QueuedTransaction storage queuedTx = queuedTransactions[txHash];
 267 |     |         require(queuedTx.status == TransactionStatus.Approved, "Transaction not approved");
 268 |     |         
 269 |     |         queuedTx.status = TransactionStatus.Executed;
 270 |     |         
 271 |     |         success = IGnosisSafe(safe).execTransactionFromModule(to, value, data, operation);
 272 |     |         
 273 |     |         emit TransactionExecuted(txHash, safe, success);
 274 |     |     }
 275 |     |     
 276 |     |     /**
 277 |     |      * @notice Execute approved queued transaction by hash
 278 |     |      */
 279 |     |     function executeQueuedTransaction(bytes32 txHash) external nonReentrant returns (bool) {
 280 |     |         QueuedTransaction storage queuedTx = queuedTransactions[txHash];
 281 |     |         require(queuedTx.status == TransactionStatus.Approved, "Transaction not approved");
 282 |     |         require(block.timestamp <= queuedTx.queuedAt + MAX_QUEUE_TIME, "Transaction expired");
 283 |     |         
 284 |     |         address safe = queuedTx.safe;
 285 |     |         require(isOperator(safe, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 286 |     |         
 287 |     |         queuedTx.status = TransactionStatus.Executed;
 288 |     |         
 289 |     |         bool success = IGnosisSafe(safe).execTransactionFromModule(
 290 |     |             queuedTx.to,
 291 |     |             queuedTx.value,
 292 |     |             queuedTx.data,
 293 |     |             queuedTx.operation
 294 |     |         );
 295 |     |         
 296 |     |         emit TransactionExecuted(txHash, safe, success);
 297 |     |         return success;
 298 |     |     }
 299 |     |     
 300 |     |     // ============ Queued Transaction Management ============
 301 |     |     
 302 |     |     /**
 303 |     |      * @notice Approve a queued transaction
 304 |     |      */
 305 |     |     function approveQueuedTransaction(bytes32 txHash) external {
 306 |     |         QueuedTransaction storage queuedTx = queuedTransactions[txHash];
 307 |     |         require(queuedTx.status == TransactionStatus.Pending, "Invalid status");
 308 |     |         require(isOperator(queuedTx.safe, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 309 |     |         require(!txApprovals[txHash][msg.sender], "Already approved");
 310 |     |         
 311 |     |         txApprovals[txHash][msg.sender] = true;
 312 |     |         queuedTx.approvalCount++;
 313 |     |         
 314 |     |         emit TransactionApproved(txHash, msg.sender);
 315 |     |         
 316 |     |         // Check if enough approvals (require >50% of operators)
 317 |     |         uint256 requiredApprovals = (safeConfigs[queuedTx.safe].operators.length + 1) / 2;
 318 |     |         if (queuedTx.approvalCount >= requiredApprovals) {
 319 |     |             queuedTx.status = TransactionStatus.Approved;
 320 |     |         }
 321 |     |     }
 322 |     |     
 323 |     |     /**
 324 |     |      * @notice Reject a queued transaction
 325 |     |      */
 326 |     |     function rejectQueuedTransaction(bytes32 txHash) external {
 327 |     |         QueuedTransaction storage queuedTx = queuedTransactions[txHash];
 328 |     |         require(queuedTx.status == TransactionStatus.Pending, "Invalid status");
 329 |     |         require(isOperator(queuedTx.safe, msg.sender), "Not authorized");
 330 |     |         
 331 |     |         queuedTx.status = TransactionStatus.Blocked;
 332 |     |         emit TransactionBlocked(txHash, queuedTx.safe, queuedTx.riskScore, "Rejected by operator");
 333 |     |     }
 334 |     |     
 335 |     |     /**
 336 |     |      * @notice Get queued transactions for a Safe
 337 |     |      */
 338 |     |     function getQueuedTransactions(address safe) external view returns (bytes32[] memory) {
 339 |     |         return safeQueuedTxs[safe];
 340 |     |     }
 341 |     |     
 342 |     |     // ============ Whitelist/Blacklist Management ============
 343 |     |     
 344 |     |     function addToWhitelist(address safe, address target) external onlySafeOperator(safe) {
 345 |     |         whitelist[safe][target] = true;
 346 |     |         emit WhitelistUpdated(safe, target, true);
 347 |     |     }
 348 |     |     
 349 |     |     function removeFromWhitelist(address safe, address target) external onlySafeOperator(safe) {
 350 |     |         whitelist[safe][target] = false;
 351 |     |         emit WhitelistUpdated(safe, target, false);
 352 |     |     }
 353 |     |     
 354 |     |     function addToBlacklist(address safe, address target) external onlySafeOperator(safe) {
 355 |     |         blacklist[safe][target] = true;
 356 |     |         emit BlacklistUpdated(safe, target, true);
 357 |     |     }
 358 |     |     
 359 |     |     function removeFromBlacklist(address safe, address target) external onlySafeOperator(safe) {
 360 |     |         blacklist[safe][target] = false;
 361 |     |         emit BlacklistUpdated(safe, target, false);
 362 |     |     }
 363 |     |     
 364 |     |     // ============ View Functions ============
 365 |     |     
 366 |     |     function isOperator(address safe, address account) public view returns (bool) {
 367 |     |         address[] memory operators = safeConfigs[safe].operators;
 368 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < operators.length; i++) {
 369 |     |             if (operators[i] == account) return true;
 370 |     |         }
 371 |     |         // Safe owners are also operators
 372 |     |         return IGnosisSafe(safe).isOwner(account);
 373 |     |     }
 374 |     |     
 375 |     |     function getAuditLogs(address safe, uint256 limit) external view returns (AuditLog[] memory) {
 376 |     |         uint256[] memory indexes = safeAuditIndexes[safe];
 377 |     |         uint256 count = indexes.length < limit ? indexes.length : limit;
 378 |     |         AuditLog[] memory logs = new AuditLog[](count);
 379 |     |         
 380 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {
 381 |     |             logs[i] = auditLogs[indexes[indexes.length - 1 - i]];
 382 |     |         }
 383 |     |         
 384 |     |         return logs;
 385 |     |     }

 388 |     |     
 389 |     |     function setPolicyEngine(address _policyEngine) external onlyOwner {
 390 |     |         require(_policyEngine != address(0), "Invalid address");
 391 |     |         policyEngine = _policyEngine;
 392 |     |         emit PolicyEngineUpdated(_policyEngine);
 393 |     |     }
 394 |     |     
 395 |     |     function setMLOracle(address _mlOracle) external onlyOwner {
 396 |     |         mlOracleService = _mlOracle;
 397 |     |         emit MLOracleUpdated(_mlOracle);
 398 |     |     }

 400 |     |     function pause() external onlyOwner {
 401 |     |         _pause();
 402 |     |     }
 403 |     |     
 404 |     |     function unpause() external onlyOwner {
 405 |     |         _unpause();
 406 |     |     }
 407 |     |     
 408 |     |     // ============ Internal Functions ============
 409 |     |     
 410 |     |     function _queueTransaction(
 411 |     |         bytes32 txHash,
 412 |     |         address safe,
 413 |     |         address to,
 414 |     |         uint256 value,
 415 |     |         bytes memory data,
 416 |     |         uint8 operation,
 417 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 418 |     |         string memory reason
 419 |     |     ) internal {
 420 |     |         queuedTransactions[txHash] = QueuedTransaction({
 421 |     |             txHash: txHash,
 422 |     |             safe: safe,
 423 |     |             to: to,
 424 |     |             value: value,
 425 |     |             data: data,
 426 |     |             operation: operation,
 427 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 428 |     |             queuedAt: block.timestamp,
 429 |     |             approvalCount: 0,
 430 |     |             status: TransactionStatus.Pending,
 431 |     |             riskReason: reason
 432 |     |         });
 433 |     |         
 434 |     |         safeQueuedTxs[safe].push(txHash);
 435 |     |         
 436 |     |         emit TransactionQueued(txHash, safe, riskScore, reason);
 437 |     |     }
 438 |     |     
 439 |     |     function _checkDailyVolume(address safe, uint256 value) internal {
 440 |     |         DailyVolume storage dv = dailyVolumes[safe];
 441 |     |         uint256 currentDay = block.timestamp / 1 days;
 442 |     |         
 443 |     |         if (dv.lastResetDay < currentDay) {
 444 |     |             dv.volume = 0;
 445 |     |             dv.lastResetDay = currentDay;
 446 |     |         }
 447 |     |         
 448 |     |         uint256 limit = safeConfigs[safe].dailyLimit;
 449 |     |         if (limit > 0) {
 450 |     |             require(dv.volume + value <= limit, "Daily limit exceeded");
 451 |     |         }
 452 |     |         
 453 |     |         dv.volume += value;
 454 |     |     }
 455 |     |     
 456 |     |     function _getRiskAssessment(
 457 |     |         address safe,
 458 |     |         address to,
 459 |     |         uint256 value,
 460 |     |         bytes memory data
 461 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 riskScore, string memory reason) {
 462 |     |         // In production, this would call the ML oracle service
 463 |     |         // For now, return a simulated risk score based on simple heuristics
 464 |     |         
 465 |     |         // Check if recipient is a contract
 466 |     |         uint256 codeSize;
 467 |     |         assembly {
 468 |     |             codeSize := extcodesize(to)
 469 |     |         }
 470 |     |         
 471 |     |         riskScore = 100; // Base score
 472 |     |         reason = "Standard transaction";
 473 |     |         
 474 |     |         // Large value transactions
 475 |     |         if (value > 10 ether) {
 476 |     |             riskScore += 200;
 477 |     |             reason = "High value transaction";
 478 |     |         } else if (value > 1 ether) {
 479 |     |             riskScore += 100;
 480 |     |             reason = "Medium value transaction";
 481 |     |         }
 482 |     |         
 483 |     |         // Contract interaction
 484 |     |         if (codeSize > 0 && data.length > 0) {
 485 |     |             riskScore += 150;
 486 |     |             reason = "Contract interaction";
 487 |     |         }
 488 |     |         
 489 |     |         // New address check would require historical data
 490 |     |         // This is placeholder for ML integration
 491 |     |         
 492 |     |         return (riskScore, reason);
 493 |     |     }
 494 |     |     
 495 |     |     function _logAudit(
 496 |     |         bytes32 txHash,
 497 |     |         address safe,
 498 |     |         uint256 riskScore,
 499 |     |         RiskAction action,
 500 |     |         string memory reason
 501 |     |     ) internal {
 502 |     |         uint256 index = auditLogs.length;
 503 |     |         auditLogs.push(AuditLog({
 504 |     |             txHash: txHash,
 505 |     |             safe: safe,
 506 |     |             riskScore: riskScore,
 507 |     |             action: action,
 508 |     |             timestamp: block.timestamp,
 509 |     |             reason: reason
 510 |     |         }));
 511 |     |         safeAuditIndexes[safe].push(index);
 512 |     |     }
 513 |     | }

  11 |     | /**
  12 |     |  * @title AMTTPPolicyManager Interface
  13 |     |  */
  14 |     | interface IAMTTPPolicyManager {
  15 |     |     function validateTransaction(
  16 |     |         address user,
  17 |     |         address counterparty,
  18 |     |         uint256 amount,
  19 |     |         uint256 riskScore
  20 |     |     ) external view returns (bool allowed, uint8 recommendedRiskLevel, string memory reason);
  21 |     | }
  22 |     | 
  23 |     | /**
  24 |     |  * @title AMTTP - Anti-Money Transfer Transfer Protocol
  25 |     |  * @dev Main contract with integrated fraud protection and policy validation
  26 |     |  */
  27 |     | contract AMTTP is 
  28 |     |     Initializable, 
  29 |     |     ERC20Upgradeable, 
  30 |     |     OwnableUpgradeable, 
  31 |     |     UUPSUpgradeable, 
  32 |     |     PausableUpgradeable,
  33 |     |     ReentrancyGuardUpgradeable 
  34 |     | {
  35 |     |     
  36 |     |     // Core Components
  37 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public authorizedOracles;
  38 |     |     mapping(bytes32 => Transaction) public transactions;
  39 |     |     mapping(address => UserProfile) public userProfiles;
  40 |     |     mapping(address => bool) public kycVerified;
  41 |     |     
  42 |     |     // Policy integration
  43 |     |     IAMTTPPolicyManager public policyManager;
  44 |     |     bool public policyValidationEnabled;
  45 |     |     
  46 |     |     // Structs
  47 |     |     struct Transaction {
  48 |     |         address from;
  49 |     |         address to;
  50 |     |         uint256 amount;
  51 |     |         uint256 timestamp;
  52 |     |         uint256 riskScore;
  53 |     |         uint8 status; // 0: pending, 1: approved, 2: rejected, 3: escrowed
  54 |     |         bytes32 dataHash;
  55 |     |     }
  56 |     |     
  57 |     |     struct UserProfile {
  58 |     |         uint256 totalTransactions;
  59 |     |         uint256 totalVolume;
  60 |     |         uint256 lastActivity;
  61 |     |         uint8 reputationScore;
  62 |     |         bool isActive;
  63 |     |     }
  64 |     |     
  65 |     |     // Events
  66 |     |     event TransactionInitiated(bytes32 indexed txId, address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount);
  67 |     |     event TransactionApproved(bytes32 indexed txId, uint256 riskScore);
  68 |     |     event TransactionRejected(bytes32 indexed txId, uint256 riskScore, string reason);
  69 |     |     event TransactionEscrowed(bytes32 indexed txId, uint256 riskScore);
  70 |     |     event RiskScoreUpdated(bytes32 indexed txId, uint256 oldScore, uint256 newScore);
  71 |     |     event UserProfileUpdated(address indexed user, uint8 reputationScore);
  72 |     |     event KYCStatusUpdated(address indexed user, bool verified);
  73 |     |     event OracleUpdated(address indexed oracle, bool authorized);
  74 |     |     event PolicyManagerUpdated(address indexed newManager);
  75 |     |     
  76 |     |     // Modifiers
  77 |     |     modifier onlyOracle() {
  78 |     |         require(authorizedOracles[msg.sender], "Not authorized oracle");
  79 |     |         _;
  80 |     |     }
  81 |     |     
  82 |     |     modifier validTransaction(bytes32 txId) {
  83 |     |         require(transactions[txId].from != address(0), "Transaction not found");
  84 |     |         _;
  85 |     |     }
  86 |     |     
  87 |     |     /// @custom:oz-upgrades-unsafe-allow constructor
  88 |     |     constructor() {
  89 |     |         _disableInitializers();
  90 |     |     }
  91 |     |     
  92 |     |     function initialize(
  93 |     |         string memory name,
  94 |     |         string memory symbol,
  95 |     |         uint256 initialSupply
  96 |     |     ) public initializer {
  97 |     |         __ERC20_init(name, symbol);
  98 |     |         __Ownable_init();
  99 |     |         __UUPSUpgradeable_init();
 100 |     |         __Pausable_init();
 101 |     |         __ReentrancyGuard_init();
 102 |     |         
 103 |     |         _mint(msg.sender, initialSupply);
 104 |     |         authorizedOracles[msg.sender] = true;
 105 |     |         policyValidationEnabled = false;
 106 |     |     }
